## RESULTS.md entries, verbatim

##### 2026-10-07 — E0 add-on: native vs ITRANS tokens per word (FLORES+ Kannada, 31,530 words in context)

RESULTS.md lines 99 to 109; first line committed in `76c8073` on 2026-10-07 16:40:03 +0530; last line in `76c8073` on 2026-10-07 16:40:03 +0530; labels from the title and parent heading: none

`````text
## 2026-10-07 — E0 add-on: native vs ITRANS tokens per word (FLORES+ Kannada, 31,530 words in context)

- `results/e00_gate/tokens_per_word.csv`. Mean tokens per word, native -> ITRANS (share of words >= 4 tokens):
  Qwen2.5/3 11.79 -> 4.32 (96% -> 59%); Qwen3.5 5.28 -> 4.18 (77% -> 57%); Param-1 4.13 -> 4.01;
  Phi-4-mini/o200k 3.25 -> 3.96 (40% -> 53%); Gemma 4 3.20 -> 4.01 (40% -> 54%); Sarvam-1 2.34 -> 5.31 (18% -> 72%).
- Implication for E4: the fragmentation ordering of native vs ITRANS **flips across model families** (ITRANS less
  fragmented on Qwen, more fragmented on Gemma 4 / Phi-4 / Sarvam-1). E4 can use this as a crossover: an effect
  driven by fragmentation should change sign between the families; an effect driven by script should not.
  Gated candidates (Llama 3.x, Gemma 2/3, Aya) are pending access.

---
`````

##### 2026-10-08 — E0 GATE (4 of 5 models; Gemma 4 E2B not run)

RESULTS.md lines 205 to 231; first line committed in `22013f7` on 2026-10-08 01:52:43 +0530; last line in `0a1d054` on 2026-10-14 18:42:59 +0530; labels from the title and parent heading: result, failed or not run

`````text
## 2026-10-08 — E0 GATE (4 of 5 models; Gemma 4 E2B not run)

- `scripts/e00_gate.py`; results `results/e00_gate/summary.csv`, `summary.json`, `scores_<model>.parquet`. n = 200 items
  per task (IndicXNLI kn test aligned to XNLI en; IndicSentiment kn test with English review), stratified, seed 0.
  Log-likelihood of English label words after an English instruction (templates e0_*_v1). Raw accuracy, 95%
  bootstrap CI. Chance: XNLI 33.3%, sentiment 50%.
- Gemma 4 E2B: the E0 job was stopped by Claude Code for low system memory while loading Gemma (the loader briefly
  materializes the 10 GB checkpoint in CPU RAM). Not restarted pending Aarnav's go-ahead.

  | model | sentiment en / native / ITRANS | XNLI en / native / ITRANS |
  |---|---|---|
  | Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct | 98.5 / 70.5 / 49.5 | 79.5 / 51.0 / 37.5 |
  | Qwen3-1.7B | 98.5 / 86.5 / 58.0 | 61.5 / 43.0 / 37.0 |
  | Qwen3.5-2B | 97.5 / 78.5 / 67.5 | 43.0 / 34.0 / 34.0 |
  | Sarvam-1 | 95.5 / 98.0 / 80.0 | 57.5 / 52.0 / 40.5 |

- Gate rule (native >= chance + 10 and English >= chance + 15 on one task): **all four pass**, via sentiment;
  Qwen2.5 and Sarvam-1 also pass on XNLI. Qwen3.5-2B collapses to one label on XNLI (99.5% of predictions in native
  and ITRANS; calibrated English 62.0%).
- **Finding relevant to M1/M2: ITRANS accuracy is below native in every model and task, including the Qwen models
  where ITRANS is far less fragmented** (Qwen2.5: native 70.5 vs ITRANS 49.5 on sentiment, with 99.5% of ITRANS
  predictions on one label; Qwen3 86.5 vs 58.0). Fewer tokens did not make ITRANS easier; familiarity with the form
  (training data) dominates at the accuracy level. This argues against the simplest fragmentation story for accuracy
  and has to be reported. Several ITRANS cells are at chance (all XNLI-ITRANS, Qwen2.5 sentiment-ITRANS), which limits
  M2/M6 for those cells.

---
`````

##### 2026-10-14 — Step 0 decisions

RESULTS.md lines 232 to 240; first line committed in `0a1d054` on 2026-10-14 18:42:59 +0530; last line in `0a1d054` on 2026-10-14 18:42:59 +0530; labels from the title and parent heading: none

`````text
## 2026-10-14 — Step 0 decisions

- Qwen 2.5/3 slot: **Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct** (passes E0 on both tasks).
- Gemma 4 E2B: retried with a new low-memory text-only loader (language-model tensors streamed from safetensors in
  bf16 onto a meta-initialized model; per-layer embedding table on CPU). Loads in 11 s, 4.25 GB GPU, 5.3 GB peak
  working set; chat sanity check correct. **Gemma 4 stays.** Note: Gemma's tokenizer does not add BOS by default and the
  model degenerates without it; all our Gemma inputs prepend BOS explicitly.

---
`````

##### 2026-10-14 — E0 natural romanization: defects and regeneration queue (no API calls made for this check)

RESULTS.md lines 273 to 288; first line committed in `81d420c` on 2026-10-14 22:16:36 +0530; last line in `81d420c` on 2026-10-14 22:16:36 +0530; labels from the title and parent heading: none

`````text
## 2026-10-14 — E0 natural romanization: defects and regeneration queue (no API calls made for this check)

501 of 587 unique E0 texts were romanized before the Gemini daily quota; the remaining 86 (all sentiment reviews) run
tomorrow with the same model and prompt. Correction to the 2026-10-14 status report: the 9 "flagged" outputs are not
word-count outliers (all word ratios are 0.95–1.0); the flag fired on **leftover Kannada script**.
The prompt asks for no diacritics or special symbols, so 20 of the 501 outputs violate it
(`data/processed/natural_e0_regen_queue_v1.json`):
- 9 with leftover Kannada script (usually one untransliterated word or suffix, e.g. "ಪಡೆಯುವಂತೆ", "ondಕ್ಕಿಂತ");
- 2 with another Indic script (Malayalam "tക്ഷണ" for ತಕ್ಷಣ; Telugu "ంత" inside a word);
- 9 with Latin diacritics or IPA (IAST-style ā ē ī ū ṇ ḍ ḷ ṭ ś ṣ ṅ ñ; š; ü; IPA ɛ ɩ).
One further output keeps "À" because the Kannada source has "À la carte"; it is valid.
All 20 will be re-asked tomorrow with the same model, prompt and temperature (attempts 2 and 3; the attempt number
only changes the cache key). First attempts are kept in the data; rows still defective after attempt 3 will be
reported, not silently fixed.

---
`````

##### 2026-10-15 — E0 natural romanization quality check

RESULTS.md lines 468 to 481; first line committed in `d0a6aa0` on 2026-10-15 01:19:38 +0530; last line in `d0a6aa0` on 2026-10-15 01:19:38 +0530; labels from the title and parent heading: none

`````text
## 2026-10-15 — E0 natural romanization quality check

- **Spot checked by a native speaker (Aarnav, 2026-10-15), no issues found:** 20 outputs sampled at random (seed
  20261015) from the 482 non-defective outputs then available (`data/annotation/natural_spotcheck20_v1.json`).
- Back-translation (below) is not used as a quality check: the back-translator reads romanized Kannada poorly, so
  low similarity mostly reflects the reader, not the romanization. Among the 26 lowest-similarity texts, no clear
  romanization error was found.
- **Source noise** (`data/annotation/source_noise_v1.json`): 2 E0 texts whose Kannada does not match the dataset's
  English (xnli-test-555 hypothesis: the Kannada drops "a vacuum"; xnli-test-1375 hypothesis: the Kannada says "I
  cannot count anything"). Excluded from the back-translation statistics. Correction to an earlier draft that
  listed 4 such items: the other two (xnli-test-1558, indicsentiment-test-662) are faithful translations that the
  back-translator misread ("Ogle" -> "Google"; "not durable" -> "cannot be endured").

---
`````

They are joined by the economist Glenn Loury, who, as a necessary policy, supports a positive approach by endorsing the tradition of cutting the class. | 0.50597966 | False |
| xnli-test-4411 | hypothesis | ಗ್ಲೆನ್ ಲೂರಿ ಬೀದಿ ಬದಿ ಶುಚಿಗೊಳಿಸುವ ಕೆಲಸದಲ್ಲಿ ತೊಡಗಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Glen louri beedi badi shuchigolisuva kelasadalli todagiddare. | False | Glenn Loury is a street sweeper. | Glen was found in the class that cleans the floor. | 0.49883384 | False |
| xnli-test-3132 | premise | ಈ ಕಾರ್ಮಿಕರು ಸಿಕ್ಕಿಬೀಳಲಿಲ್ಲ, ಆದರೆ ಮೇಲಿನ ಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿದ್ದ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ನಿವಾಸಿಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಭಿನ್ನವಾಗಿ, ಅವರು ಪರಿಣಾಮ ಬೀರಿದ ತಕ್ಷಣ ಕೆಳಗೆ ಇಳಿಯದಿರಲು ನಿರ್ಧರಿಸಿದ್ದರು. | ee karmikaru sikkibeelalilla, aadare melina mahadigalallidda hechina nivasigaliginta bhinnavai, avaru parinama beerida takshana kelage iliyadiralu nirdharisiddaru. | False | These workers were not trapped, yet unlike most occupants on the upper floors, they had chosen not to descend immediately after impact. | These workers are not available, but unlike the senior officials’ large households, they have decided that there will be no immediate results. | 0.36023208 | False |
| xnli-test-3132 | hypothesis | ಮೇಲಿನ ಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿನ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ನಿವಾಸಿಗಳು ತಕ್ಷಣವೇ ಕೆಳಗೆ ಇಳಿದರು. | melina mahadigalalli ella nivasigalu takshanave kelage ilidaru. | False | All occupants on the upper floors immediately descended upon impact. | All the residents on the upper floors were moved down immediately. | 0.8477423 | False |
| xnli-test-3031 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ನಡೆದ ಸಿಐಎ-ಎಫ್ಬಿಐ ಸಭೆಯ ಎರಡು ದಿನಗಳ ನಂತರ ಮಿಹ್ಧರ್ ಹೊಸ ಯುಎಸ್ ವೀಸಾ ಪಡೆದರು. | New Yorknalli nadeda CIA-FBI sabheya eradu dinagala natara Mihdhar hosa US visa padedaru. | False | Mihdhar received a new U.S. visa two days after the CIA-FBI meeting in New York. | Mihdhar, a CIA-FBI member who was in New York, got a new US visa after two days. | 0.8975452 | False |
| xnli-test-3031 | hypothesis | "" "ಮಿಹಧರ್ ಎಂದಿಗೂ ವೀಸಾ ಪಡೆದಿಲ್ಲ, ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ಅವರು ಎಂದಿಗೂ ನಮ್ಮ ಬಳಿಗೆ ಬರಲಿಲ್ಲ." | Mihadhar endigu visa padedilla, aadre avaru endigu namma balige baralilla. | False | Mihdhar never got a visa, so he never came to the us. | Mihadhar never got a visa, but he never comes to our house. | 0.74457246 | False |
| xnli-test-736 | premise | ಕೆಲವು ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳ ಹಿಂದೆ ಅವರು ಆರು ಸದಸ್ಯರ ಜೂರಿಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದರು, ಅವರು ಯಾವಾಗಲೂ ಹನ್ನೆರಡು ಪುರುಷರು ಎಂದು ನಿಮಗೆ ತಿಳಿದಿದೆ ಎಂದು ನಾನು ಭಾವಿಸಿದ್ದೆ-ಹನ್ನೆರಡು ಪುರುಷರು ಪ್ರಯತ್ನಿಸಿದರು ಮತ್ತು ಮಾತನಾಡಿದರು | kelavu thingalugala hinde avaru aaru sadasyara jurigalannu hondiddaru, avaru yavagalu hanneladu purusharu endu nimage tilidide endu nanu bhavisidde-hanneladu purusharu prayatnisidaru mattu matanadaru | False | a few months ago that they had six member juries i thought that they you know it was always twelve twelve men tried and true so to speak | Behind some matters they had the support of four members; they have always been the Hanneladu men, as you know, I thought— the Hanneladu men tried and spoke. | 0.4066801 | False |
| xnli-test-736 | hypothesis | ತೀರ್ಪುಗಾರರು ಯಾವಾಗಲೂ 13 ಜನರಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Teerpugarru yavagaloo 13 janariruttare. | False | The jury is always 13 people. | There are always 13 police officers. | 0.72017086 | False |
| xnli-test-4010 | premise | ಓಮ್ನಿಯಾ ವಿನ್ಸಿಟ್ ಅಮೋರ್ (ನೀವು ವೀಕ್ಲಿ ಸ್ಟ್ಯಾಂಡರ್ಡ್ಗಾಗಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡದಿದ್ದರೆ): ಬ್ರಿಟ್ ಹ್ಯೂಮ್ (ಫಾಕ್ಸ್ ನ್ಯೂಸ್ ಭಾನುವಾರ) ಲೆವಿನ್ಸ್ಕಿ ಏಕೆ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ ಎಂಬುದರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಊಹೆ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾಳೆ-ಅವಳು ಇನ್ನೂ ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷರ ಮೇಲೆ ಆಶಾಭಂಗವಿಲ್ಲದ ಕ್ರಶ್ ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಾಳೆ. | Omnia vincit amor (neevu weekly standardge kelsa maadadiddare): Brit Hume (Fox News Sunday) Levinsky eke saadhyavilla embudarbage uuhe maaduttaale-avalu innoo adhyakshara mele aashaabhangavillada crush hondiddhaale. | False | Omnia vincit amor (unless you work for the Weekly Standard): Brit Hume ( Fox News Sunday ) speculates on why Lewinsky might not  She still has a hopeless crush on the president. | Love conquers all (if you didn’t work for the weekly standard): Brit Hume (Fox News Sunday) says Levinsky is impossible—she’s got a hopeless crush on the president. | 0.72635686 | False |
| xnli-test-4010 | hypothesis | ಬ್ರಿಟ್ ಹ್ಯೂಮ್ ಫಾಕ್ಸ್ಗಾಗಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Brit Hume foxge kelsa madtare. | False | Brit Hume works for Fox. | Brit Hume works at Fox. | 0.9909232 | False |
| xnli-test-1359 | premise | ತನ್ನ ಸ್ವಂತ ಸಾಧನಗಳಿಗೆ ಎಡ, ಈ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯು ಎಕ್ಸರ್ಗೋನಿಕ್ ಆಗಿರುತ್ತದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಮತೋಲನ ಅನುಪಾತಕ್ಕೆ ಹೋಲಿಸಿದರೆ ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಮತೋಲನಕ್ಕೆ ಹೋಲಿಸಿದರೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚುವರಿ ಟ್ರಿಮರ್ಗಳ ಉಪಸ್ಥಿತಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಂಶ್ಲೇಷಣೆಯ ಮೂಲಕ ಸಮತೋಲನದತ್ತ ಪ್ರಯಾಸಕರವಾಗಿ ಹರಿಯುತ್ತದೆ. | tanna svanta sadhanagalige eda, ee pratikriyeyu eksargonik agiruttatte mattu hexamarna samatolana anupatakke holisidare hexamarna samatolanakke holisidare heccuvari trimmergala upasthitiyalli hexamarna sanshleshaneya moolaka samatolanadatta prayasakaravagi hariyuttade. | False | Left to its own devices, this reaction is exergonic and, in the presence of excess trimers compared to the equilibrium ratio of hexamer to trimers, will flow exergonically toward equilibrium by synthesizing the hexamer. | For its own resources, this reaction is exergonic, and when the hydrogen‑balance ratio is increased, the presence of higher‑capacity trimmers reduces the effort needed to achieve balance through hydrogen exchange. | 0.7061137 | False |
| xnli-test-1359 | hypothesis | ಈ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯು ಸ್ಪರ್ಶಿಸಲ್ಪಡದಿದ್ದರೆ, ಅದು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ ಸಮತೋಲನದತ್ತ ಸಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | ee pratikreyeyu sparshisalppadiddare, adu antimavaagi samatoolanadatta saaguttade. | False | If this reaction is untouched, it will eventually go towards equilibrium. | If this reaction is touched, it will eventually become balanced. | 0.76144993 | False |
| xnli-test-4713 | premise | ನಾನು ಪರಿಶೀಲಿಸಿದ ನಿಘಂಟುಗಳು ಮೌನವಾಗಿವೆ-ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ನಾನು ಭಾವಿಸುತ್ತೇನೆ-ಈ ಇಂದ್ರಿಯಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ. | naanu parishilisida nighantugalu mounavaagive-aadare, naanu bhaavisuttene-ee indriyagala mele. | False | The dictionaries I have checked are silent--improperly so, I think--on these senses. | The dictionaries I have examined are silent, but I will contemplate these senses. | 0.81847644 | False |
| xnli-test-4713 | hypothesis | ನಿಘಂಟುಗಳು ಈ ಭಾವನೆಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಮಾತನಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | Nighantugalu ee bhavanegalu bagge hecchu mathaduvudilla. | False | Dictionaries do not talk about these senses very much. | Dictionaries don't say much about these buildings. | 0.38306832 | False |
| xnli-test-4204 | premise | ಪ್ರೂಡೆನ್ಸ್, ನಮ್ಮ ಸಲಹೆ ಅಂಕಣಕಾರ, ನಿವೃತ್ತರಾಗಿದ್ದಾರೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳ ಅಂಕಣವನ್ನು ಅವಳ ಸೋದರ ಸೊಸೆ, ಪ್ರೂಡೆನ್ಸ್ ಎಂದು ಸಹ ಹೆಸರಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | prudens, namma salahe ankankara, nivruttaragiddare, mattu avala ankanavannu avala sodara sose, prudens endu saha hesarisalagide. | False | Prudence, our advice columnist, has retired, and her column has been taken over by her niece, also named Prudence. | Prudens, our advice is to stay calm, and his brother also calls him Prudens. | 0.3557729 | False |
| xnli-test-4204 | hypothesis | ಮುಂದಿನ ಹತ್ತು ವರ್ಷಗಳ ವರೆಗೆ ಬುದ್ಧಿವಂತಿಕೆಯೇ ನಮ್ಮ ಸಲಹೆಗಾರರಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | mundina hattu varshagala varege buddhivantikeyee namma salahegararagiruttare. | False | Prudence will still be our advice columnist for the next ten years. | Our advisors will be wise for the next ten years. | 0.6260736 | False |
| xnli-test-2969 | premise | ಸಂಭವಿಸಬಹುದಾದ ಅಡಚಣೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ಉದಾಹರಣೆಯನ್ನು ಜನಪ್ರಿಯ ವೆಬ್ಸೈಟ್ಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ಇತ್ತೀಚಿನ ಸೇವಾ ನಿರಾಕರಣೆಯ ದಾಳಿಗಳು ಒದಗಿಸುತ್ತವೆ. | sambhavisabahudada adachanehala ondu udaharaneyanu janapriya websitegala mele itechina seva nirakaneya daligalu odagisuttave. | False | An example of disruptions that could occur is provided by recent denialofservice attacks on popular websites. | An example of a possible ad campaign is being reviewed by service evaluation teams on popular websites. | 0.24605393 | False |
| xnli-test-2969 | hypothesis | ಇತ್ತೀಚಿನ ದಿನಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಯಾವುದೇ ಸೇವಾ ನಿರಾಕರಣೆಯ ದಾಳಿ ನಡೆದಿಲ್ಲ. | itticina dinagalalli yavude seva nirakaraneya dali nadedilla. | False | There has not been a denial of service attack recently. | In these recent days, I have not taken part in any service work. | 0.23819071 | False |
| xnli-test-2678 | premise | ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ವಿಮೆಯು ಪಿಂಚಣಿ ಮತ್ತು ಇತರ ನಿವೃತ್ತಿ ಯೋಜನೆಗಳಂತಹ ಒಕ್ಕೂಟ ನೌಕರರಿಗಾಗಿ ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಲಾದ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳನ್ನು ಒಳಗೊಳ್ಳುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | samajika vimeyu pinchaani mattu itara nivrutti yojanegalantha okkuta noukararigagi sthapisalada karyakramagalannu okkolluvudilla. | False | Social insurance does not include programs established solely or primarily for Federal employees, such as pension and other retirement plans. | It does not include programs established for a single employee, such as social welfare, pension, and other retirement schemes. | 0.8147558 | False |
| xnli-test-2678 | hypothesis | ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ವಿಮೆಯು ಕೇವಲ ಫೆಡರಲ್ ಕೆಲಸಗಾರರಿಗಾಗಿ ಇರುವ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳನ್ನು ಮಾತ್ರ ಒಳಗೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | samajika vimeyu kevala federal kelasagararigagi iruva karyakramagalannu matra olagolluttade. | False | Social insurance only includes programs that are just for federal workers. | Social welfare only includes programs that are for federal employees. | 0.90416116 | False |
| xnli-test-2491 | premise | ನೀವು ರೂಲೆಟ್ ಅಥವಾ ಕ್ರ್ಯಾಪ್ಸ್ ಟೇಬಲ್ಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ರೋಲರ್ಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ಆಡಲು ಅಥವಾ ಸ್ಲಾಟ್ ಯಂತ್ರಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಕೆಲವು ನಾಣ್ಯಗಳನ್ನು ಹಾಕಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | neevu rulet athava kraps tebalgalalli hechina rolergalondige aadalu athava slot yantragalalli kelavu naanyagalannu haakalu saadhyavaaguttade. | False | You'll be able to play alongside the  high rollers  at the roulette or craps tables or put a few coins in the slot machines. | You can play high rollers at roulette or craps tables, or place some bets on slot machines. | 0.81480956 | False |
| xnli-test-2491 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ವೇಗಾಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಜೂಜಾಟವನ್ನು ಆಡಬಹುದು. | neevu vegasnalli jujatavannu aadabahudu. | False | You can gamble in Vegas. | You can play the game in Vegas. | 0.7957082 | False |
| xnli-test-3859 | premise | ನಗರದ ಇತರ ಲಾಭೋದ್ದೇಶವಿಲ್ಲದ ಚಿತ್ರಮಂದಿರಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಭಿನ್ನವಾಗಿ, ನಮ್ಮ ನಟರು ತಮ್ಮ ಕಲೆಯಿಂದ ತಮ್ಮ ಜೀವನವನ್ನು ನಡೆಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | nagarada ithara labhoddeshavillada chitramandiragaliginta bhinnavagi, namma nataru tamma kaleyinda tamma jeevanavannu nadesuttare. | False | Unlike other not-for-profit theatres in the city, our actors make their living from their craft. | Unlike other unprofitable cinemas in the city, our actors live their lives through their own art. | 0.7267424 | False |
| xnli-test-3859 | hypothesis | ನಗರದಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಲಾಭರಹಿತ ರಂಗಮಂದಿರಗಳಿವೆ. | nagaradalli ondಕ್ಕಿಂತ hecchu labharahitha rangamandiragalive. | True | There is more than one not-for-profit theater in the city. | There are more than one profitable colorful temples in the city. | 0.38195553 | False |
| xnli-test-3456 | premise | ಅನೇಕ PAPD ಅಧಿಕಾರಿಗಳು ಸಂಕೀರ್ಣದ ನೆಲಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿದ್ದರು-ಕೆಲವರು ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವಲ್ಲಿ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು, ಇತರರು 5 WTCಯಲ್ಲಿ PAPD ಡೆಸ್ಕ್ ಅನ್ನು ನಿರ್ವಹಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು ಅಥವಾ ಲಾಬಿ ಕಮಾಂಡ್ ಪೋಸ್ಟ್ಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು. | aneka PAPD akarigalu sankirnad nelamahadigalliddaru-kelavaru sthalantarisuvalli sahay maduttiddaru, itararu 5 WTeyalli PAPD desk annu nirvahisuttiddaru athava labi command postgalalli sahay maduttiddaru. | False | Many PAPD officers were on the ground floors of the complex-some assisting in evacuation, others manning the PAPD desk in 5 WTC or assisting at lobby command posts. | Several PAPD officers were posted at remote outposts—some assisted in border areas, while others operated a PAPD desk in the five WT locations or provided help at the Labi command posts. | 0.71196675 | False |
| xnli-test-3456 | hypothesis | 5 WTCಯಲ್ಲಿ PAPD ಎಲ್ಲಿಯೂ ಕಂಡುಬಂದಿಲ್ಲ. | 5 WTCyalli PAPD elliyoo kandubandilla. | False | The PAPD was nowhere to be found at 5 WTC. | PAPD was not found anywhere in the 5 WTC. | 0.9070977 | False |
| xnli-test-3553 | premise | ದಯವಿಟ್ಟು ನಮ್ಮ ರದ್ದಾದ ದಾನಿಗಳ ಪಟ್ಟಿಗೆ ಸ್ಲೈಡ್ ಮಾಡಬೇಡಿ. | Dayavittu namma raddada danigala pattige slide madabedi. | False | Please do not slide on to our lapsed donor list. | Please do not slide the page of our donors. | 0.6787009 | False |
| xnli-test-3553 | hypothesis | ನಮ್ಮ ಕಳೆದು ಹೋದ ದಾನಿಗಳ ಪಟ್ಟಿಯನ್ನು ಜಗತ್ತಿಗೆ ತೋರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಕಟಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ, ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ನನ್ನನ್ನು ನಂಬಿ, ನೀವು ಹೀಗೆ ನಾಚಿಕೆಪಡಲು ಬಯಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ! | namma kaledu hoda danigala pattiyannu jagattige torisalu prakatalagide, aadre nannu nambi, nevu hige nachikepadalu bayasuvudilla! | False | Our lapsed donor list is published for the world to see, so trust me, you don't want to be shamed like that! | Our bold film’s story has been shown to the world, but trust me, you shouldn’t be forced to like it this way. | 0.13834983 | True |
| xnli-test-1402 | premise | ವಾಂತಿ, ಅತಿಸಾರ, ತೂಕ ಕಳೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದು, ಮತ್ತು ಕೆಲವೊಮ್ಮೆ ಮರಣವೂ ಮಾಲ್ ಜೋಜೊ ಲಕ್ಷಣಗಳಾಗಿವೆ. | Vanti, atisara, thooka kaledukolluvudu, mattu kelavomme maranavoo maal jojoo lakshanagalaagive. | False | The symptoms of mal ojo are vomiting, diarrhea, loss of weight, and sometimes even death. | Vanti, extremely, taking a long time, and some also have deadly symptoms. | 0.39441773 | False |
| xnli-test-1402 | hypothesis | ಮಾಲೆ ಓಜೊ ಅನೇಕ ಋಣಾತ್ಮಕ ಲಕ್ಷಣಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದೆ. | maale ojo aneka runatmaka lakshanagalannu hondide. | False | Mal ojo has many negative symptoms. | Tomorrow Ojo has many emotional symptoms. | 0.61725754 | False |
| xnli-test-461 | premise | ಮತ್ತು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ, ನಿಮಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತು, ಅವರು ಸುತ್ತಮುತ್ತಲಿನ ಜನರನ್ನು ಕೇಳುತ್ತಲೇ ಇದ್ದರು, ಮತ್ತು ಅವರು ಎಲ್ಲಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂದು ಯಾರಿಗೂ ತಿಳಿದಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ, ಮತ್ತು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ, ನಿಮಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತು, ಅವರು ತಮ್ಮನ್ನು ತಾವೇ ಹೊಂದಿಕೊಂಡರು ಜೋವನ್ನು ಮತ್ತೆ ನೋಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲವೆಂದು. | mattu anthimavagi, nige gottu, avaru suttamuttalina janarannu keluttale iddaru, mattu avaru ellidare endu yarigu tilidiralilla, mattu anthimavagi, nige gottu, avaru tammannu tave hondikondru jovannu matte noduvudillavendu. | False | And so you know, eventually, you know, you know, they kept asking people around, and nobody knew where they were, and eventually, you know, they just reconciled themselves that they just wouldn't see Joe again. | And finally, you know, they are listening to the people who spread rumors, and no one knows where they are; and finally, you know, they have taken the copper and will not look at the youth. | 0.39449492 | False |
| xnli-test-461 | hypothesis | ಒಂದು ದಿನ ಜೋ ಈಗಷ್ಟೇ ಅಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಹೋಗಿದ್ದನು ಮತ್ತು ಅವನು ಎಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಹೋದನು ಎಂಬುದು ಯಾರಿಗೂ ತಿಳಿಯಲಿಲ್ಲ. | ondu dina joe igaste allige hogiddanu mattu avanu ellige hodanu embುದು yarigu tiliyali lla. | True | One day Joe was just gone and nobody could figure out where he went. | One day Joe went somewhere and then went somewhere else, but no one knows. | 0.8618643 | False |
| xnli-test-2704 | premise | ತುರ್ತು ವಿಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿನ ಮಧ್ಯಸ್ಥಿಕೆಗಳು ಪರಿಣಾಮಕಾರಿಯಾಗಿವೆ ಮತ್ತು ಚಿಕಿತ್ಸೆಯ ಉಲ್ಲೇಖಗಳು ಕಾರ್ಯನಿರ್ವಹಿಸಬಲ್ಲವು ಎಂದು ಪುರಾವೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ಬೆಳೆಯುತ್ತಿರುವ ಗುಂಪು ತೋರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | turtu vibhagadalli madhyastikeyalu parinamakarivagive mattu chikitseya ullekhagalu karyanirvisaballavu endu puravegala ondu beleyuttiruva gumpu torisuttade. | False | A growing body of evidence demonstrates that interventions in the emergency department are effective and that treatment referral can work. | In the turtu department, mediators are effective, treatment references can be implemented, and it shows a group that values speed. | 0.45017004 | False |
| xnli-test-2704 | hypothesis | ತುರ್ತು ವಿಭಾಗದಿಂದ ಬರುವ ಉಲ್ಲೇಖಗಳು ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡಬಹುದು. | turtu vibhagadinda baruva ullekhagalu kelasa madabahudu. | False | Referrals from the emergency department can work. | Records coming from the turtu department can be processed. | 0.21824683 | True |
| xnli-test-2937 | premise | ಸಂಶೋಧನೆಯ ಈ ಕ್ಷೇತ್ರವನ್ನು ಎತ್ತಿ ತೋರಿಸುವ ಅವರ ಕಲ್ಪನೆಯು ಶ್ರೇಷ್ಠ ಅರ್ಹತೆಯನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದರೂ, ಕಾರ್ಯಾಚರಣೆಯು ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಯಾಗಿದೆ. | samshodhaneya ee kshetravannu etti thorisuva avara kalpaneyu sreshta arhataiyannu hondiddaru, kaaryacharaneyu samasveyagide. | False | While his idea of highlighting this area of research holds great merit, operationalization is problematic. | Their conception of presenting this field of research is highly deserving, but the methodology is problematic. | 0.6700279 | False |
| xnli-test-2937 | hypothesis | ಜಾಗವನ್ನು ಸುಪ್ರಕಾಶಗೊಳಿಸುವುದರಿಂದ ಯಾವುದೆ ಮೌಲ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ. | jagavannu suprakashagolisuvudarinda yavude maulyavilla. | False | Highlighting the area has no value. | Because the world is illuminated by the supreme light, it has no value. | 0.36900914 | False |
| xnli-test-116 | premise | ಮತ್ತು, ಆ ಸಮಯದಲ್ಲಿ ನನ್ನ ಕೆಲಸಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದು, ಅಣುಬಾಂಬ್ ಸ್ವತಃ ಸ್ಫೋಟಿಸುವ ಪರಮಾಣು ಶಸ್ತ್ರಾಸ್ತ್ರಗಳ ಟ್ರಿಗರ್ ಮೇಲೆ ಪ್ಯಾರಾಶೂಟ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಹೇಗೆ ಹಾಕಬೇಕೆಂದು ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಗಳಿಗೆ ತರಬೇತಿ ನೀಡುವುದಾಗಿತ್ತು. | mattu, aa samayadalli nanna kelasagalalli ondu, anubomb svata sphotisuva paramanu shastragala trigger mele parachootgalannu hege hakabekendu vyaktigalige tarabheti niduvudagittu. | False | And, uh, one of my jobs at the time was training individuals how to put parachutes on a nuclear weapons trigger to, uh, which detonates the atomic bomb itself. | Also, at that time in my work there was a guide on how to attach parachutes to the trigger of a self‑detonating nuclear weapon for people. | 0.80640507 | False |

| xnli-test-555 | premise | ಹೌದು, ನೀವು ಬಳಸುವಂತಹ ತುಪ್ಪಳವನ್ನು ಪಡೆಯುವಂತೆ ಅವರು ಸಲಹೆ ನೀಡಿದರು | Houdu, neevu balasuvantha tuppalavannu ಪಡೆಯುವಂತೆ avaru salahe needidaru | True | yeah he suggested just getting uh um a uh um a mop like you would use | Listen, they gave you advice to get a child-sized spoon. | 0.27740937 | False |

Bepko 1995 United Way of Central Indiana Campaign Chair | Gerald L. Bepko, 1995 United Way of Central Indiana publicity head | 0.8937232 | False |

| xnli-test-1099 | premise | ಪಚೂಕಾ 1940 ರ ದಶಕದ ಪಚೂಕೋದ ಪ್ರತಿರೂಪವಾಗಿತ್ತು, ಆದರೆ ನಗರ ಕೊಳೆಗೇರಿ ಪರಿಸರದಲ್ಲಿ ಬೆಳೆಯುತ್ತಿರುವ ಚಿಕಾನಾದ ಯುವತಿಯರಲ್ಲಿ ಒಟ್ಟಿಗೆ ಬರುವ ಮನೆಯ ಹುಡುಗಿಯರ ಮಾದರಿಯೂ ಆಗಿತ್ತು. | pachuka 1940 ra dashakada pachukoda pratiroopavaittu, aadare nagara kolegeri parisaradallo beleyuttiruva chikanada yuvatiyarellu ottige baruva maneya hudugiyara maadariyoo aagittu. | False | The pachuca was the counterpart of the pachuco of the 1940s but also the home-girl archetype that comes together in the young Chicana growing up in an urban ghettoized environment. | Pachuka was a replica of the 1940s era, but the Karnataka girl living in the city college environment turned out to be the mother of a boy who came straight to the house. | 0.60859793 | False |

| xnli-test-399 | premise | ನಾನು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾದಷ್ಟು ಬೇಗ ತಿಂದೆ, ಮತ್ತು ನಂತರ ಅವಳು ಅಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಬಂದಳು ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳು ನನಗೆ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡಿದಳು. | naanu sadhyavaadashtu bega thinde, mattu nanthara avalu allige bandalu mattu avalu nanage sahaaya maadidalu. | False | I ate real quick, as fast as I could and then she came in there and she ended up helping me with it. | I ate as quickly as possible, and then she came there and helped me. | 0.8973674 | False |

| xnli-test-4799 | premise | ಇಲ್ಲಿ ಆಯಿಲ್ ಕ್ರೀಕ್ ಉದ್ದಕ್ಕೂ, ಭಾರತೀಯರು ದೇಶೀಯ ಬಳಕೆಗಾಗಿ ನೀರಿನಿಂದ ಮೇಲ್ಮೈ ತೈಲವನ್ನು ಸ್ಕಿಮ್ಡ್ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಬಿಳಿ ವಸಾಹತುಗಾರರು ಅದನ್ನು ಔಷಧೀಯ ಉದ್ದೇಶಗಳಿಗಾಗಿ ಬಾಟಲಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ತುಂಬಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅದನ್ನು ಸೆನೆಕಾ ತೈಲ ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯುತ್ತಾರೆ. | illi oil creek uddakku, bharatiyaru deshiya balakegagi neerinda melmai tailavannu skimd maduttare, mattu bili vasahatugararu adannu aushadhiya uddeshegaligagi botaliyalli tumbisuttare mattu adannu seneca taila endu kareyuttare. | False | Here along Oil Creek, Indians skimmed the surface oil off the water for domestic uses, and white settlers bottled it for medicinal purposes and called it Seneca Oil. | Here at Oil Creek, the locals skim oil from the water for the nation's strength, while the white people crush it in a pot for medicinal purposes and call it Seneca oil. | 0.8611037 | False |
| xnli-test-4799 | hypothesis | ಬಿಳಿಯರಿಗೆ ಈ ಎಣ್ಣೆಯು ಒಂದು ಪರಿಣಾಮಕಾರಿ ಔಷಧವಾಗಿತ್ತು. | biliyarige ee enneyu ondu parinamakari aushadhavagittu. | False | The oil was an effective medicine for the white settlers. | For the white person, this has become a contraceptive medicine. | 0.41771814 | False |

| xnli-test-1554 | hypothesis | ಯಾರೋ ಯಾವ ದಾರಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಹೋಗಬೇಕೆಂದು ಕೇಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Yaaro yaava daariyalli hogabekendu keluttiddare. | False | Someone is asking which way to go. | Someone is asking which road to take. | 0.8587963 | False |
| xnli-test-3475 | premise | ಅವರು ತಮ್ಮ ಹೋಟೆಲ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಉಳಿದ ತಂಡದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಸೇರಿಕೊಂಡರು. | avaru tamma hotelnalli ulida tandadondige serikondaru. | False | He joined the rest of his team at their hotel. | They met the person who stayed in their hotel. | 0.60663605 | False |
| xnli-test-3475 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಹೋಟೆಲ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಕಾಣಬಾರದೆಂದು ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರು, ಆದರೆ ಬದಲಿಗೆ ಪೋಸ್ಟ್ ಆಫೀಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಬೀದಿಯ ಉದ್ದಕ್ಕೂ ಅವರನ್ನು ಭೇಟಿಯಾಗಲು ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾಪಿಸಿದರು. | avaru hotellnalli kaanabaaradendu thlididdaru, aadare badalige post officenalli beediya uddakkoo avarannu bhetiyaagalu prasthaapisidaru. | False | He knew not to be seen at the hotel, but instead offered to meet them across the street at the post office. | They knew they would not be seen in the hotel, but instead sent them to the post office for a meeting. | 0.75606036 | False |
| xnli-test-2041 | premise | ಮತ್ತು ತಮ್ಮ ಸುಂದರವಾದ ಕರಾವಳಿ ಕೇವಲ ತಮ್ಮದಲ್ಲ ಎಂಬುದು ಅವರಿಗೆ ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರೂ, ಅವರು ಸಹಿಷ್ಣುತೆಯುಳ್ಳವರೂ ಸಹಾಯಕರರೂ ಆಗಿ ಉಳಿಯುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Mattu thamma sundaravada karavali kevala thammadalla embudu avarige thiliddaru, avaru sahishnuteyullavaru sahayakararu agi uliyuthare. | False | And they remain tolerant and helpful, even though they know that their lovely coast is no longer just theirs. | And they knew that the beautiful coastline did not belong only to them; they are patient people who serve as helpers. | 0.7149409 | False |
| xnli-test-2041 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಈಗ ಕರಾವಳಿಯನ್ನು ಇತರರೊಂದಿಗೆ ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳಬೇಕಾಗಿದೆ. | avaru iga karavaliyannu itararondige hanchikollabekaagide. | False | They have to share the coast with other people now. | They now have to share the Karavali with others. | 0.48287916 | False |
| xnli-test-3250 | premise | ಮೇ 1996ರ ಆರಂಭದಲ್ಲಿ, ಬಿನ್ ಲಾಡಿನ್ ಸುಡಾನ್ ಬಿಟ್ಟು ಹೋಗುತ್ತಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂಬ ಗುಪ್ತಚರ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಸಿಐಎ ಪಡೆದುಕೊಂಡಿತು. | May 1996ra aarambhadalli, bin ladin sudan bittu hoguttiddare enba guptachara mahitiyannu cia padekoltitu. | False | In early May 1996, the CIA received intelligence that Bin Ladin might be leaving Sudan. | In early May 1996, the CIA obtained secret intelligence that Bin Laden had left Sudan. | 0.89075166 | False |
| xnli-test-3250 | hypothesis | ಬಿನ್ ಲಾಡಿನ್ ಮೇ ತಿಂಗಳಲ್ಲೇ ಸುಡಾನ್ ಬಿಟ್ಟು ಹೋಗುತ್ತಾರೆ ಎಂದು ಸಿಐಎ ನಂಬಿತ್ತು. | Bin ladin me tingalalle sudan bittu hoguttare endu cia nambittu. | False | The CIA believed that Bin Ladin would leave Sudan as early as May. | They said they would leave after finishing the house, and I believed them. | 0.19675149 | True |
| xnli-test-2142 | premise | ನೀವು ಚಹಾ ಸಮಾರಂಭದಲ್ಲಿ ಪರಿಣತರಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ನೀವು 14 ನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ಪಿಂಗಾಣಿ ಚಹಾ ಬಟ್ಟಲುಗಳು, ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಕ್ಯಾಡಿಗಳ ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಸಂಗ್ರಹವನ್ನು ಪ್ರಶಂಸಿಸುತ್ತೀರಿ. | neevu chaha samaramabhadalli parinataragiddare, neevu 14 ne shatamana pingani chaha battulugalu, chaha ketalgalu mattu caddygalu atyuttama sangrahavanu prashamsisuttiri. | False | If you've become an adept of the tea ceremony, you'll appreciate the excellent collection of 14th-century  ceramic tea bowls, tea kettles, and caddies, as well as bamboo spoons, whisks, and flower vases. | If you took part in the tea war, you have a 14 % collection of tea leaves, tea buds and caddies, praising the finest collection. | 0.62562853 | False |
| xnli-test-2142 | hypothesis | ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳು ವಿರೂಪವಾಗಿವೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅವುಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ತಿಳಿದುಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದು ಬೇಸರದ ಸಂಗತಿ. | chaha kettlegalu viroopagive mattu avugala bagge tilidukolluvudu besarada sangati. | False | The tea kettles are ugly and it's so boring to learn about them. | Those who drink tea are lazy, and learning about them is a serious problem. | 0.58894134 | False |
| xnli-test-1887 | premise | ಬೆದರಿಕೆಗಳು ಸೇವೆ ಮಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ, ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್. | bedarikegalu seve maaduvudilla, captain. | False | Threats will not serve, Captain. | Complaints are not being served, captain. | 0.5189656 | False |
| xnli-test-1887 | hypothesis | "ಬೆದರಿಕೆಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿರುವುದು ಸ್ವೀಕಾರಾರ್ಹವಲ್ಲ," ಎಂದು ಒಬ್ಬರು ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದರು. | bedarikelannu hondiruvudu sveekararhavalla, endu obbaru captainge helidaru. | False | It was unacceptable to have threats, said one to the Captain. | Someone told the captain that the complaints are not acceptable. | 0.7315361 | False |
| xnli-test-611 | premise | ನಾವು ನಿಜವಾದ ನಿಸರ್ಗವಾದಿಗಳಲ್ಲ ಅಥವಾ ಏನೂ ಅಲ್ಲ | Navu nijavada nisargavadigalla athava enu alla | False | we're not real naturalists or anything but um | We are not of true nature or anything else | 0.6232983 | False |
| xnli-test-611 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಒಂದು ದಿನ ನಿಸರ್ಗಶಾಸ್ತ್ರಜ್ಞರಾಗಲು ಪ್ರಯತ್ನಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ ಆದರೆ ಈಗ ನಾವು ನಿಸರ್ಗಶಾಸ್ತ್ರಜ್ಞರಲ್ಲ. | navu ondu dina nisargashastrajnyaragalu prayatnisuttve adre iga navu nisargashastrajnyaralla. | False | We would like to someday strive to be naturalists but right now we are not. | We try to be natural scientists for a day, but now we are not natural scientists. | 0.66803765 | False |
| xnli-test-4334 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ನಲ್ಲೂ ಈ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಹೇಳಲಾಗದು. ಕೊಕೇನ್ ವಿವಾದದ ಕುರಿತಾದ ತನ್ನ ಸಂಪಾದಕೀಯದಲ್ಲಿ, ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ ಬುಶ್ಗೆ ಪ್ರಾಮಾಣಿಕವಾಗಿರಲು ಸಲಹೆ ನೀಡಿತು, ಮತ್ತು ದೇಶವು ತನ್ನ ಕ್ರಮವನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳಲು ಅವಕಾಶ ಮಾಡಿಕೊಟ್ಟಿತು. | New York Times nallu ee bagge helalagadu. Cocaine vivadada kuritada tanna sampadakiyalli, Times Bushge pramanikavagiru salhe neditu, mattu desavu tanna kramavannu tegedukollalu avakasa madikottitu. | False | The same can't be said for the New York Times. In its editorial on the cocaine controversy, the Times advised Bush to be honest, and to let the country take his measure. | The New York Times has not said anything about this. In its editorial criticizing the cocaine controversy, the Times advised being credible to Bush and gave the country a chance to determine its own course. | 0.79967654 | False |
| xnli-test-4334 | hypothesis | ಬುಷ್ ಶಾಶ್ವತವಾಗಿ ಸುಳ್ಳು ಹೇಳಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ ಹೇಳಿದೆ. | bush shashvatavagi sullu helabeku endu times helide. | False | The Times said Bush should just lie to everone. | They kept saying we must keep lying forever. | 0.46178344 | False |
| xnli-test-4209 | premise | ಅವರು ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳನ್ನು ಗುಂಡಿಕ್ಕಿ ಕೊಲ್ಲುತ್ತಾರೆ, ಅಲ್ಲವೇ? | avaru vidyarthigalannu gundikki kolluthare, allave? | False | They Shoot Students, Don't They? | They are cheating the students, right? | 0.5641039 | False |
| xnli-test-4209 | hypothesis | ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ದಾಳಿ ನಡೆಸಲು ಅವರ ಬಳಿ ಶಸ್ತ್ರಾಸ್ತ್ರಗಳಿವೆ. | vidyarthigala mele dali nadesalu avara bali shastrastragaluve. | False | They have weapons they use against students. | We will place their sacrificial weapons on the students. | 0.69506514 | False |
| xnli-test-1028 | premise | [ಈ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರವನ್ನು] ಸ್ವಾತಂತ್ರ್ಯದಲ್ಲಿ ರೂಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು ಮತ್ತು ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪುರುಷರು ಸಮಾನವಾಗಿ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂಬ ವಾದಕ್ಕೆ ಸಮರ್ಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | [ee rashtravannu] svatantryadalli roopisalayitu mattu ella purusharu samanavagi srustisalpattaare emba vaadakke samarpisalayitu. | False | [This nation was] conceived in liberty and dedicated to the proposition that all men are created equal. | This country was painted with freedom and dedicated to the claim that all men were created equal. | 0.72738695 | False |
| xnli-test-1028 | hypothesis | ಕೆಲವು ಜನರು ಇತರರಿಗಿಂತ ಅಂತರ್ಗತವಾಗಿ ಉತ್ತಮರು ಎಂಬ ನಂಬಿಕೆಯ ಮೇಲೆ ಈ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರವನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | kelavu janaru itharagintha antharghathavaagi uththamaru emba nambikeya mele ee rashtravannu sthaapisalaayithu. | False | This nation was founded on the belief that some people are inherently better than others. | Some people, believing themselves to be superior, established this country. | 0.59438276 | False |
| xnli-test-2112 | premise | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯು ಬೆಚ್ಚಗಿನ (ಆದರೆ ಬಿಸಿ ಅಲ್ಲ) ಹವಾಮಾನ ಮತ್ತು ಬೆಚ್ಚಗಿನ ಸಮುದ್ರ ತಾಪಮಾನವನ್ನು ತರುತ್ತದೆ, ಇದು ಡೈವಿಂಗ್, ಸ್ನಾರ್ಕ್ಲಿಂಗ್ ಮತ್ತು ಇತರ ಜಲ ಕ್ರೀಡೆಗಳಿಗೆ ಸೂಕ್ತವಾಗಿದೆ. | besigeyu bechchagina (aadare bisi alla) havaamaana mattu bechchagina samudra taapamaanavannu taruttade, idu daiving, snaarkling mattu itara jala kreetegalige sooktavaagide. | False | Summer brings warm (but not hot) weather and warm sea temperatures, making it ideal for diving, snorkeling, and other water sports. | The breeze is cool (not hot) and brings a sea temperature, making it suitable for diving, snorkeling and other water activities. | 0.6595632 | False |
| xnli-test-2112 | hypothesis | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಇಲ್ಲಿ ತಾಪಮಾನ 100 ಡಿಗ್ರಿ ಸೆಲ್ಸಿಯಸ್ ಇರುತ್ತದೆ. | besigeyalli illi tapamana 100 degree celsius iruttade. | False | It is always 100 degrees or hotter in the summer. | The temperature here in the kitchen is 100 degrees Celsius. | 0.5216363 | False |
| xnli-test-2927 | premise | ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಎಂಜಿನಿಯರಿಂಗ್ ಮತ್ತು ಒಪ್ಪಂದದ ಮಾತುಕತೆಗೆ ಆರರಿಂದ ಎಂಟು ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು ಬೇಕಾಗಿದ್ದರೂ, ಎರಡು 900 ಮೆಗಾವ್ಯಾಟ್ ಘಟಕಗಳನ್ನು ಪೂರ್ಣಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಒಟ್ಟು 17ರಿಂದ 19 ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು ಬೇಕಾಗುತ್ತವೆ. | arambhika engineering mattu oppandada maatukatege aarinda entu tingalugalu bekagiddaru, eradu 900 megawatt ghatakagalannu purnagolisalu ottu 17rinda 19 tingalugalu bekaguttave. | False | Even if preliminary engineering and contract negotiation took as long as six to eight months, the total time for completing two 900 MWe units would be about 17 to 19 months. | The initial engineering and the required discussions needed some items, and to complete two 900‑megawatt units 17 to 19 items are required. | 0.527838 | False |
| xnli-test-2927 | hypothesis | ಒಟ್ಟು ಸಮಯವು 6 ತಿಂಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆಯಿದೆ. | ottu samayavu 6 thingaliginta kadimeyide. | False | The total time is less than 6 months. | The total time is less than six minutes. | 0.47676688 | False |
| xnli-test-1602 | premise | ನಾನು ಈ ವರ್ಷದ ಹಿಂದೆ ಅವನನ್ನು ಬೇಟೆಯಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದೆ. | nanu ee varshada hinde avanannu beteyaduttidde. | False | I've been hunting him this year past. | I have been beating him since the beginning of this year. | 0.44484228 | False |
| xnli-test-1602 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಅವರನ್ನು ಒಂದು ವರ್ಷದಿಂದ ಹತ್ತಿರದಿಂದ ಹಿಂಬಾಲಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದೇನೆ. | nanu avaranu ondu varshadinda hattiradinda himbalisuttiddene. | False | I have been following him closely for a year without him knowing. | I have been teasing him for a year since childhood. | 0.4906215 | False |
| xnli-test-2832 | premise | ನಾವು ತಲಾ ಪರಿಮಾಣ ಮತ್ತು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಪ್ರತಿ ತುಣುಕುಗಳ ನಡುವೆ ನೇರ ರೇಖೀಯ ಸಂಬಂಧವನ್ನು ಊಹಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ | navu thala parimaana maththu sadhyaviruva prathi thunukugala naduve nera rekhiya sambandhavannu oohisutheve | False | We assume a direct linear relationship between volume per capita and pieces per possible | We are considering the direct linear relationship between head size and each possible measurement. | 0.4420306 | False |
| xnli-test-2832 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಪ್ರತಿ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾದ ತುಣುಕುಗಳಂತೆ ಪರಿಮಾಣವು ನಿಖರವಾಗಿ ಒಂದೇ ಎಂದು ಊಹಿಸಿದೆವು. | navu prati sadhyavada tunukugalante parimanavu nikharavagi onde endu uhisdevu. | False | We figured the volume is exactly the same as the pieces per possible. | We will declare that the amount is exactly one, like a needle of every possible kind. | 0.46532708 | False |
| xnli-test-3845 | premise | ಬಲವಾದ ಹಣಕಾಸಿನ ನಿರ್ವಹಣೆ, ಚಲನಶೀಲ ರಂಗಭೂಮಿ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಶೈಕ್ಷಣಿಕ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳ ದಾಖಲೆಯನ್ನು ಮುಂದುವರಿಸಲು ನಮಗೆ ಇನ್ನೂ ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸಹಾಯ ಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ನಿಮಗೆ ತಿಳಿಸಲು ಈ ಪತ್ರವನ್ನು ಬರೆಯಲಾಗಿದೆ. | balavada hanakasina nirvahane, chalanasheela rangabhoomi nirmaanagalu mattu atyuttama kaikshanika karyakramagala dakkaleyannu munduvarisalu namage innu nimma sahaya beku endu nimge tilisalu ee patravannu bareyalagide. | False | This letter is to let you know that we still need your help to continue our record of strong fiscal management, vibrant theatrical productions and outstanding educational programs. | We are writing this letter to let you know that we still need your help to advance the effective implementation, the development of dynamic playgrounds, and the best instant programs. | 0.56251276 | False |
| xnli-test-3845 | hypothesis | ನಮ್ಮ ರಂಗಭೂಮಿ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣಗಳು ಮುಂದುವರಿಯಲು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸಹಾಯ ಬೇಕು. | namma rangabhumi nirmanagalu munduvariyalu nimma sahaya beku. | False | We need your help to keep our theatrical productions running. | We need your help to move forward with our stage constructions. | 0.6228809 | False |
| xnli-test-3648 | premise | ನಿಮಗೆ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡಲು, ಬರೆಯಲು, ಫ್ಯಾಕ್ಸ್ ಅಥವಾ ಇ-ಮೇಲ್ ಮತ್ತು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮಗೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ತಿಳಿಸಿ. | nimage sahaaya maalu, bareyalu, phaks athavaa i-mel mattu nimma bagge nige hecchu thilisi. | False | To better help us assist you, write, fax or E-mail and tell us more about yourself. | To help you, write, fax, or email and tell us more about yourself. | 0.9073441 | False |
| xnli-test-3648 | hypothesis | ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮಗೆ ಸಾಕಷ್ಟು ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರೆ ನಾವು ನಿಮ್ಮನ್ನು ದಿನಾಂಕದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಹೊಂದಿಸಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವಲ್ಲಿ ಉತ್ತಮವಾದ ಕೆಲಸವನ್ನು ಮಾಡುತ್ತೇವೆ. | nimma bagge namage sakuṣṭu tiḷididdare nāvu nimmannu dināṅkadoḍondige hondsikoḷḷuvalli uttamavāda kelasavannu māḍuttēve. | True | We do a better job at matching you with a date if we know a lot about you. | If we know a little about you, we will do the best work in taking care of you every day. | 0.5405082 | False |
| xnli-test-2853 | premise | ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ಟೈಟಲ್ V ಕಾರ್ಯಾಚರಣಾ ಅನುಮತಿಯನ್ನು ಮಾರ್ಪಡಿಸಲು ಒಟ್ಟು ಅಂದಾಜು ಸಮಯವು ಸುಮಾರು 17 ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು, ಜೊತೆಗೆ ಅನುಸರಣೆ ಪರೀಕ್ಷೆಯನ್ನು ಪೂರ್ಣಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಹೆಚ್ಚುವರಿ ಸಮಯ. | aatradari, title V kaaryaacharana anumatiyannu maarpadisalu ottu andaaju samayavu sumaru 17 tingalugalu, jothege anusarane pariksheyannu purnagolisalu hechchuvari samaya. | False | Therefore, the total estimated time to modify the Title V operating permit is about 17 months, plus the additional time to complete compliance testing. | At present, to change the Title V work‑execution permission, the allotted time is exactly 17 minutes, along with enough time to complete the compliance test. | 0.640587 | False |
| xnli-test-2853 | hypothesis | ಟೈಟಲ್ V ಆಪರೇಟಿಂಗ್ ಪರ್ಮಿಟ್ ಅನ್ನು ಮಾರ್ಪಡಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ. | Title V operating permit annu madparisalu sadhyavilla. | False | It is not possible to modify the Title V operating permit. | It is not possible to renew the Title V operating permit. | 0.91088325 | False |
| xnli-test-4794 | premise | "ಆಲಿಸ್ ಆಕ್ಷೇಪಿಸಿದಾಗ," "ಆದರೆ ಅದು ವಿಭಿನ್ನ ರೀತಿಯ ಕಟ್ಟುಪಾಡು," "ಅವರು ಉತ್ತರಿಸಿದರು," "ಇದು ನನ್ನೊಂದಿಗೆ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ರೀತಿಯ ಕಟ್ಟುಪಾಡಾಗಿತ್ತು, ನಾನು ನಿಮಗೆ ಭರವಸೆ ನೀಡಬಲ್ಲೆ!" | Alis akshepisidaaga, "Aadare adu vibhinna reetiya kattupaadu," Avaru uttarisidaru, "Idu nannondige ella reetiya kattupaadagittu, naanu nimage bharavase needaballe!" | False | When Alice objected,  But that's a different kind of fastness,  he replied,  It was all kinds of fastness with me, I can assure you! | When Alis objected, “But that’s a different kind of nonsense,” he replied, “It has been nonsense in every way for me, and I’ll assure you.” | 0.39874876 | False |
| xnli-test-4794 | hypothesis | ಇವೆಲ್ಲವನ್ನೂ ಆಲಿಸ್ ಒಪ್ಪಿಕೊಂಡಿದ್ದರು. | ivellavannoo alis oppikondiddaru. | False | Alice agreed with all of it. | They all sat together. | 0.348093 | False |
| xnli-test-3746 | premise | ತಿಳಿವಳಿಕೆ ಮತ್ತು ಬದ್ಧತೆಯ ನಾಯಕರ ಹೊಸ ಪೀಳಿಗೆಯನ್ನು ನಾವು ಅಭಿವೃದ್ಧಿಪಡಿಸುವ ಸಲುವಾಗಿ ನಾವು ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರಿಗೆ ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಪ್ರಾಮುಖ್ಯತೆಯ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಶಿಕ್ಷಣ ನೀಡುವುದು ನಿರ್ಣಾಯಕವಾಗಿದೆ. | thilivalike mattu baddhathreya nayakara hosa peeligeyannu naavu abhivrudhipadisuva saluvaagi naavu amerikannarige daanadharmada praamukhyatheya bagge shikshana neevuduvudhu nirnaayakavaagide. | False | It is critical that we educate Americans about the importance of Philanthropy that we develop a new generation of informed and committed leaders. | By developing the new policy of the leaders of Thilivalike and Baddhathre, we are teaching Americans about the importance of charity, which is decisive. | 0.6018998 | False |
| xnli-test-3746 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಸಾರ್ವಜನಿಕ ಶಾಲೆಗಳನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿಕೊಂಡು ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರಿಗೆ ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಮಹತ್ವವನ್ನು ಕಲಿಸಬೇಕು. | naavu sarvajanika shaalegalannu balasikondu amerikanarige daanadharmada mahatvannu kalisabeku. | False | We should use public schools to teach Americans about the importance of philanthropy. | We should teach Americans the importance of charity by supporting public schools. | 0.8691138 | False |
| xnli-test-3079 | premise | ಸೌತ್ ಟವರ್ ಕುಸಿತವನ್ನು ಗಮನಿಸಿದ ಈ ಅಧಿಕಾರಿ, ತನ್ನ ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವ ಸೂಚನೆಯ ಮೂಲಕ ಉತ್ತರ ಟವರ್ನಲ್ಲಿರುವ ESU ಘಟಕಗಳಿಗೆ ವರದಿಯನ್ನು ಸಲ್ಲಿಸಿದರು. | South tower kusithavannu gamanisida ee adhikari, thanna sthalantharisuva soochaneya moolaka uttara towernalliroduva ESU ghatakagalige varadiyannu salisidaru. | False | This officer, who had observed the South Tower collapse, reported it to ESU units in the North Tower in his evacuation instruction. | The official who inspected the South tower’s vibration sent a warning, via his relocation signal, to the ESU components operating the North tower. | 0.623579 | False |
| xnli-test-3079 | hypothesis | ಉತ್ತರ ಗೋಪುರದಲ್ಲಿ ಇಎಸ್ ಯು ಘಟಕಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ಅಧಿಕಾರಿ ಮಾತನಾಡುವ 30 ನಿಮಿಷಗಳ ಮೊದಲು ದಕ್ಷಿಣ ಗೋಪುರವು ಕುಸಿದು ಬಿದ್ದಿತು. | uttara gopuradalli isu ghatakangalondige adhikari matanaduva 30 nimishagala modalu dakshina gopuravu kusidu bidditu. | False | The South Tower collapsed 30 minutes before the officer talked to ESU units in the North Tower. | In the north tower, the official will speak to the victims for 30 minutes before the south tower collapsed. | 0.7825834 | False |
| xnli-test-1766 | premise | ನಮ್ಮಲ್ಲಿ ಬಿಷಪ್ನ ಸೋದರಳಿಯರ ಸರಕು ಇದ್ದರೂ ಅದು ಅವನ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಹಿಡಿಯುವಂತೆ ಮಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | nammalli bishopna sodaraliyara saraku iddaru adu avana kaiyannu hidiyuvante maduvudilla. | False | Though we had a cargo of Bishop's nieces it wouldn't make him hold his hand. | In our place the bishop’s brothers were there, and they would never do anything like covering his hand. | 0.54530025 | False |
| xnli-test-1766 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ತುಂಬ ಒತ್ತಡದ ಸಮಯಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಮಾತ್ರ ಅವನ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಹಿಡಿದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು. | avaru tumbaa ottadada samayagalalli maatra avana kaiyannu hididukolluttiddaru. | False | They would only hold his hand in times of great stress. | They only kept his hand hidden during very busy times. | 0.6361261 | False |
| xnli-test-2771 | premise | ಡೇಟಾದ ವಿಶ್ವಾಸಾರ್ಹತೆಯನ್ನು ನಿರ್ಧರಿಸಲು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಆಧಾರವನ್ನು ಪ್ರಸ್ತುತಪಡಿಸಿ. | Datada vishwasarhateyannu nirdharisalu nimma adharavannu prastutupadisi. | False | Present your basis for assessing the reliability of the data as undetermined. | Present your evidence to determine the data’s reliability. | 0.80411357 | False |
| xnli-test-2771 | hypothesis | ಈ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಸಂಪೂರ್ಣವಾಗಿ ವಿಶ್ವಾಸಾರ್ಹ ಎಂದು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | ee mahitiyannu sampurnavagi vishwasarh endu pariganisalagide. | False | The data was assessed to be entirely reliable. | This information is considered completely reliable. | 0.75513244 | False |
| xnli-test-350 | premise | ಆಗ ನಾನು ನನ್ನ ಚಕ್ರವನ್ನು ಸುತ್ತುತ್ತಿದ್ದೆ, ನನಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ, ದಿನವಿಡೀ ಹಾಗೆ ತೋರುತ್ತಿತ್ತು. | aaga nanu nanna chakravannu suttuttidde, nanage gottilla, dinavidi haage toruttittu. | False | Then I was spinning my wheel for about, I don't know, it seemed like all day that day. | Then I was turning my wheel, I didn’t know, and the day turned out like that. | 0.72330683 | False |
| xnli-test-350 | hypothesis | ಬಹಳ ಹೊತ್ತು ಚಕ್ರ ತಿರುಗಿಸಿದ್ದೆ. | bahala hottu chakra thirugisidde. | False | I spun my wheel for a long time. | It was very hot and the wheel was turning. | 0.53351206 | False |
| xnli-test-279 | premise | ಆಕೆಯ ಸಹೋದರಿ ಬಿಳಿ ಬಣ್ಣಕ್ಕೆ ದಾಟಬಹುದು, ಮತ್ತು ವಾಸ್ತವವಾಗಿ ಬಿಳಿ ಬಣ್ಣಕ್ಕೆ ದಾಟಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | aakeya sahodari bili bannakke daatabahudu, mattu vaastavaagi bili bannakke daatiddare. | False | Her sister could pass for white, and in fact did pass for white. | My sister can be painted white, and indeed was painted white. | 0.7047546 | False |
| xnli-test-279 | hypothesis | ಅವಳ ಸಹೋದರಿಯು ನೆರೆಹೊರೆಯ ಎಲ್ಲರ ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಸುಂದರವಾದ ಚರ್ಮವನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಳು. | avala sahodariyu nerehooreya ellara atyanta sundaravada carmavannu hondiddalu. | False | Her sister had the fairest skin of anyone in the neighborhood. | Her sister also found the extremely beautiful charm of everyone in Nerehoore. | 0.5245973 | False |
| xnli-test-840 | premise | ತೋಳಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ನೃತ್ಯಗಳು ನಾನು ಕುರಿಮರಿಗಳ ಮೌನವನ್ನು ನೋಡಿದ್ದೇನೆ ಎಂದು ನಾವು ನೋಡಿದ್ದೇವೆ | tolagalondige nrutyagalu nanu kurimarigala mounavannu nodiddene endu navu nodiddevi | False | uh Dances With Wolves we just saw that kind of late what else have i seen uh Silence of the Lambs | From the very beginning, the dances, I saw the silence of the crickets, and that is what we saw. | 0.33627105 | False |
| xnli-test-840 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ವೋಲ್ಫ್ಸ್ ಜೊತೆ ಡ್ಯಾನ್ಸ್ ನೋಡಿದೆ. | naanu wolfs jote dance nodide. | False | I watched Dances with Wolves. | I saw dancing with wolves. | 0.8685026 | False |
| xnli-test-1076 | premise | ವಿಶ್ವವು ಕನಿಷ್ಠ ಒಮ್ಮೆಯಾದರೂ ಉದ್ದದ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪ್ರೋಟೀನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ತಯಾರಿಸಲು ವಿಶ್ವದ ಪ್ರಸಕ್ತ ಜೀವನಕಾಲಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಕನಿಷ್ಠ ಅನೇಕ ಪಟ್ಟು ಸಮಯ ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | vishvavu kanishta ommeyadaru uddada sadhyaviruva ella proteingalanu tayarisalu vishvada prasakta jeevanakalakkinta kanishta aneka pattu samaya tgedukolluttade. | False | It would take at least many times the current lifetime of the universe for the universe to manage to make all possible proteins of length at least once. | The world, even the smallest organism, takes a great deal of time to produce all the possible proteins that could exist, compared to the lifespan of a living being on Earth. | 0.6735711 | False |
| xnli-test-1076 | hypothesis | ಇದು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪ್ರೋಟೀನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ತಯಾರಿಸಲು 100 ಶತಕೋಟಿ ವರ್ಷಗಳನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | idu sadhyaviruva ella protinkalannu thayarisalu 100 shatakoti varshagalannu thegedukolluthade. | False | It wuold take 100 billion years to make all possible proteins. | It takes 100 billion years to prepare all possible proteins. | 0.9331258 | False |
| xnli-test-2036 | premise | "" "ಮೆಡಿಟರೇನಿಯನ್ನಲ್ಲಿನ ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಬಂದರುಗಳು ಜೂನ್, ಜುಲೈ, ಆಗಸ್ಟ್ ಮತ್ತು ಮಾಯೆ" "ಎಂದು 16ನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ವೆನಿಸ್ ಅಡ್ಮಿರಲ್ ಆಂಡ್ರಿಯಾ ಡೋರಿಯಾ ಹೇಳಿದರು, ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯ ಸವಾರಿಯ ಹೊರತಾಗಿ, ಒಂದು ನೌಕಾಪಡೆಯು ಇಲ್ಲಿ ಆಶ್ರಯಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಉತ್ತಮವಾಗಿ ಕಾರ್ಯನಿರ್ವಹಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ." | "mediterrenyannalli na atyuttama bandrugalu june, julai, agust mattu maye" endu 16ne shatamandada venis admiral andriya doria helidaru, besigeya savariya horataagi, ondu naukapadeyu illi ashrayakkinta uttamavagi karyanirvahisalu sadhyavilla. | False | The best ports in the Mediterranean are June, July, August, and Mae,  said the 16th-century Venetian admiral Andrea Doria, noting that beyond the summer sailing season, a fleet couldn't do better than shelter here. | He said that the 16th‑century Venetian admiral Andrea Doria, in a hurry, could not operate even a single warship here any better than by relying on it. | 0.36829707 | False |
| xnli-test-2036 | hypothesis | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯ ಋತುವಿನಲ್ಲಿ ಉತ್ತಮ ಬಂದರುಗಳಿವೆ. | besigeya rutuvinalli uttama bandarulive. | False | The summer season has the best ports. | The best has arrived in the busy season. | 0.32911626 | False |
| xnli-test-1234 | premise | ಸರ್ಕ್ಯೂಟ್ ನ್ಯಾಯಾಧೀಶರಾಗಿ ಏಕಾಂಗಿಯಾಗಿ ಕುಳಿತು, ಮುಖ್ಯ ನ್ಯಾಯಮೂರ್ತಿ ಟ್ಯಾನಿ ಸಾಂವಿಧಾನಿಕ ನಿಬಂಧನೆಗಳನ್ನು ವ್ಯಾಖ್ಯಾನಿಸುತ್ತಾ, ರಿಟ್ ಅರ್ಜಿಯನ್ನು ಅಮಾನತುಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ನ ಅಧಿಕಾರದ ಅಗತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಪ್ರತಿಪಾದಿಸಿದರು. | circuit nyayadhisharagi ekangiyagi kulitu, mukhya nyayamurthi tany savidhanika nibandhanegalalnu vyakhyanisutta, writ arjiyannu amanatugolisalu congressna adhikarada avashyakatayannu pratipadisdaru. | False | Sitting alone as a circuit judge, Chief Justice Taney interpreted the constitutional provision to require congressional authorization for suspending the writ. | Having sat alone as a circuit judge, he explained the constitutional provisions of the chief justice and asserted the necessity of Congress’s authority to entrust the writ petition. | 0.7484875 | False |
| xnli-test-1234 | hypothesis | ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ಗೆ ಅಧಿಕಾರ ಹಿಡಿಯಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ ಎಂದು ಟಾನಿ ಹೇಳಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Congressge adhikaara hidiyalu sadhyavilla endu Tony heliddare. | False | Taney said Congress had no power to stop the writ. | Tony said that it is not possible to take away power from the Congress. | 0.5733596 | False |
| xnli-test-2959 | premise | ಸುಮಾರು 2,100 ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯ ವೃತ್ತಿಪರರು ತಮ್ಮ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯ ವರದಿಗಳನ್ನು ಆನ್-ಲೈನ್ ಮೂಲಕ 1999ರಲ್ಲಿ ಪಡೆದುಕೊಂಡರು, ಮತ್ತು HIC ಇತರ ವೈದ್ಯಕೀಯ ವೃತ್ತಿಪರರಿಗೆ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯನ್ನು ಸೇರಿಸಲು ಮತ್ತಷ್ಟು ಸುಧಾರಣೆಗಳನ್ನು ಕಾರ್ಯಗತಗೊಳಿಸಿತು. | sumaru 2,100 samanya vruttipararu thamma prathikriyeya varadigalannu on-line moolaka 1999ralli padekonbaru, mattu HIC ithara vaidakeeya vruttipararige prathikriyeyannu serisalu mathastu sudharanegalannu karyagathagolisithu. | False | About 2,100 general practitioners accessed their feedback reports on-line during 1999, and HIC implemented further enhancements to include feedback to other medical practitioners. | In 1999, about 2,100 ordinary professionals submitted their feedback online, and HIC also added feedback for other legal professionals and implemented the necessary improvements. | 0.8181179 | False |
| xnli-test-2959 | hypothesis | ಎಚ್ಐಸಿ ಯಾವುದೇ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆ ಬದಲಾವಣೆಗಳನ್ನು ಮಾಡಿಲ್ಲ. | HIC yavude pratikriye badalavanegalannu madilla. | False | HIC made no feedback changes. | He/she does not change any response. | 0.33068824 | False |
| xnli-test-3276 | premise | ಜಾರ್ಜ್ ಎಚ್. ಡಬ್ಲ್ಯು. ಬುಷ್ ಅವರ ಆಡಳಿತದಲ್ಲಿ ಎನ್. ಎಸ್. ಸಿ. ಯ ಸದಸ್ಯರಾಗಿದ್ದ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಭದ್ರತಾ ಸಲಹೆಗಾರ ಕಾಂಡೊಲೀಜಾ ರೈಸ್ ಅವರು ಬುಷ್ ಅವರ ವೈಟ್ ಹೌಸ್ ಸಿಬ್ಬಂದಿಯ ಮುಖ್ಯ ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಗಳಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | George H. W. Bush avra adalitadalli N. S. C. ya sadasyaragida rastriya bhadrata salahegara Condoleezza Rice avru Bush avra White House sibbandiya mukhya vyaktigalagiruttare. | False | The principal figures on Bush's White House staff would be National Security Advisor Condoleezza Rice, who had been a member of the NSC staff in the administration of George H.W. | In George H. W. Bush’s administration, Condoleezza Rice, who was a member of the NSC and the national security adviser, was one of the key figures associated with Bush’s White House. | 0.8128203 | False |
| xnli-test-3276 | hypothesis | ಕಾಂಡೋಲೀಜಾ ರೈಸ್ ಬುಷ್ನ ಸಿಬ್ಬಂದಿಯ ಮೇಲೆ ತುಲನಾತ್ಮಕವಾಗಿ ಅಜ್ಞಾತ ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಯಾಗಿದ್ದರು. | kondoliza rais bushna sibbandiya mele tulanatmakavagi agnyata vyaktiyagiddaru. | False | Condoleezza Rice was a relatively unknown figure on Bush's staff. | They were an ignorant person who behaved arrogantly toward the relationship. | 0.110684134 | True |

| xnli-test-433 | premise | ಇದು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಸರಳವಾಗಿದೆ, ಅವಳು ನನಗೆ ನೀಡಿದಂತೆಯೇ ಎಲ್ಲವೂ ವಿವರವಾಗಿ ಮತ್ತು ಜಟಿಲವಾಗಿದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಎರಡನೆಯದು ಸರಳವಾಗಿದೆ. | idu heccu saralavagide, avalu nanage nididanteye ellavu vivaravagi mattu jatilavagide mattu eradaneyadu saralavagide. | False | It's like more simplified, like the one that she had given me was all detailed and complicated and the second one is all simple. | This is very simple, she is like a dream to me, everything is detailed and complex, and the second one is simple. | 0.61960995 | False |
| xnli-test-433 | hypothesis | ಅವಳು ನನಗೆ ಎರಡು ರೀತಿಯ ಸೂಚನೆಗಳನ್ನು ನೀಡಿದಳು ಮತ್ತು ನಾನು ವಿವರವಾದ ಸೂಚನೆಗಳನ್ನು ಆದ್ಯತೆ ನೀಡಿದ್ದೆ. | avalu nanage eradu reetiya soochanaigalannu needidalu mattu naanu vivaradaada soochanaigalannu aadyate needidde. | False | She gave me two types of instructions and i prefered the detailed ones. | She asked me for two types of information, and I first requested detailed information. | 0.49177086 | False |
| xnli-test-2784 | premise | ಜರ್ಮನ್ SCR ವ್ಯವಸ್ಥೆಯ ಪೂರೈಕೆದಾರ ನಾಲ್ಕು ವಾರಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆ ಅವಧಿಯ ಅವಧಿಯ ಒಳಗೆ ಜರ್ಮನ್ ಸಾಮರ್ಥ್ಯದ ಒಂದು ಗಮನಾರ್ಹ ಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿ SCR ಅನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಿದನು. | german SCR vyavastheya puraikedara nalku varagaliginta kadime avadhiya avadhiya olage german samarthyadha ondu gamanarha bhagadalli SCR annu sthapisidanu. | False | A German SCR system supplier installed SCR on a significant portion of the German capacity within outage periods consisting of less than four weeks. | German SCR was established in less than four years, and within that short period a capable German segment set up the SCR. | 0.62882435 | False |
| xnli-test-2784 | hypothesis | ಜರ್ಮನಿಯ SCR ವ್ಯವಸ್ಥೆಯು 20 ವರ್ಷಗಳಿಂದ ಯುರೋಪ್ನಲ್ಲಿದೆ. | germaniya SCR vyavasteyu 20 varshagalinda europnallide. | False | A German SCR system has been in Europe for 20 years. | Germany's SCR system has been in Europe for 20 years. | 0.938125 | False |
| xnli-test-1486 | premise | ಈ ಗುಂಪಿನಲ್ಲಿ ಇಪ್ಪತ್ತನೇ ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯೂ ಇದೆ, ಇದು ಕೊಲಂಬಿಯಾ ಜಿಲ್ಲೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಅರ್ಹರಾಗಿರದ ನಾಗರಿಕರಿಗೆ ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷ ಮತ್ತು ಉಪಾಧ್ಯಕ್ಷರಿಗೆ ಮತ ಚಲಾಯಿಸುವ ಹಕ್ಕನ್ನು ವಿಸ್ತರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | ee gumpinalli ippattene thidupadiyu ide, idu colombia jillayalli arharagirada nagarikarige adhyaksha mattu upadhyaksharige matha chalayisuva hakkannu vistharistade. | False | Also in this group is the Twenty-Third Amendment, which extends the right to vote for the president and vice president to citizens otherwise qualified in the District of Columbia. | In this group there is currently a dispute, and it expands the right of the mayor and deputy mayor of the city in Colombia district to conduct religious activities. | 0.33628827 | False |
| xnli-test-1486 | hypothesis | 23 ನೇ ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯು ನೀವು ರಾಜಧಾನಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ವಾಸಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದರೆ ನೀವು ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರಪತಿಗೆ ಮತ ಹಾಕಬಹುದು ಎಂದು ಹೇಳುತ್ತದೆ. | 23 ne thiddupadiyu neevu rajadhaniyalli vasisuttiddare neevu rashtrapatige matha hakabahudu endu heluttade. | False | The 23rd amendment says you can vote for the president if you live in the capital unless you were a felon. | If you are 23 years old and living in the capital, you can vote for the president, it says. | 0.7257701 | False |
| xnli-test-4423 | premise | ನಾನು ನನಗಾಗಿ ಒಂದು ಸೆಟ್ ಅನ್ನು ಆರ್ಡರ್ ಮಾಡಬೇಕೋ? | naanu nanagaagi ondu set annu order maabeku? | False | Would I order a set for myself? | Should I order a set for myself? | 0.91828704 | False |
| xnli-test-4423 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಸೆಟ್ ನಿರ್ಲಕ್ಷಿಸುತ್ತೀರಾ? | Naanu set nirlakshisuttira? | False | Would I ignore the set? | Am I being watched? | 0.12641305 | True |
| xnli-test-2849 | premise | ವಿದೇಶಿ ಕೃಷಿ ಕಾರ್ಮಿಕರು ಯುನೈಟೆಡ್ ಸ್ಟೇಟ್ಸ್ನೊಳಗೆ ಆಗಾಗ್ಗೆ ಚಲಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | videshi krushi karmikaru united statesnologe aagagge chalisuttare. | False | Alien farmworkers move frequently within the United States. | Foreign farm workers are going to the United States. | 0.6962388 | False |
| xnli-test-2849 | hypothesis | "" "ವಲಸಿಗರು ಅಮೆರಿಕಕ್ಕೆ ಬಂದಾಗ ಎಂದಿಗೂ ಚಲಿಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ." | valasigaru americakke bandaga endigu calisuvudilla. | False | Immigrants never move once they arrive in the US. | Valasigaru never works when he comes to America. | 0.2555011 | False |
| xnli-test-1636 | premise | ಬಂದರಿನಾದ್ಯಂತ ಅರೇಬೆಲ್ಲಾದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಅಬೀಮ್, ನೀರಿನ ಅಂಚಿಗೆ ಇಳಿದ ಆ ಭವ್ಯ ನಗರದ ಫ್ಲಾಟ್-ಫ್ರಂಟ್ ಬಿಳಿ ಕಟ್ಟಡಗಳಾಗಿದ್ದವು. | bandarinadyanta arebelladondige abeem, neerina anchige ilida aa bhavya nagarada flat-front bili kattadagalagiddavu. | False | Abeam with the Arabella, across the harbour, were the flat-fronted white buildings of that imposing city that came down to the very water's edge. | At the very end of the band, there was a huge flat‑front white building in that magnificent city. | 0.5657488 | False |
| xnli-test-1636 | hypothesis | ಅರೇಬೆಲ್ಲಾ ಬಂದರಿನ ತುದಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ನಗರದ ಉಳಿದ ಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿತ್ತು. | Arebella bandarina thudiyalli nagarada ulida bhagadallithtu. | False | The Arabella was located just across the harbour's edge where the rest of the city was located. | In the middle of the road where the police came, there was a big pothole. | 0.035452116 | True |
| xnli-test-3380 | premise | "ಸಿಎನ್ಎನ್ ಮುಂದೆ ಸುಟ್ಟ ಪ್ರೆಡೇಟರ್ ಅನ್ನು ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ ಮೆರವಣಿಗೆ ಮಾಡುವಾಗ ಸಂಭಾವ್ಯ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮವನ್ನು ಮುಕ್ತಾಯಗೊಳಿಸುವ ಅಪಾಯವನ್ನು ಮೀರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ ಎಂದು ನಾನು ನಂಬುವುದಿಲ್ಲ" ಎಂದು ಅವರು ಬರೆದಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | CNN munde sutta predator annu taliban meravanige maduvaga sambhavya karyakramavannu mukhtayagolisuva apayavannu mirisuttade endu nanu nambuvudilla endu avaru barediddare | False | I do not believe the possible recon value outweighs the risk of possible program termination when the stakes are raised by the Taliban parading a charred Predator in front of CNN, he wrote. | I don’t believe they wrote that the disaster that will free the program when the Taliban go on a march after the predator that ran before CNN. | 0.5480417 | False |
| xnli-test-3380 | hypothesis | ಸಿಎನ್ಎನ್ ವಾಹಿನಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಪ್ರಿಡೇಟರ್ನಿಂದ ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ ಪಲಾಯನ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿರುವುದನ್ನು ತೋರಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | CNN vahiniyalli predator ninda taliban palayana maduttiruvudannu torisalagide. | False | The Taliban was shown fleeing from the Predator on CNN. | CNN channel is showing the Taliban fleeing from the predator. | 0.9305656 | False |
| xnli-test-3787 | premise | "" "ದುರದೃಷ್ಟವಶಾತ್, ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಪ್ರಾಮುಖ್ಯತೆಯ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮ್ಮ ದೃಷ್ಟಿಕೋನವನ್ನು ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರು ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದಿಲ್ಲ." | duradrishtavashat, danadharmada pramukhyateya bagge namma drishtikonavannu ella amerikarru hanchikolluvudilla. | False | Unfortunately, our view of the importance of philanthropy is not shared by all Americans. | Unfortunately, not all Americans will embrace our perspective on the importance of charity. | 0.83320355 | False |
| xnli-test-3787 | hypothesis | ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರು ಚರ್ಚ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಬೆಂಬಲಿಸುವುದು ಮುಖ್ಯವೆಂದು ಭಾವಿಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | ella amerikannaru churchgalannu bembalisuvudhu mukhyavendu bhaavisuvudilla. | False | Not all Americans think it's important to support churches. | All Americans do not consider building churches to be important. | 0.59854496 | False |
| xnli-test-1796 | premise | ನಂತರ ಕೆಳಕಂಡಂತಿವೆಃ "" "ನೀವು ಅದನ್ನು ಆದೇಶಿಸಿದಿರೋ?" "" "ಅಪನಂಬಿಕೆಯ ಉಚ್ಚಾರಣೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಅವನು ಕೇಳಿದನು, ಆದರೆ ಲಾರ್ಡ್ ಜೂಲಿಯನ್ ಹುಬ್ಬೇರಿಸಿದನು." "" | nanthara kelakandanthive: "" "neevu adannu aadeshisidiro?" "" "apanambikey uchaaraneyalli avanu kelidanu, aadare lord julian hubberisidanu." "" | False | Then:   You ordered it?  he said in accents of unbelief, whilst Lord Julian raised his eyebrows. | Later I asked: “Did you order that?” He asked in disbelief, but Lord Julian laughed. | 0.7391218 | False |
| xnli-test-1796 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಮಾತನಾಡುವಾಗ ಅವರ ಉಚ್ಚಾರಣೆಯು ನಂಬಲು ಅಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಗಿತ್ತು. | avaru mathanaduvaga avara uccharaneyu nambalu asadhyavagittu. | False | His accents were unbelievable when he spoke. | It was impossible to believe their pronunciation. | 0.6236855 | False |
| xnli-test-2657 | premise | ಮೌಲ್ಯ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸುವ, ವ್ಯಾಪಾರ ಫಲಿತಾಂಶಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಗ್ರಾಹಕ ಕೇಂದ್ರಿತ ಪಾಲುದಾರರಾಗಲು | maulya srustisuva, vyapara phalitamangalalli grahaka kendrita paludararagalu | False | To be a Value Creating, Customer Focused Partner in Business Results | Value‑creating, customer‑focused initiatives in profit‑driven businesses | 0.6841837 | False |
| xnli-test-2657 | hypothesis | ಪಾಲುದಾರರು ಯಶಸ್ವಿಯಾಗಲು ಗ್ರಾಹಕರ ಮೇಲೆ ಕೇಂದ್ರೀಕರಿಸಬೇಕು. | paludararu yashasviagalu grahakara mele kendrisabekku. | False | Partners need to be focused on customers to be successful. | Successful people must focus on the customer. | 0.8082562 | False |
| xnli-test-4581 | premise | ಎಲ್ಲಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಮಿಗಿಲಾಗಿ, ನಾವು ಅಸಂತೋಷದ ಸತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದೇವೆ, ವಾಕ್ಚಾತುರ್ಯದ ಬರವಣಿಗೆಯು ಕೆಲವೊಮ್ಮೆ ಸ್ಮರಣೀಯವಾಗಿದೆ, ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಯನ್ನು ಜಟಿಲಗೊಳಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | Ellakkinta migilaagi, naavu asantoshaada satyavannu hondiddeve, vaakchaaturyada baravanigeyu kelvomme smaraniyavaagaitu, samasyeyannu jatilagolisuttade. | False | On top of all this, we have the unhappy fact that eloquent writing is indeed sometimes memorable, compounding the problem. | More than anything, we have uncovered the truth of discontent, the rain of the verse has become memorable, and the problem is getting tangled. | 0.32055742 | False |
| xnli-test-4581 | hypothesis | ಜನರು ಕಳಪೆ ಬರವಣಿಗೆಯನ್ನು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ನೆನಪಿಸಿಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತಾರೆ. | janaru kalape baravanigeyannu hecchu nenapisikolluttaare. | False | People are much more likely to remember poor writing. | People tend to remember the dream more. | 0.41147003 | False |
| xnli-test-4136 | premise | ಈ ಎಂಟು ಸರಳ ತಂತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿಕೊಂಡು, ನೀವು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸ್ವಂತ ಮನೆಯಲ್ಲೇ ಆರಾಮವಾಗಿ ಒಂದು ಸುದ್ದಿ ಕಥೆಯನ್ನು ರಚಿಸಬಹುದು. | ee entu sarala tantragalannu balasikondu, neevu nimmag swanta maneyalle aramavaagi ondu suddi katheyannu rachisabahudu. | False | Using these eight simple techniques, you can fabricate a news story in the comfort of your own home. | By using these simple tools, you can comfortably write a short story in your own home. | 0.48274055 | False |
| xnli-test-4136 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಕೆಲವು ಸರಳ ತಂತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು ಅನುಸರಿಸಿದರೆ ನೀವು ಯಾವುದೇ ಸ್ಥಳದಿಂದ ಸುದ್ದಿ ಕಥೆಯನ್ನು ಬರೆಯಬಹುದು. | neevu kelavu sarala tantragalannu anusarisidare neevu yaavude sthaladinda suddi katheyannu bareyabahudu. | False | You can write a news story from any location if you follow some simple techniques. | If you follow some simple steps, you can write a story from any place. | 0.7216295 | False |
| xnli-test-1490 | premise | ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯ ಧ್ವನಿಯು ಚುನಾವಣಾ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯ ಮೇಲೆ ರಾಜ್ಯಗಳ ನಿಯಂತ್ರಣಕ್ಕೆ, ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಕಚೇರಿಗೆ ಸಹ ಗೌರವಯುತವಾಗಿ ಉಳಿದಿದೆ. | tiddupadiya dhvaniyu chunavana prakriyeya mele rajygala niyantranakke, rashtriya kacherige saha gouravayutavagi ulidide. | False | The tone of the amendment remains deferential to the states' control of the electoral process, even for national office. | The drum’s sound has risen proudly for the states’ control over the selection process, along with national security. | 0.3406452 | False |
| xnli-test-1490 | hypothesis | ರಾಜ್ಯಗಳು ಚುನಾವಣಾ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯನ್ನು ನಿಯಂತ್ರಿಸುತ್ತವೆ. | rajyagalu chunavana prakriyeyanu niyantrisuttave. | False | The states rule the electoral process. | States control the selection process. | 0.6848881 | False |
| xnli-test-1279 | premise | "ದುಃಖದ ಹುಡುಗಿ" ಯಂತಹ ಹೆಸರುಗಳು | Duhkhada hudugi yanthaha hesarugalu | False | Names, such as  Sad Girl | The sad girl has many names | 0.6504857 | False |
| xnli-test-1279 | hypothesis | ಹೆಸರುಗಳು, ಸಂತೋಷದ ಹುಡುಗಿಯಂತೆ | hesarugalu, samtoshada hudugiyante | False | Names, like happy girl | Names, like a happy girl | 0.88776386 | False |
| xnli-test-1809 | premise | ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಬ್ಲಡ್ನ ಪಕ್ಕದಲ್ಲಿ ನಿಂತು, ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಸೂಚಿಸಿದ ನಂತರ, ಅವನು ತಿರುಗಿ ನೋಡುತ್ತಾನೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಆಶ್ಚರ್ಯದಿಂದ ಕೂಗುತ್ತಾನೆ. | captain bloodna pakkadalli nintu, captain kaiyannu soochisida nantara, avanu thirugi noduthaane, mattu aashcharyadinda kooguthaane. | False | Standing beside Captain Blood, he looked astern, following the indication of the Captain's hand, and cried out in his amazement. | Standing beside Captain Blood, after the captain pointed his hand, he turned to look and flinched in surprise. | 0.83448124 | False |
| xnli-test-1809 | hypothesis | ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಬ್ಲಡ್ ಆತ್ಮಸ್ಥೈರ್ಯವನ್ನು ಉತ್ತೇಜಿಸುವಲ್ಲಿ ಕುಖ್ಯಾತರಾಗಿದ್ದರು. | captain blood atmashairyavannu uttejisuvalli kukhyataragiddaru. | False | Captain Blood was notoriously good at promoting morale. | Captain Blood was famous for encouraging self‑pride. | 0.7770346 | False |
| xnli-test-447 | premise | ತನಗೇನು ಅಗತ್ಯವಿದೆಯೆಂದು ಅವನು ನನಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದನು ಮತ್ತು ಇಂದು ತನಗೇನು ಅಗತ್ಯವಿದೆಯೆಂದು ಅವನು ನನಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದನು. | tanaganu agatyavideyendu avanu nanage helidanu mattu indu tanaganu agatyavideyendu avanu nanage helidanu. | False | He told me what exactly he needed and that he did need it by today. | He told me that he is needed and then he told me again that he is needed. | 0.53017795 | False |
| xnli-test-447 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಅದನ್ನು ಯಾವಾಗ ಬೇಕಾದರೂ ಹಾಕಬಹುದು ಎಂದು ಅವರು ಹೇಳಿದರು. | Naanu adanu yavaga bekadaru hakabahudu endu avaru helidaru. | False | He said I could turn it in whenever. | I told him that he can put it whenever he wants. | 0.6128291 | False |
| xnli-test-256 | premise | ನೀವು ಶಾಲೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ದೊಡ್ಡವರಾಗಿದ್ದಾಗ, ನೀವು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುವ ಅಥವಾ ದ್ವೇಷಿಸುವ ಯಾವುದಾದರೂ ಪುಸ್ತಕಗಳಿವೆಯೇ? | neevu shaaleyalli doddavaragiddaga, neevu ishtapaduva athava dveshisuva yavudadaru pusthakagaliveye? | False | Um, are there any, you said you don't remember reading anything in particular, like when you were older in school, were there any books that you read that you liked or hated? | When you were older in school, are there any books you liked or disliked? | 0.7144165 | False |
| xnli-test-256 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಕೆಲವು ಪುಸ್ತಕಗಳನ್ನು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುತ್ತೀರಾ ಅಥವಾ ದ್ವೇಷಿಸುತ್ತೀರಾ? | neevu kelavu pustakagalannu ishtapaduttira athava dveshisuttira? | False | Did you like or hate certain books? | Do you like or dislike some books? | 0.9160671 | False |
| xnli-test-4813 | premise | ಸಶಸ್ತ್ರ ಪಡೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ವಿಭಾಗವೆಂದರೆ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣ ಬ್ಯಾಟಾಲಿಯನ್, ಇದನ್ನು ತ್ವರಿತವಾಗಿ CB ಎಂದು ಸಂಕ್ಷಿಪ್ತಗೊಳಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | sashastra padegala ondu vibhagavendare nirmaana battalion, idannu thritavaagi CB endu sankshiptagolisalayitu. | False | One segment of the armed forces is the Construction Batallion, which was quickly abbreviated to C.B. | A construction battalion is a unit of the armed forces, and it was later abbreviated as CB. | 0.74864846 | False |
| xnli-test-4813 | hypothesis | ನಿರ್ಮಾಣ ಬ್ಯಾಟಾಲಿಯನ್ ಸಶಸ್ತ್ರ ಪಡೆಗಳ ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಪ್ರಮುಖ ಶಾಖೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದಾಗಿದೆ. | nirmaana battalion sashastra padegala atyanta pramukha shakhegalalli ondagide. | False | The Construction Batallion is one of the most important branches of the armed forces. | The construction battalion is present in the most important branches of the armed forces. | 0.86852115 | False |
| xnli-test-4980 | premise | "" "ನನ್ನ ಕರೆಯನ್ನು ಸ್ವೀಕರಿಸಿದ್ದಕ್ಕೆ ಧನ್ಯವಾದಗಳು" "ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯುತ್ತಾರೆ." | nanna kareyannu sveekarisiddakke dhanyavaadagalu endu kareyuttaare. | False | Caller says,  Thank you for taking my call. | They say thank you for accepting my request. | 0.47461346 | False |
| xnli-test-4980 | hypothesis | ಕರೆಗೆ ಯಾರೂ ಸ್ಪಂದಿಸಲಿಲ್ಲ. | Karege yaaru spandaslilla. | False | No one answered the caller. | No one feels any sympathy for him. | 0.22350118 | False |
| xnli-test-1660 | premise | ಅಯ್ಯೋ-- ಸುಳ್ಳುಗಳ ಒಪ್ಪಂದ, ನಾನು ನಿಮಗೆ ಸಾಬೀತುಪಡಿಸಬಹುದಾದಂತೆ. | ayyo-- sullugala oppanda, nanu nimage sabithupadisabahudadante. | False | Aye--a deal of lies, devil a doubt, as I could prove to you. | Oh dear, stop the nonsense, I can teach you the syllabus. | 0.22008613 | False |
| xnli-test-1660 | hypothesis | ನಿಮ್ಮ ಅರಿವಿಲ್ಲದೆ ನಾನು ಸಂಗ್ರಹಿಸಿದ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪುರಾವೆಗಳಿಂದಾಗಿ ನಾನು ಇದನ್ನು ಸಾಬೀತುಪಡಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಯಿತು. | nimma arivillade naanu samgrahisida ella puravegalindagi naanu idanu saabithupadisalu sadhyavayitu. | False | I could prove this because of all the evidence I had collected without your knowledge. | Without your knowledge, I was able to prove that I am the one who collected all the evidence. | 0.85526854 | False |
| xnli-test-4358 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ ಬೀದಿಯಲ್ಲಿ (ಬಾಸ್ ಟ್ವೀಡ್ನ ಪ್ರೇತದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಡೊನಾಲ್ಡ್ ಟ್ರಂಪ್ ನಗ್ನ ಸ್ವಿಂಗ್ ನೃತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಹೊರತುಪಡಿಸಿ) ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಕಿರಿಕಿರಿಯನ್ನುಂಟುಮಾಡುವ ದೃಶ್ಯವೆಂದರೆ ಯಾರಾದರೂ ಸೆಲ್ಫೋನ್ ಬಳಸುತ್ತಿರುವುದು. | New York beediyalli (boss tweedna prethadondige Donald Trump nagna swing nruthyavannu horathupadisi) atyantha kirikiriyuntu maaduva drushyavendare yaaradaru cellphone balasuthiruvadhu. | False | The most irritating sight on a New York street (excluding Donald Trump's naked swing dancing with the ghost of Boss Tweed) is anyone jabbering into a cell phone. | In New York City (the boss made Donald Trump do a swing dance to impress the president), the scene is so wildly exciting that someone is holding a cellphone. | 0.67081547 | False |
| xnli-test-4358 | hypothesis | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ಜನರು ಆಪಲ್ ಫೋನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | New Yorknalli hechchina janaru apple phonegalannu hondiddare. | False | People mostly have Apple phones in New York. | Wealthy people in New York have Apple phones. | 0.8631375 | False |
| xnli-test-439 | premise | ಇಸವಿ 1962ರ ಕೊನೆಯಲ್ಲಿ, ಆಹ್, ವಾಷಿಂಗ್ಟನ್ ಡಿ. | isavi 1962ra koneyalli, aah, washingtan di. | False | Late 1962, uh, I got orders to go to Washington, D.C. | At the end of 1962, ah, I was washing. | 0.5327999 | False |
| xnli-test-439 | hypothesis | ಸೇನೆಯು ನನ್ನನ್ನು ತಕ್ಷಣವೇ DCಗೆ ಕಳುಹಿಸಿತು. | seneyu nannannu takshanave DCge kaluhisitu. | False | The army sent me to DC right away. | The army just sent me to DC. | 0.84064746 | False |
| xnli-test-3353 | premise | ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆಯ ವಿರುದ್ಧದ ಹೋರಾಟದಲ್ಲಿ ಈ ವ್ಯತ್ಯಾಸಗಳು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಕೃತಕವಾಗಿ ತೋರುತ್ತಿವೆ. | bhayotpaadaneya viruddhada horaatadalli ee vyatyasagalu hechchu krutakavaagi thoruttive. | False | In the struggle against terrorism these distinctions seem increasingly artificial. | In resisting oppression, these differences become more evident. | 0.51762104 | False |
| xnli-test-3353 | hypothesis | ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆ ವಿರುದ್ಧ ಹೋರಾಟವಿದೆ. | bayotpadane viruddha horatavide. | False | There is a struggle against terrorism. | It is a contrary behavior. | 0.11324946 | True |
| xnli-test-156 | premise | ನಾನು ಏನಾದರೂ ಮಾಡಬಹುದಿತ್ತು. | naanu enadaru madabahudittu. | False | If there was anything I could do. | I could have done something. | 0.515877 | False |
| xnli-test-1082 | premise | ಸೊಂಜಾ ತನ್ನ ಮಗಳ ಕಿರುಚಾಟವನ್ನು ಅನುಕರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಾರಂಭಿಸಿದಳು. | sonja thanna magala kiruchatavannu anukarisalu prarambhisidalu. | False | Sonja the  child  began to mimic her daughter's tantrums. | Sonja began to think about her own daughter's cuteness. | 0.6030333 | False |
| xnli-test-1082 | hypothesis | ಸೋನ್ಯಾ ಅಸಮಾಧಾನಗೊಂಡಿದ್ದಳು. | Sonya asamadhanagondiddalu. | False | Sonja was upset. | Sonya was dissatisfied. | 0.5823371 | False |
| xnli-test-4691 | premise | ಕೊನೆಯದಾಗಿ, ನಿರಂತರತೆಯು ಒಂದು ಸದ್ಗುಣವಾಗಿದೆ, ಅಥವಾ ಅದನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿಲ್ಲದವರು ಅದನ್ನು ತಮ್ಮ ಮೇಲೆ ಹೇರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | koneyadagi, nirantareyuyondu sadgunavagide, athava adanu hondilladavaru adanu thamma mele heruthare. | False | Continence, after all, is a virtue, or so say those who do not have it imposed on them. | Finally, it has become a continuous virtue, or those who are not good will bring it upon themselves. | 0.5808321 | False |
| xnli-test-4691 | hypothesis | ತಮ್ಮ ಮೇಲೆ ನಿರ್ಬಂಧವನ್ನು ಹೇರದಿರುವವರು ಅದನ್ನು ಉತ್ತಮ ಗುಣವೆಂದು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | thamma mele nirbandhavannu heradiruvavaru adannu uttama gunavendu pariginisuvaru. | False | Those who do not have continence imposed on them consider it a good quality. | Those who impose obligations on themselves regard it as a great virtue. | 0.49925244 | False |
| xnli-test-2521 | premise | ಈ ವಿಶ್ಲೇಷಣೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಬಳಸಲಾದ ಮೂಲ ಅಧ್ಯಯನಗಳ ನಮ್ಮ ಪರೀಕ್ಷೆಯು GAM ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಗಳಿಂದ ಸಂಭಾವ್ಯ ಪರಿಣಾಮ ಬೀರುವ ಆರೋಗ್ಯ ಅಂತ್ಯ ಬಿಂದುಗಳು ಬೇಸ್ ಮತ್ತು ಪರ್ಯಾಯ ಅಂದಾಜುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಆಸ್ಪತ್ರೆಯ ಪ್ರವೇಶವನ್ನು ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮಾಡಿವೆ ಎಂದು ಕಂಡುಹಿಡಿಯುತ್ತದೆ. | ee vishleshaneyalli badasalada mula adhyayanagala namma pariksheyu GAM samasyegalinda sambhavya parinama beuru aarogya anthya bindugalu base mattu paryaya andajugalalli aspatreya praveshavanu kammi madive endu kanduhidiyuttade. | False | Our examination of the original studies used in this analysis finds that the health endpoints that are potentially affected by the GAM issues  reduced hospital admissions in both the Base and Alternative Estimates | In this analysis, our review of the underlying studies indicates that because of GAM problems the likely outcome is a reduction in health endpoints, and based on the base and alternative estimates, hospital admissions will be lowered. | 0.8964219 | False |
| xnli-test-2521 | hypothesis | ಆರೋಗ್ಯ ಎಂಡ್ಪಾಯಿಂಟ್ಗಳು ಕೆಲವು ಆಸ್ಪತ್ರೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಜನರ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆಯನ್ನು ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮಾಡಿದವು. | arogya endpointgalu kelu aspatregalli janara sankhyeyanu kamdi madidavu. | False | Health endpoints reduced the number of people in some hospitals. | The health endpoints reduced the number of people in the hospital. | 0.92098975 | False |
| xnli-test-2143 | hypothesis | ನೂರಾರು ವರ್ಷಗಳಷ್ಟು ಹಳೆಯದಾದ ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳನ್ನು ನೀವು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುತ್ತೀರಿ. | ooraru varshagalashtu haleyadada chaha kettalgalannu neevu ishtapaduttiri. | False | You will like the tea kettles that are hundreds of years old. | You like very old tea kettles. | 0.77321047 | False |
| xnli-test-3971 | premise | ಒಂದು ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮದ ನಂತರ, ಒಂದು ಯುವ ದಂಪತಿಯು ಹಲೋ ಹೇಳಲು ವೇದಿಕೆಗೆ ಬಂದರು. | ondu karyakramada nantara, ondu yuva dampatiyu halo helalu vedikige bandaru. | False | Following a show, a young couple came up to the stage to say hello. | After a program, a young couple came onto the stage to say hello. | 0.91376 | False |
| xnli-test-3971 | hypothesis | 80 ವರ್ಷಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆ ವಯಸ್ಸಿನವರು ಯಾರೂ ಈ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮದಲ್ಲಿ ಭಾಗವಹಿಸಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ. | 80 varshakkinta kame vayassinavaru yaaru ee karyakramadalli bhagavahisiralilla. | False | No one at the show was under the age of 80. | Who are the people over 80 years old who have not taken part in this program. | 0.33476025 | False |
| xnli-test-456 | hypothesis | "ನನ್ನ ತಾಯಿ ಹತ್ತಿ ಮತ್ತು ಜೋಳವನ್ನು ಆರಿಸಬೇಕಾಗಿತ್ತು. | nanna tayi hatti mattu jolavannu arisbekagittu. | False | My mom had to pick cotton and corn. | I had to take care of my mother's farm and do the work. | 0.7462723 | False |
| xnli-test-3405 | premise | ಇದು ಎಂದಿಗೂ ಅಧಿಕೃತ ಅಂತರ್ ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿ ಚರ್ಚೆಯ ವಿಷಯವಾಗಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ. | idu endigu adhikruta antara ejensi charcheya vishayaviralilla. | False | It was never the subject of formal interagency deliberation. | This is never a matter for excessive internal agency discussion. | 0.61146283 | False |
| xnli-test-3405 | hypothesis | ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಗಳು ಪ್ರತಿ ವಾರ ಬೇರೆ ಬೇರೆ ಸ್ಥಳಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ತಮ್ಮ ಕೂಟಗಳನ್ನು ನಡೆಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದವು. | agencygalu prati vaara bere bere sthalagalalli tamma kootagalannu nadesuttidavu. | False | The agencies conducted their meetings in a different location every week. | The agencies conduct their groups at different places every week. | 0.836087 | False |
| xnli-test-3231 | premise | ಪ್ರಾಮಾಣಿಕತೆಯನ್ನು ಉತ್ತೇಜಿಸುವ ಮತ್ತು ಖಾಸಗಿತನವನ್ನು ಕಾಪಾಡುವ ಉದ್ದೇಶದಿಂದ, ನಾವು ಇಂಟರ್ವ್ಯೂ ಮಾಡಿದ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಗಳನ್ನು ಗುರುತಿಸದಿರಲು ನಾವು ಒಪ್ಪಿಕೊಂಡೆವು. | pramaanikateyannu uttējisuvura mattu khaasagitanavannu kaapaaduva uddeshandinda, naavu intervīu maaḍida hechina vyaktigaḷannu gurutisadiralu naavu oppikoṇḍevu. | True | In the interests of promoting candor and protecting privacy, we agreed not to identify most individuals we interviewed. | With the aim of encouraging authenticity and protecting privacy, we have recorded the prominent individuals we interviewed. | 0.55888546 | False |
| xnli-test-3231 | hypothesis | ಪ್ರತಿ ಸಂದರ್ಶನದ ವಿಷಯಕ್ಕೆ ಸಂಬಂಧಿಸಿದ ಸಂಪೂರ್ಣ ಜೀವನಚರಿತ್ರೆ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಕೋರಿಕೆಯ ಮೇರೆಗೆ ನೀಡಲಾಗುವುದು. | prati sandarshanada vishayakke sambandhisida sampoorna jeevanacharitre mahitiyannu korikey melege needalaguvudu. | False | Full biographical information for each interview subject will be made available on request. | Requesting the complete life‑history information related to each viewing topic is placed above. | 0.42583162 | False |
| xnli-test-1449 | premise | ವಾಸ್ತವದಲ್ಲಿ, ಈ ಹರಡುವಿಕೆಯು ಹಾನಿಕಾರಕ ನೇರಳೆ ಹಿಮಪಾತದ ಹರಡುವಿಕೆಯ ಮೊದಲ ಹೆಜ್ಜೆಯಾಗಿದೆ. | vastavadalli, ee haraduvikeyu haanikarakada nerale himapaatada haraduvikeya modala hejjeyaagide. | False | Indeed, this spreading is the first time step of the spreading of a purple avalanche of damage. | In the forest, this six‑year‑old is the first to be harmed by the poisonous ... | 0.2654404 | False |
| xnli-test-1449 | hypothesis | ಇದು ಬಂಡೆಗಳ ಹಾನಿಯ ಮೊದಲ ಭಾಗವಾಗಿದೆ. | idu bandegala haaniya modala bhaagavaagide. | False | This is the first part of damage to the rocks. | This is the first part of the series of episodes. | 0.29920635 | False |
| xnli-test-1440 | premise | ಹಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ನೈಸರ್ಗಿಕ ಆಯ್ಕೆಯು ಮಾತ್ರ ಅದನ್ನು ಹೀಗೆ ಸರಿಪಡಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿದೆ. | Haagiddare, naisargika aikyeyu maatra adanu heege saripadisalu saadhyavide. | False | If so, only natural selection can have tuned it thus. | If that’s the case, only natural harmony can fix it like this. | 0.3760883 | False |
| xnli-test-1440 | hypothesis | ಹಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ಅದು ದೇವರಿಂದ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟದ್ದು. | Hagidre, adu devarinda srustisalpatthaddu. | False | If so, then it was created that way by God. | If that's the case, it was not created by God. | 0.7750861 | False |
| xnli-test-2829 | premise | 13, 212 ವಸತಿ ಮಾರ್ಗಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಪ್ರತಿಯೊಂದಕ್ಕೂ ಪರಿಮಾಣ ಮತ್ತು ವಿತರಣೆಯ ಅಂಕಿಅಂಶಗಳ ಜೊತೆಗೆ, ಸಿಸಿಎಸ್ ಪ್ರತಿ ಮಾರ್ಗದಿಂದ ಸೇವೆ ಸಲ್ಲಿಸುವ ಸಂಬಂಧಿತ 5-ಅಂಕಿಯ ಜಿಪ್ ಕೋಡ್ ಅನ್ನು ಒದಗಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | 13, 212 vasathi margagalalli pratiyondakku parimana mattu vitaraneya ankialgala jothege, sics prati margadinda seve sallisuva sambandhita 5-ankiya zip code annu odagisuttade. | False | In addition to the volume and delivery statistics for each of the 13,212 residential routes, CCS provides the associated 5-Digit ZIP Code served by each route. | 13,212 residential streets each have measurement and distribution numbers, and the associated five-digit ZIP code is read from each road’s service. | 0.7420926 | False |
| xnli-test-2829 | hypothesis | ವಸತಿ ಮಾರ್ಗಗಳ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆಯ ಅಂಕಿಅಂಶಗಳನ್ನು ಸಿಸಿಎಸ್ ಒದಗಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ. | Vasati margagala samkhyeya amkiamshegalanu CCS odagisalu sadhyavilla. | False | CCS cannot provide statistics for the number of residential routes. | CCS cannot handle the number of residential roads. | 0.6453176 | False |
| xnli-test-1371 | premise | ಈ ಅವಧಿಯ ಒಂದು ದೊಡ್ಡ ಒಳಾಂಗಣವು 1928ರಲ್ಲಿ ಮೈಸ್ ವ್ಯಾನ್ ಡೆರ್ ರೋಹೆ ವಿನ್ಯಾಸಗೊಳಿಸಿದ ತುಗೆನ್ಡಾಟ್ ಮನೆಯ ಮುಖ್ಯ ವಾಸಸ್ಥಾನವಾಗಿದೆ. | ee avadhiya ondu dodda olanganavu 1928rali mais van der rohe vinyasagolisida tugendat maneya mukhya vasasthanavide. | False | One of the great interiors of this period is the main living space of the Tugendhat House, designed by Mies Van der Rohe in 1928. | This era’s major landmark was the main house designed by Van der Rohe, built in 1928. | 0.7642139 | False |
| xnli-test-1371 | hypothesis | ಮೈಸ್ ವ್ಯಾನ್ ಡೆರ್ ರೊಹೆ ತುಗೆನ್ಡಾಟ್ ಹೌಸ್ ಅನ್ನು ವಿನ್ಯಾಸಗೊಳಿಸಿದರು. | Mies van der Rohe Tugendhat house annu vinyasagolisidaru. | False | Mies Van der Rohe designed the Tugendhat House. | Mies van der Rohe designed the Tugendhat house. | 0.99999994 | False |

| xnli-test-2623 | premise | ಹಿಂಪಡೆಯಲು ಒಂದು ಔಪಚಾರಿಕ ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾವನೆ ಇಲ್ಲದೆ, ಫೆಡರಲ್ ನ್ಯಾಯಾಲಯದಲ್ಲಿ ದಾಖಲೆಯ ವಕೀಲರು, ನೈತಿಕವಾಗಿ ಮತ್ತು ನ್ಯಾಯಾಲಯದ ನಿಯಮಗಳ ಅಡಿಯಲ್ಲಿ, ಉದ್ಭವಿಸಬೇಕಾದ ಯಾವುದೇ ವಿಷಯಕ್ಕೆ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯಿಸಲು ಜವಾಬ್ದಾರರಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | himpedayalu ondu oupacharika prastavane illade, federal nyayalayadalli dakhaleya vakilaru, naitikavagi mattu nyayalayada niyamagala adiyalli, udbhavisabakada yavude vishayakke pratiyisalu javabdararagiruttare. | False | Absent a formal motion to withdraw, an attorney of record in federal court remains responsible, both ethically and under the rules of court, for responding to any matter that should arise. | For the underprivileged, without any administrative proposal, the intervening lawyers in the federal court, ethically and at the foundation of the court’s rules, are responsible for responding to any emerging issue. | 0.5470847 | False |

| xnli-test-4234 | premise | ಮುಖಪುಟದ ಕಥೆಯು ಕೊಸೊವೊವನ್ನು ಕೆತ್ತಲು ಅಥವಾ ಅದನ್ನು ರಕ್ಷಣಾಧಿಕಾರಿಯಾಗಿ ನಿರ್ವಹಿಸುವುದು ನ್ಯಾಟೋದ ಗುರಿಯಾಗಿರಬೇಕೆ ಎಂಬುದರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಚರ್ಚಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | mukhaputada katheyu kosovovannu kettalu athava adanu rakshanadhikariyagi nirvahisuvudu nyatoda guruyagirabeke embudara bagge charchisuttade. | False | The cover story debates whether NATO's goal should be to carve up Kosovo or administer it as a protectorate. | It discusses whether the story of the mask should be heard or carried out as a protective authority, and that it must be treated as a serious matter. | 0.30301344 | False |
