| xnli-test-2587 | premise | ಮಂಡಳಿಯ ಅಧಿಕಾರಿಯೊಬ್ಬರ ಪ್ರಕಾರ, ಮಂಡಳಿಯ ಸೆಕ್ಷನ್ 605 (ಬಿ) ಪ್ರಮಾಣಪತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು ಸಣ್ಣ ವ್ಯವಹಾರ ಆಡಳಿತ (ಎಸ್ಬಿಎ) ಮುಖ್ಯ ವಕೀಲರಿಗೆ ಪ್ರತ್ಯೇಕವಾಗಿ ನೀಡಲಾಗಿಲ್ಲ. | mandalia akarhiyobbara prakara, mandalia sekshan 605 (bi) pramanapatragalannu sanna vyavahara adalita (sba) mukhya vakilarige pratyekavagi nidalagilla. | False | According to a Board official, the Board's section 605(b) certifications were not provided separately to the Small Business Administration (SBA) Chief Counsel for Advocacy. | According to the mandal’s authority, mandal section 605(b) certificates are not specifically required for the chief officers of the Small Business Administration. | 0.6888145 | False | According to a council official, the council's Section 605(b) certificates have not been separately provided to the Small Business Administration (SBA) chief counsel. | 0.8597277 | 0.170913180683136 | False |
| xnli-test-2587 | hypothesis | ಬೋರ್ಡ್ ಎಸ್ಬಿಎ ಪ್ರಮಾಣಪತ್ರವನ್ನು ನೀಡಿಲ್ಲ. | Board SBI pramanapatravannu nidilla. | False | The Board didn't give the SBA certifications. | The board did not issue the SBI certificate. | 0.8045752 | False | The board did not issue the SBC certificate. | 0.8589573 | 0.05438209064941402 | False |
| xnli-test-458 | premise | ನನ್ನ, ನನ್ನ ತಾಯಿ ಅವರ ಅಚ್ಚುಮೆಚ್ಚಿನವುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದಾಗಲಿಲ್ಲ ಮತ್ತು, ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ಅವರು ಹೊಲಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡಲು ಕೆಳಗಿಳಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟರು, ಆದರೆ ಇತರ ಕೆಲವು ಮಕ್ಕಳು ಹೊಲಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ. | nanna, nanna taayi avara achchumechchinavugalalli ondagalilla mattu, aadaredu avaru holagalalli kelasa madalu kelagilisalpatru, aadare itara kelu makkalu holagalalli kelasa maduttirlilla. | False | My, my mother wound up not being one of his favorites early on and, ah, so she was relegated to working in the fields whereas some of the other kids didn't work in the fields. | My mother is not among the lazy ones; she is willing to work in the fields, whereas other children do not work in the fields. | 0.4295323 | False | My mother was not one of my favorites, so she was sent down to work in the fields, but some other children did not work in the fields. | 0.7130599 | 0.28352760219116213 | False |
| xnli-test-458 | hypothesis | ಅಮ್ಮ ಹೊರಗೆ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡಬೇಕಿತ್ತು. | Amma horage kelsa madabekittu. | False | My mother had to work outside. | Mother had to work outside. | 0.91665196 | False | Mom had to work outside. | 0.92089593 | 0.00424397410491939 | False |
| xnli-test-4411 | premise | ಏಕೀಕರಣವಾದಿ ನಂಬಿಕೆಯನ್ನು ಇಟ್ಟುಕೊಳ್ಳುವವರು ಪ್ರೊಫೆಸರ್ ಹೆನ್ರಿ ಲೂಯಿಸ್ ಗೇಟ್ಸ್ ಮತ್ತು ಕಾರ್ನೆಲ್ ವೆಸ್ಟ್ ಅವರಂತಹ ಕಪ್ಪು ಉದಾರವಾದಿಗಳು. ಅವರ ಜೊತೆ ಸೇರಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವವರು ಕಪ್ಪು ಅರ್ಥಶಾಸ್ತ್ರಜ್ಞ ಗ್ಲೆನ್ ಲೌರಿ, ಒಂದು ಅಗತ್ಯವಾದ ನೀತಿಯಾಗಿ ಸಕಾರಾತ್ಮಕ ಕ್ರಮವನ್ನು ಅನುಮೋದಿಸಲು ಶ್ರೇಣಿಯನ್ನು ಮುರಿಯುವ ಸಂಪ್ರದಾಯವಾದಿ. | eekikaranavadi nambikeyannu ittukolluvavaru professor henry lewis gates mattu cornell west avarantha kappu udaravadigalu. avar jothe serikolluvavaru kappu arthashastrajna glenn loury, ondu avashyakavada neetiyagi sakaratmaka kramavannu anumodisalu shreniyannu muriyuva sampradayavadi. | False | Keeping the integrationist faith are black liberals like Professors Henry Louis Gates and Cornel West. Joining them is black economist Glenn Loury, a conservative who broke ranks to endorse affirmative action as a necessary policy. | Those who uphold the belief in eekikaranavadi are Professor Henry Lewis Gates and Cornell West, along with the prominent scholars in that field. They are joined by the economist Glenn Loury, who, as a necessary policy, supports a positive approach by endorsing the tradition of cutting the class. | 0.50597966 | False | Those who hold an integrative belief are black liberals like Professor Henry Louis Gates and Cornel West. Joining them are black economist Glenn Loury, a traditionalist who breaks the mold to endorse positive action as a necessary policy. | 0.8948934 | 0.3889137478216552 | False |
| xnli-test-4411 | hypothesis | ಗ್ಲೆನ್ ಲೂರಿ ಬೀದಿ ಬದಿ ಶುಚಿಗೊಳಿಸುವ ಕೆಲಸದಲ್ಲಿ ತೊಡಗಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Glen louri beedi badi shuchigolisuva kelasadalli todagiddare. | False | Glenn Loury is a street sweeper. | Glen was found in the class that cleans the floor. | 0.49883384 | False | Glen Luri is engaged in street cleaning work. | 0.64128566 | 0.14245181788269046 | False |
| xnli-test-3132 | premise | ಈ ಕಾರ್ಮಿಕರು ಸಿಕ್ಕಿಬೀಳಲಿಲ್ಲ, ಆದರೆ ಮೇಲಿನ ಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿದ್ದ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ನಿವಾಸಿಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಭಿನ್ನವಾಗಿ, ಅವರು ಪರಿಣಾಮ ಬೀರಿದ ತಕ್ಷಣ ಕೆಳಗೆ ಇಳಿಯದಿರಲು ನಿರ್ಧರಿಸಿದ್ದರು. | ee karmikaru sikkibeelalilla, aadare melina mahadigalallidda hechina nivasigaliginta bhinnavai, avaru parinama beerida takshana kelage iliyadiralu nirdharisiddaru. | False | These workers were not trapped, yet unlike most occupants on the upper floors, they had chosen not to descend immediately after impact. | These workers are not available, but unlike the senior officials’ large households, they have decided that there will be no immediate results. | 0.36023208 | False | These workers were not trapped, but unlike most residents on the upper floors, they had decided not to go down immediately after the impact. | 0.9727559 | 0.6125238289660644 | False |
| xnli-test-3132 | hypothesis | ಮೇಲಿನ ಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿನ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ನಿವಾಸಿಗಳು ತಕ್ಷಣವೇ ಕೆಳಗೆ ಇಳಿದರು. | melina mahadigalalli ella nivasigalu takshanave kelage ilidaru. | False | All occupants on the upper floors immediately descended upon impact. | All the residents on the upper floors were moved down immediately. | 0.8477423 | False | All the residents on the upper floors immediately went down. | 0.8948595 | 0.04711719277877813 | False |
| xnli-test-3031 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ನಡೆದ ಸಿಐಎ-ಎಫ್ಬಿಐ ಸಭೆಯ ಎರಡು ದಿನಗಳ ನಂತರ ಮಿಹ್ಧರ್ ಹೊಸ ಯುಎಸ್ ವೀಸಾ ಪಡೆದರು. | New Yorknalli nadeda CIA-FBI sabheya eradu dinagala natara Mihdhar hosa US visa padedaru. | False | Mihdhar received a new U.S. visa two days after the CIA-FBI meeting in New York. | Mihdhar, a CIA-FBI member who was in New York, got a new US visa after two days. | 0.8975452 | False | Two days after the CIA-FBI meeting held in New York, Mihdar obtained a new US visa. | 0.93766034 | 0.04011513649444576 | False |
| xnli-test-3031 | hypothesis | "" "ಮಿಹಧರ್ ಎಂದಿಗೂ ವೀಸಾ ಪಡೆದಿಲ್ಲ, ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ಅವರು ಎಂದಿಗೂ ನಮ್ಮ ಬಳಿಗೆ ಬರಲಿಲ್ಲ." | Mihadhar endigu visa padedilla, aadre avaru endigu namma balige baralilla. | False | Mihdhar never got a visa, so he never came to the us. | Mihadhar never got a visa, but he never comes to our house. | 0.74457246 | False | Mihadhar has never obtained a visa, so he has never come to us. | 0.8848425 | 0.14027005499176026 | False |
| xnli-test-736 | premise | ಕೆಲವು ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳ ಹಿಂದೆ ಅವರು ಆರು ಸದಸ್ಯರ ಜೂರಿಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದರು, ಅವರು ಯಾವಾಗಲೂ ಹನ್ನೆರಡು ಪುರುಷರು ಎಂದು ನಿಮಗೆ ತಿಳಿದಿದೆ ಎಂದು ನಾನು ಭಾವಿಸಿದ್ದೆ-ಹನ್ನೆರಡು ಪುರುಷರು ಪ್ರಯತ್ನಿಸಿದರು ಮತ್ತು ಮಾತನಾಡಿದರು | kelavu thingalugala hinde avaru aaru sadasyara jurigalannu hondiddaru, avaru yavagalu hanneladu purusharu endu nimage tilidide endu nanu bhavisidde-hanneladu purusharu prayatnisidaru mattu matanadaru | False | a few months ago that they had six member juries i thought that they you know it was always twelve twelve men tried and true so to speak | Behind some matters they had the support of four members; they have always been the Hanneladu men, as you know, I thought— the Hanneladu men tried and spoke. | 0.4066801 | False | A few months ago they had a jury of six members, and I thought you knew that they were always twelve men—twelve men tried and spoke. | 0.8531262 | 0.4464461278556824 | False |
| xnli-test-736 | hypothesis | ತೀರ್ಪುಗಾರರು ಯಾವಾಗಲೂ 13 ಜನರಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Teerpugarru yavagaloo 13 janariruttare. | False | The jury is always 13 people. | There are always 13 police officers. | 0.72017086 | False | There are always 13 judges. | 0.8201854 | 0.1000145628973389 | False |
| xnli-test-4010 | premise | ಓಮ್ನಿಯಾ ವಿನ್ಸಿಟ್ ಅಮೋರ್ (ನೀವು ವೀಕ್ಲಿ ಸ್ಟ್ಯಾಂಡರ್ಡ್ಗಾಗಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡದಿದ್ದರೆ): ಬ್ರಿಟ್ ಹ್ಯೂಮ್ (ಫಾಕ್ಸ್ ನ್ಯೂಸ್ ಭಾನುವಾರ) ಲೆವಿನ್ಸ್ಕಿ ಏಕೆ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ ಎಂಬುದರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಊಹೆ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾಳೆ-ಅವಳು ಇನ್ನೂ ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷರ ಮೇಲೆ ಆಶಾಭಂಗವಿಲ್ಲದ ಕ್ರಶ್ ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಾಳೆ. | Omnia vincit amor (neevu weekly standardge kelsa maadadiddare): Brit Hume (Fox News Sunday) Levinsky eke saadhyavilla embudarbage uuhe maaduttaale-avalu innoo adhyakshara mele aashaabhangavillada crush hondiddhaale. | False | Omnia vincit amor (unless you work for the Weekly Standard): Brit Hume ( Fox News Sunday ) speculates on why Lewinsky might not  She still has a hopeless crush on the president. | Love conquers all (if you didn’t work for the weekly standard): Brit Hume (Fox News Sunday) says Levinsky is impossible—she’s got a hopeless crush on the president. | 0.72635686 | False | Omnia Vinset Amor (if you don’t work for the weekly standard): Britt Hume (Fox News Sunday) speculates about why Levinsky can’t—she still has an undismayed crush on the president. | 0.88013446 | 0.1537776033102417 | False |
| xnli-test-4010 | hypothesis | ಬ್ರಿಟ್ ಹ್ಯೂಮ್ ಫಾಕ್ಸ್ಗಾಗಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Brit Hume foxge kelsa madtare. | False | Brit Hume works for Fox. | Brit Hume works at Fox. | 0.9909232 | False | Brit Hume works for Fox. | 1.0000001 | 0.009076919209289547 | False |
| xnli-test-1359 | premise | ತನ್ನ ಸ್ವಂತ ಸಾಧನಗಳಿಗೆ ಎಡ, ಈ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯು ಎಕ್ಸರ್ಗೋನಿಕ್ ಆಗಿರುತ್ತದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಮತೋಲನ ಅನುಪಾತಕ್ಕೆ ಹೋಲಿಸಿದರೆ ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಮತೋಲನಕ್ಕೆ ಹೋಲಿಸಿದರೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚುವರಿ ಟ್ರಿಮರ್ಗಳ ಉಪಸ್ಥಿತಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಕ್ಸಾಮರ್ನ ಸಂಶ್ಲೇಷಣೆಯ ಮೂಲಕ ಸಮತೋಲನದತ್ತ ಪ್ರಯಾಸಕರವಾಗಿ ಹರಿಯುತ್ತದೆ. | tanna svanta sadhanagalige eda, ee pratikriyeyu eksargonik agiruttatte mattu hexamarna samatolana anupatakke holisidare hexamarna samatolanakke holisidare heccuvari trimmergala upasthitiyalli hexamarna sanshleshaneya moolaka samatolanadatta prayasakaravagi hariyuttade. | False | Left to its own devices, this reaction is exergonic and, in the presence of excess trimers compared to the equilibrium ratio of hexamer to trimers, will flow exergonically toward equilibrium by synthesizing the hexamer. | For its own resources, this reaction is exergonic, and when the hydrogen‑balance ratio is increased, the presence of higher‑capacity trimmers reduces the effort needed to achieve balance through hydrogen exchange. | 0.7061137 | False | This response will be exergonic for its own devices, and compared to the hexamer's equilibrium ratio, the presence of additional trimers drives the synthesis toward equilibrium through the hexamer. | 0.8710704 | 0.1649566849792481 | False |
| xnli-test-1359 | hypothesis | ಈ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯು ಸ್ಪರ್ಶಿಸಲ್ಪಡದಿದ್ದರೆ, ಅದು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ ಸಮತೋಲನದತ್ತ ಸಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | ee pratikreyeyu sparshisalppadiddare, adu antimavaagi samatoolanadatta saaguttade. | False | If this reaction is untouched, it will eventually go towards equilibrium. | If this reaction is touched, it will eventually become balanced. | 0.76144993 | False | If this response is not touched, it will eventually move toward equilibrium. | 0.83812547 | 0.07667553730041499 | False |
| xnli-test-4713 | premise | ನಾನು ಪರಿಶೀಲಿಸಿದ ನಿಘಂಟುಗಳು ಮೌನವಾಗಿವೆ-ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ನಾನು ಭಾವಿಸುತ್ತೇನೆ-ಈ ಇಂದ್ರಿಯಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ. | naanu parishilisida nighantugalu mounavaagive-aadare, naanu bhaavisuttene-ee indriyagala mele. | False | The dictionaries I have checked are silent--improperly so, I think--on these senses. | The dictionaries I have examined are silent, but I will contemplate these senses. | 0.81847644 | False | The dictionaries I consulted are silent, so I think, about these senses. | 0.8774298 | 0.05895334334426883 | False |
| xnli-test-4713 | hypothesis | ನಿಘಂಟುಗಳು ಈ ಭಾವನೆಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಮಾತನಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | Nighantugalu ee bhavanegalu bagge hecchu mathaduvudilla. | False | Dictionaries do not talk about these senses very much. | Dictionaries don't say much about these buildings. | 0.38306832 | False | Dictionaries don't talk much about these emotions. | 0.47049007 | 0.08742174819725035 | False |
| xnli-test-4204 | premise | ಪ್ರೂಡೆನ್ಸ್, ನಮ್ಮ ಸಲಹೆ ಅಂಕಣಕಾರ, ನಿವೃತ್ತರಾಗಿದ್ದಾರೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳ ಅಂಕಣವನ್ನು ಅವಳ ಸೋದರ ಸೊಸೆ, ಪ್ರೂಡೆನ್ಸ್ ಎಂದು ಸಹ ಹೆಸರಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | prudens, namma salahe ankankara, nivruttaragiddare, mattu avala ankanavannu avala sodara sose, prudens endu saha hesarisalagide. | False | Prudence, our advice columnist, has retired, and her column has been taken over by her niece, also named Prudence. | Prudens, our advice is to stay calm, and his brother also calls him Prudens. | 0.3557729 | False | Providence, our advisory accountant, has retired, and her ledger is also named Providence after her niece. | 0.5603524 | 0.2045794850440979 | False |
| xnli-test-4204 | hypothesis | ಮುಂದಿನ ಹತ್ತು ವರ್ಷಗಳ ವರೆಗೆ ಬುದ್ಧಿವಂತಿಕೆಯೇ ನಮ್ಮ ಸಲಹೆಗಾರರಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | mundina hattu varshagala varege buddhivantikeyee namma salahegararagiruttare. | False | Prudence will still be our advice columnist for the next ten years. | Our advisors will be wise for the next ten years. | 0.6260736 | False | Intelligence will be our advisor for the next ten years. | 0.4526666 | -0.17340698952064515 | False |
| xnli-test-2969 | premise | ಸಂಭವಿಸಬಹುದಾದ ಅಡಚಣೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ಉದಾಹರಣೆಯನ್ನು ಜನಪ್ರಿಯ ವೆಬ್ಸೈಟ್ಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ಇತ್ತೀಚಿನ ಸೇವಾ ನಿರಾಕರಣೆಯ ದಾಳಿಗಳು ಒದಗಿಸುತ್ತವೆ. | sambhavisabahudada adachanehala ondu udaharaneyanu janapriya websitegala mele itechina seva nirakaneya daligalu odagisuttave. | False | An example of disruptions that could occur is provided by recent denialofservice attacks on popular websites. | An example of a possible ad campaign is being reviewed by service evaluation teams on popular websites. | 0.24605393 | False | Recent denial-of-service attacks on popular websites provide an example of possible disruptions. | 0.8689649 | 0.6229109805072022 | False |
| xnli-test-2969 | hypothesis | ಇತ್ತೀಚಿನ ದಿನಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಯಾವುದೇ ಸೇವಾ ನಿರಾಕರಣೆಯ ದಾಳಿ ನಡೆದಿಲ್ಲ. | itticina dinagalalli yavude seva nirakaraneya dali nadedilla. | False | There has not been a denial of service attack recently. | In these recent days, I have not taken part in any service work. | 0.23819071 | False | In recent days, there has been no denial-of-service attack. | 0.9523638 | 0.7141730790815735 | False |
| xnli-test-2678 | premise | ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ವಿಮೆಯು ಪಿಂಚಣಿ ಮತ್ತು ಇತರ ನಿವೃತ್ತಿ ಯೋಜನೆಗಳಂತಹ ಒಕ್ಕೂಟ ನೌಕರರಿಗಾಗಿ ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಲಾದ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳನ್ನು ಒಳಗೊಳ್ಳುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | samajika vimeyu pinchaani mattu itara nivrutti yojanegalantha okkuta noukararigagi sthapisalada karyakramagalannu okkolluvudilla. | False | Social insurance does not include programs established solely or primarily for Federal employees, such as pension and other retirement plans. | It does not include programs established for a single employee, such as social welfare, pension, and other retirement schemes. | 0.8147558 | False | Social security does not include programs established for union employees such as pensions and other retirement plans. | 0.83854115 | 0.023785350093078583 | False |
| xnli-test-2678 | hypothesis | ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ವಿಮೆಯು ಕೇವಲ ಫೆಡರಲ್ ಕೆಲಸಗಾರರಿಗಾಗಿ ಇರುವ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳನ್ನು ಮಾತ್ರ ಒಳಗೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | samajika vimeyu kevala federal kelasagararigagi iruva karyakramagalannu matra olagolluttade. | False | Social insurance only includes programs that are just for federal workers. | Social welfare only includes programs that are for federal employees. | 0.90416116 | False | Social insurance only includes programs for federal workers. | 0.9530599 | 0.048898751727905254 | False |
| xnli-test-2491 | premise | ನೀವು ರೂಲೆಟ್ ಅಥವಾ ಕ್ರ್ಯಾಪ್ಸ್ ಟೇಬಲ್ಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ರೋಲರ್ಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ಆಡಲು ಅಥವಾ ಸ್ಲಾಟ್ ಯಂತ್ರಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಕೆಲವು ನಾಣ್ಯಗಳನ್ನು ಹಾಕಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | neevu rulet athava kraps tebalgalalli hechina rolergalondige aadalu athava slot yantragalalli kelavu naanyagalannu haakalu saadhyavaaguttade. | False | You'll be able to play alongside the  high rollers  at the roulette or craps tables or put a few coins in the slot machines. | You can play high rollers at roulette or craps tables, or place some bets on slot machines. | 0.81480956 | False | You can play with high rollers at roulette or craps tables, or insert some coins into slot machines. | 0.8073945 | -0.007415055452880903 | False |
| xnli-test-2491 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ವೇಗಾಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಜೂಜಾಟವನ್ನು ಆಡಬಹುದು. | neevu vegasnalli jujatavannu aadabahudu. | False | You can gamble in Vegas. | You can play the game in Vegas. | 0.7957082 | False | You can gamble in Vegas. | 1.0 | 0.20429180000000002 | False |
| xnli-test-3859 | premise | ನಗರದ ಇತರ ಲಾಭೋದ್ದೇಶವಿಲ್ಲದ ಚಿತ್ರಮಂದಿರಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಭಿನ್ನವಾಗಿ, ನಮ್ಮ ನಟರು ತಮ್ಮ ಕಲೆಯಿಂದ ತಮ್ಮ ಜೀವನವನ್ನು ನಡೆಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | nagarada ithara labhoddeshavillada chitramandiragaliginta bhinnavagi, namma nataru tamma kaleyinda tamma jeevanavannu nadesuttare. | False | Unlike other not-for-profit theatres in the city, our actors make their living from their craft. | Unlike other unprofitable cinemas in the city, our actors live their lives through their own art. | 0.7267424 | False | Unlike other non‑profit theaters in the city, our actors make a living from their art. | 0.96685183 | 0.2401094304824829 | False |
| xnli-test-3859 | hypothesis | ನಗರದಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಲಾಭರಹಿತ ರಂಗಮಂದಿರಗಳಿವೆ. | nagaradalli ondಕ್ಕಿಂತ hecchu labharahitha rangamandiragalive. | True | There is more than one not-for-profit theater in the city. | There are more than one profitable colorful temples in the city. | 0.38195553 | False | There is more than one non-profit theater in the city. | 0.97808987 | 0.5961343390223695 | False |
| xnli-test-3456 | premise | ಅನೇಕ PAPD ಅಧಿಕಾರಿಗಳು ಸಂಕೀರ್ಣದ ನೆಲಮಹಡಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿದ್ದರು-ಕೆಲವರು ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವಲ್ಲಿ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು, ಇತರರು 5 WTCಯಲ್ಲಿ PAPD ಡೆಸ್ಕ್ ಅನ್ನು ನಿರ್ವಹಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು ಅಥವಾ ಲಾಬಿ ಕಮಾಂಡ್ ಪೋಸ್ಟ್ಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು. | aneka PAPD akarigalu sankirnad nelamahadigalliddaru-kelavaru sthalantarisuvalli sahay maduttiddaru, itararu 5 WTeyalli PAPD desk annu nirvahisuttiddaru athava labi command postgalalli sahay maduttiddaru. | False | Many PAPD officers were on the ground floors of the complex-some assisting in evacuation, others manning the PAPD desk in 5 WTC or assisting at lobby command posts. | Several PAPD officers were posted at remote outposts—some assisted in border areas, while others operated a PAPD desk in the five WT locations or provided help at the Labi command posts. | 0.71196675 | False | Many PAPD officers were on the complex's ground floors—some were helping with the relocation, others were managing the PAPD desk at 5 WTC or assisting at the lobby command posts. | 0.95925146 | 0.24728471341323854 | False |
| xnli-test-3456 | hypothesis | 5 WTCಯಲ್ಲಿ PAPD ಎಲ್ಲಿಯೂ ಕಂಡುಬಂದಿಲ್ಲ. | 5 WTCyalli PAPD elliyoo kandubandilla. | False | The PAPD was nowhere to be found at 5 WTC. | PAPD was not found anywhere in the 5 WTC. | 0.9070977 | False | PAPD was not found anywhere in 5 WTC. | 0.90854007 | 0.0014423700569152764 | False |
| xnli-test-3553 | premise | ದಯವಿಟ್ಟು ನಮ್ಮ ರದ್ದಾದ ದಾನಿಗಳ ಪಟ್ಟಿಗೆ ಸ್ಲೈಡ್ ಮಾಡಬೇಡಿ. | Dayavittu namma raddada danigala pattige slide madabedi. | False | Please do not slide on to our lapsed donor list. | Please do not slide the page of our donors. | 0.6787009 | False | Please do not slide to our cancelled donors list. | 0.81076115 | 0.13206025369796748 | False |
| xnli-test-3553 | hypothesis | ನಮ್ಮ ಕಳೆದು ಹೋದ ದಾನಿಗಳ ಪಟ್ಟಿಯನ್ನು ಜಗತ್ತಿಗೆ ತೋರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಕಟಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ, ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ನನ್ನನ್ನು ನಂಬಿ, ನೀವು ಹೀಗೆ ನಾಚಿಕೆಪಡಲು ಬಯಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ! | namma kaledu hoda danigala pattiyannu jagattige torisalu prakatalagide, aadre nannu nambi, nevu hige nachikepadalu bayasuvudilla! | False | Our lapsed donor list is published for the world to see, so trust me, you don't want to be shamed like that! | Our bold film’s story has been shown to the world, but trust me, you shouldn’t be forced to like it this way. | 0.13834983 | True | Our list of lost donors has been published to show the world, so trust me, you don’t want to be embarrassed like this. | 0.7745687 | 0.6362188469485474 | False |
| xnli-test-1402 | premise | ವಾಂತಿ, ಅತಿಸಾರ, ತೂಕ ಕಳೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದು, ಮತ್ತು ಕೆಲವೊಮ್ಮೆ ಮರಣವೂ ಮಾಲ್ ಜೋಜೊ ಲಕ್ಷಣಗಳಾಗಿವೆ. | Vanti, atisara, thooka kaledukolluvudu, mattu kelavomme maranavoo maal jojoo lakshanagalaagive. | False | The symptoms of mal ojo are vomiting, diarrhea, loss of weight, and sometimes even death. | Vanti, extremely, taking a long time, and some also have deadly symptoms. | 0.39441773 | False | Vomiting, diarrhea, weight loss, and sometimes death are also symptoms. | 0.6760341 | 0.2816163629031372 | False |
| xnli-test-1402 | hypothesis | ಮಾಲೆ ಓಜೊ ಅನೇಕ ಋಣಾತ್ಮಕ ಲಕ್ಷಣಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದೆ. | maale ojo aneka runatmaka lakshanagalannu hondide. | False | Mal ojo has many negative symptoms. | Tomorrow Ojo has many emotional symptoms. | 0.61725754 | False | Malle Ojo has many negative traits. | 0.60245657 | -0.014800970328369134 | False |
| xnli-test-461 | premise | ಮತ್ತು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ, ನಿಮಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತು, ಅವರು ಸುತ್ತಮುತ್ತಲಿನ ಜನರನ್ನು ಕೇಳುತ್ತಲೇ ಇದ್ದರು, ಮತ್ತು ಅವರು ಎಲ್ಲಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂದು ಯಾರಿಗೂ ತಿಳಿದಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ, ಮತ್ತು ಅಂತಿಮವಾಗಿ, ನಿಮಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತು, ಅವರು ತಮ್ಮನ್ನು ತಾವೇ ಹೊಂದಿಕೊಂಡರು ಜೋವನ್ನು ಮತ್ತೆ ನೋಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲವೆಂದು. | mattu anthimavagi, nige gottu, avaru suttamuttalina janarannu keluttale iddaru, mattu avaru ellidare endu yarigu tilidiralilla, mattu anthimavagi, nige gottu, avaru tammannu tave hondikondru jovannu matte noduvudillavendu. | False | And so you know, eventually, you know, you know, they kept asking people around, and nobody knew where they were, and eventually, you know, they just reconciled themselves that they just wouldn't see Joe again. | And finally, you know, they are listening to the people who spread rumors, and no one knows where they are; and finally, you know, they have taken the copper and will not look at the youth. | 0.39449492 | False | And finally, you know, they kept asking the people around them, and no one knew where they were, and in the end, you know, they decided they would never see Jo again. | 0.7921896 | 0.39769467808349607 | False |
| xnli-test-461 | hypothesis | ಒಂದು ದಿನ ಜೋ ಈಗಷ್ಟೇ ಅಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಹೋಗಿದ್ದನು ಮತ್ತು ಅವನು ಎಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಹೋದನು ಎಂಬುದು ಯಾರಿಗೂ ತಿಳಿಯಲಿಲ್ಲ. | ondu dina joe igaste allige hogiddanu mattu avanu ellige hodanu embುದು yarigu tiliyali lla. | True | One day Joe was just gone and nobody could figure out where he went. | One day Joe went somewhere and then went somewhere else, but no one knows. | 0.8618643 | False | One day Joe had just gone there and no one knew where he had gone. | 0.8830829 | 0.02121862627334592 | False |
| xnli-test-2704 | premise | ತುರ್ತು ವಿಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿನ ಮಧ್ಯಸ್ಥಿಕೆಗಳು ಪರಿಣಾಮಕಾರಿಯಾಗಿವೆ ಮತ್ತು ಚಿಕಿತ್ಸೆಯ ಉಲ್ಲೇಖಗಳು ಕಾರ್ಯನಿರ್ವಹಿಸಬಲ್ಲವು ಎಂದು ಪುರಾವೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ಬೆಳೆಯುತ್ತಿರುವ ಗುಂಪು ತೋರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | turtu vibhagadalli madhyastikeyalu parinamakarivagive mattu chikitseya ullekhagalu karyanirvisaballavu endu puravegala ondu beleyuttiruva gumpu torisuttade. | False | A growing body of evidence demonstrates that interventions in the emergency department are effective and that treatment referral can work. | In the turtu department, mediators are effective, treatment references can be implemented, and it shows a group that values speed. | 0.45017004 | False | A growing body of evidence shows that interventions in the emergency department are effective and that treatment references can work. | 0.95924854 | 0.5090785027856446 | False |
| xnli-test-2704 | hypothesis | ತುರ್ತು ವಿಭಾಗದಿಂದ ಬರುವ ಉಲ್ಲೇಖಗಳು ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡಬಹುದು. | turtu vibhagadinda baruva ullekhagalu kelasa madabahudu. | False | Referrals from the emergency department can work. | Records coming from the turtu department can be processed. | 0.21824683 | True | References from the emergency department may work. | 0.8335047 | 0.6152578468188477 | False |
| xnli-test-2937 | premise | ಸಂಶೋಧನೆಯ ಈ ಕ್ಷೇತ್ರವನ್ನು ಎತ್ತಿ ತೋರಿಸುವ ಅವರ ಕಲ್ಪನೆಯು ಶ್ರೇಷ್ಠ ಅರ್ಹತೆಯನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದರೂ, ಕಾರ್ಯಾಚರಣೆಯು ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಯಾಗಿದೆ. | samshodhaneya ee kshetravannu etti thorisuva avara kalpaneyu sreshta arhataiyannu hondiddaru, kaaryacharaneyu samasveyagide. | False | While his idea of highlighting this area of research holds great merit, operationalization is problematic. | Their conception of presenting this field of research is highly deserving, but the methodology is problematic. | 0.6700279 | False | Even though their idea of elevating this field of research has excellent merit, the implementation is problematic. | 0.7664685 | 0.09644062493286132 | False |
| xnli-test-2937 | hypothesis | ಜಾಗವನ್ನು ಸುಪ್ರಕಾಶಗೊಳಿಸುವುದರಿಂದ ಯಾವುದೆ ಮೌಲ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ. | jagavannu suprakashagolisuvudarinda yavude maulyavilla. | False | Highlighting the area has no value. | Because the world is illuminated by the supreme light, it has no value. | 0.36900914 | False | There is no value in brightening the space. | 0.5414332 | 0.17242407514129637 | False |
| xnli-test-116 | premise | ಮತ್ತು, ಆ ಸಮಯದಲ್ಲಿ ನನ್ನ ಕೆಲಸಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದು, ಅಣುಬಾಂಬ್ ಸ್ವತಃ ಸ್ಫೋಟಿಸುವ ಪರಮಾಣು ಶಸ್ತ್ರಾಸ್ತ್ರಗಳ ಟ್ರಿಗರ್ ಮೇಲೆ ಪ್ಯಾರಾಶೂಟ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಹೇಗೆ ಹಾಕಬೇಕೆಂದು ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಗಳಿಗೆ ತರಬೇತಿ ನೀಡುವುದಾಗಿತ್ತು. | mattu, aa samayadalli nanna kelasagalalli ondu, anubomb svata sphotisuva paramanu shastragala trigger mele parachootgalannu hege hakabekendu vyaktigalige tarabheti niduvudagittu. | False | And, uh, one of my jobs at the time was training individuals how to put parachutes on a nuclear weapons trigger to, uh, which detonates the atomic bomb itself. | Also, at that time in my work there was a guide on how to attach parachutes to the trigger of a self‑detonating nuclear weapon for people. | 0.80640507 | False | And at that time one of my jobs was to train people on how to attach parachutes to the triggers of nuclear weapons that cause the atomic bomb to explode on its own. | 0.9314282 | 0.12502312404602056 | False |
| xnli-test-116 | hypothesis | ಪರಮಾಣು ಬಾಂಬ್ ಟ್ರಿಗರ್ ಎಳೆಯಲು ನಿಮಗೆ ಸ್ವಲ್ಪ ಬಲದ ಅಗತ್ಯವಿದೆ. | paramanu bomb trigger eleyalu nige swalpa balada agatyavide. | False | You only need a tiny bit of force to pull the atomic bomb trigger. | There is no nuclear bomb trigger; it is somewhat dangerous. | 0.6381408 | False | You need a little strength to pull the nuclear bomb trigger. | 0.7542299 | 0.11608910322113042 | False |
| xnli-test-555 | premise | ಹೌದು, ನೀವು ಬಳಸುವಂತಹ ತುಪ್ಪಳವನ್ನು ಪಡೆಯುವಂತೆ ಅವರು ಸಲಹೆ ನೀಡಿದರು | Houdu, neevu balasuvantha tuppalavannu ಪಡೆಯುವಂತೆ avaru salahe needidaru | True | yeah he suggested just getting uh um a uh um a mop like you would use | Listen, they gave you advice to get a child-sized spoon. | 0.27740937 | False | Yes, they advised you to get the kind of ghee you use. | 0.26254517 | -0.014864201600189197 | False |
| xnli-test-555 | hypothesis | ಕೊಂಡುಕೊಳ್ಳುವಂತೆ ಸಲಹೆ ನೀಡಿದರು. | kondukolluvante salahe needidaru. | False | He suggested buying a vacuum. | They gave advice as if they were in charge. | 0.21338883 | True | They advised to purchase. | 0.35852385 | 0.14513501567260742 | True |
| xnli-test-4833 | premise | ಅಂಥ ಒಂದು ಸಂಯೋಜನೆಯು, ತಂತಿಯ ಭಾಗವು ಇದ್ದಕ್ಕಿದ್ದಂತೆ ಬೆಂಕಿಗೆ ಆಹುತಿಯಾಯಿತು ಎಂಬ ಅನಿಸಿಕೆಯನ್ನು ನಿಸ್ಸಂದೇಹವಾಗಿಯೂ ಕೊಡುತ್ತದೆ. | antha ondu samyojaneyu, tantiya bhagavu iddakkiddante benkige ahutiyayitu emba anisikeneyu nissandehavagiyu koduttade. | False | Such a composition would no doubt leave the impression that the string section had suddenly caught fire. | It is a kind of combination, as if the god of speech were present, the bench has been offered as a sacrifice, an unquestionable offering. | 0.17410803 | True | Such a combination undoubtedly gives the impression that the wire part spontaneously caught fire. | 0.6190371 | 0.4449290617320252 | False |
| xnli-test-4833 | hypothesis | ಸಂಯೋಜನೆಯ ವಿಸ್ತೃತ ಸಂಶೋಧನೆಯು ಬೆಂಕಿ ತಂತಿಯ ವಿಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿ ಪ್ರಾರಂಭವಾಗಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ಸಾಬೀತುಪಡಿಸಿತು. | samyojaneya vistruta samshodhaneyu benki tantiya vibhagadalli prarambhagabeku endu sabitupadisitu. | False | Extensive research of the composition proved that the fire had to begin in the string section. | It has been established that the extensive research on the combination must start in the bank’s department. | 0.400096 | False | The extensive research of the combination proved that it should begin in the fire wire section. | 0.67736375 | 0.2772677533187866 | False |
| xnli-test-4747 | premise | ಕೆಲವೊಮ್ಮೆ ನೀವು ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಇಂಗ್ಲಿಷ್ ಮಾತನಾಡುವ ಜನರು ಆಶ್ರಯಕ್ಕೆ ಬದ್ಧರಾಗಿರಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ನಂಬಬೇಕಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | kelavomme neevu ella english maathanaaduva janaru aashrayakke baddharagirabeku endu nambabeekagutade. | False | Sometimes you have to believe that all English speakers should be committed to an asylum. | Some people believe that you, who speak only English, should be dependent on shelter. | 0.70024073 | False | Sometimes you have to believe that all English-speaking people must be committed to shelter. | 0.7910129 | 0.09077215318634035 | False |
| xnli-test-4747 | hypothesis | ಇಂಗ್ಲಿಷ್ ಭಾಷೆ ಬಹಳ ಸಮಂಜಸವಾದ ಭಾಷೆ. | English bashe bahala samanjasawada bashe. | False | English is a very logical and consistent language. | English is a very harmonious language. | 0.7006129 | False | English language is a very reasonable language. | 0.7704335 | 0.0698205855079651 | False |
| xnli-test-4004 | premise | ನೀವು ಎಷ್ಟು ಕೊಡಬಲ್ಲಿರಿ ಎಂಬುದನ್ನು ಪ್ರಾರ್ಥನಾಪೂರ್ವಕವಾಗಿ ಪರಿಗಣಿಸಿರಿ. | neevu eshtu kodaballiri embudannu prarthanapoorvakavaagi pariganisiri. | False | Please prayerfully consider how much you can give. | You should consider how much you can give, prayerfully. | 0.9149053 | False | Consider, with prayer, how much you can give. | 0.89454067 | -0.02036463246612552 | False |
| xnli-test-4004 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಎಷ್ಟು ಕೊಡಬಲ್ಲಿರಿ ಎಂಬುದನ್ನು ನೀವು ಜಾಗರೂಕತೆಯಿಂದ ಪರಿಗಣಿಸುವಿರಿ ಎಂದು ನಾವು ನಿರೀಕ್ಷಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ. | neevu eshtu kodaballiri embudannu neevu jagarookateyinda pariganisuwiri endu naavu nireekshisutteve. | False | We hope that you will carefully consider how much you can give. | We are watching whether you will carefully consider how much you can give. | 0.8665881 | False | We expect that you will carefully consider how much you can give. | 0.93366 | 0.06707187076034549 | False |
| xnli-test-3642 | premise | ಜೆರಾಲ್ಡ್ ಎಲ್. ಬೆಪ್ಕೊ 1995 ಯುನೈಟೆಡ್ ವೇ ಆಫ್ ಸೆಂಟ್ರಲ್ ಇಂಡಿಯಾನಾ ಪ್ರಚಾರ ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷ | Gerald L. Bepko 1995 United Way of Central Indiana prachara adhyaksha | False | Gerald L. Bepko 1995 United Way of Central Indiana Campaign Chair | Gerald L. Bepko, 1995 United Way of Central Indiana publicity head | 0.8937232 | False | Gerald L. Bepko 1995 United Way of Central Indiana campaign president | 0.96865785 | 0.0749346512191772 | False |
| xnli-test-3642 | hypothesis | ಜನಪ್ರಿಯ ಮತದಿಂದ ಬೆಪ್ಕೊ ಆಯ್ಕೆಯಾದರು. | janapriya matadinda bepko aykeyadaru. | False | Bepko was elected by the popular vote. | Because Janapriya talked, it turned into a problem. | 0.17103158 | True | They were selected for Bepco by popular vote. | 0.66716015 | 0.4961285733889771 | False |
| xnli-test-3210 | premise | ಈ ಸರಣಿಯು ಆಫ್ಘಾನಿಸ್ತಾನದಲ್ಲಿನ ಅಲ್ ಖೈದಾ ಗುರಿಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾಪಿಸಲಾದ ವಿವಿಧ ರೀತಿಯ ದಾಳಿಗಳನ್ನು ನಡೆಸುವ ಆಪರೇಷನ್ ಇನ್ಫಿನೈಟ್ ರೆಸಲ್ಯೂಶನ್ ಅನ್ನು ಸಹ ಒಳಗೊಂಡಿತ್ತು. | ee saraniyu afghanistanadalli al kaida gurigala mele prastapisalada vidha rityada thaligalannu nadesuva operation infinite resolution annu saha olagondithu. | False | The series also included Operation Infinite Resolve, a variety of proposed follow-on strikes on al Qaeda targets in Afghanistan. | This schedule presented on the rules in Afghanistan also includes the operation called infinite resolution. | 0.663035 | False | This series also included Operation Infinite Resolution, which carried out various types of attacks proposed against al-Qaeda targets in Afghanistan. | 0.92181003 | 0.2587750309371948 | False |
| xnli-test-3210 | hypothesis | ಈ ಯೋಜನೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದು, ಏಳು ವಿಭಿನ್ನ ಅನುಮಾನಾಸ್ಪದ ಸುರಕ್ಷಿತ ಮನೆಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ಬಾಂಬ್ ದಾಳಿ ನಡೆಸುವುದಾಗಿತ್ತು. | ee yojanegalalli ondu, elu vibhinna anumanaspada surakshita manegala mele bomb dahi nadesuvudagittu. | False | One of these plans involved bombing seven different suspected safe houses. | In this plan, a bomb was dropped on several different supposedly secure houses. | 0.7870009 | False | One of these plans was to carry out a bomb attack on seven different suspected safe houses. | 0.9381984 | 0.15119748762283325 | False |
| xnli-test-1099 | premise | ಪಚೂಕಾ 1940 ರ ದಶಕದ ಪಚೂಕೋದ ಪ್ರತಿರೂಪವಾಗಿತ್ತು, ಆದರೆ ನಗರ ಕೊಳೆಗೇರಿ ಪರಿಸರದಲ್ಲಿ ಬೆಳೆಯುತ್ತಿರುವ ಚಿಕಾನಾದ ಯುವತಿಯರಲ್ಲಿ ಒಟ್ಟಿಗೆ ಬರುವ ಮನೆಯ ಹುಡುಗಿಯರ ಮಾದರಿಯೂ ಆಗಿತ್ತು. | pachuka 1940 ra dashakada pachukoda pratiroopavaittu, aadare nagara kolegeri parisaradallo beleyuttiruva chikanada yuvatiyarellu ottige baruva maneya hudugiyara maadariyoo aagittu. | False | The pachuca was the counterpart of the pachuco of the 1940s but also the home-girl archetype that comes together in the young Chicana growing up in an urban ghettoized environment. | Pachuka was a replica of the 1940s era, but the Karnataka girl living in the city college environment turned out to be the mother of a boy who came straight to the house. | 0.60859793 | False | Pachuka was a representation of the 1940s era, but it also served as a model for the girls from homes who gathered among the young women of Chikanna growing up in the city's deteriorating environment. | 0.7316768 | 0.12307888694030766 | False |
| xnli-test-1099 | hypothesis | ಪಚುಕಗಳು ಯುವ ಚಿಕನರಾಗಿದ್ದರು. | pachukagalu yuva chikanaragiddaru. | False | Pachucas were young Chicanas. | The children were very mischievous. | 0.2556725 | False | The chicks were young chickens. | 0.32001927 | 0.06434677495002744 | False |
| xnli-test-4664 | premise | (ಎ) ಗುರಿಯಲ್ಲಿನ ಪ್ರತಿ d ಅಥವಾ t ಅನ್ನು c ಗೆ ಬದಲಾಯಿಸುವುದು. | (e) guriyilina prati d athava t annu c ge badalayisuvudu. | False | (a) Change each d or t in the target to c. | Changing every d or t in the guru to c. | 0.5577936 | False | Replacing each d or t in the target with c. | 0.89043915 | 0.33264555271759033 | False |
| xnli-test-4664 | hypothesis | ಡಿ ಗಿಂತ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಸಿ ಗುರಿಯಲ್ಲಿರಬೇಕು. | D ginta hecchu C guriyallirbeku. | False | There should be more c's in the target than d's. | C should not be taught more than D. | 0.45757467 | False | C should be higher than D. | 0.5824765 | 0.12490182669647215 | False |
| xnli-test-399 | premise | ನಾನು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾದಷ್ಟು ಬೇಗ ತಿಂದೆ, ಮತ್ತು ನಂತರ ಅವಳು ಅಲ್ಲಿಗೆ ಬಂದಳು ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳು ನನಗೆ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡಿದಳು. | naanu sadhyavaadashtu bega thinde, mattu nanthara avalu allige bandalu mattu avalu nanage sahaaya maadidalu. | False | I ate real quick, as fast as I could and then she came in there and she ended up helping me with it. | I ate as quickly as possible, and then she came there and helped me. | 0.8973674 | False | I ate as soon as I could, and then she came there and helped me. | 0.91350365 | 0.0161362468505859 | False |
| xnli-test-399 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಎರಡು ನಿಮಿಷಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆ ಅವಧಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಪಿಜ್ಜಾ ತಿಂದೆ. | Naanu eradu nimishagaliginta kadime avadhiyalli pizza tinde. | False | I ate the pizza in less than two minutes. | I ate pizza in less than two minutes. | 0.94690675 | False | I ate pizza in less than two minutes. | 0.94690675 | -4.5661926462159386e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-4799 | premise | ಇಲ್ಲಿ ಆಯಿಲ್ ಕ್ರೀಕ್ ಉದ್ದಕ್ಕೂ, ಭಾರತೀಯರು ದೇಶೀಯ ಬಳಕೆಗಾಗಿ ನೀರಿನಿಂದ ಮೇಲ್ಮೈ ತೈಲವನ್ನು ಸ್ಕಿಮ್ಡ್ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಬಿಳಿ ವಸಾಹತುಗಾರರು ಅದನ್ನು ಔಷಧೀಯ ಉದ್ದೇಶಗಳಿಗಾಗಿ ಬಾಟಲಿಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ತುಂಬಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅದನ್ನು ಸೆನೆಕಾ ತೈಲ ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯುತ್ತಾರೆ. | illi oil creek uddakku, bharatiyaru deshiya balakegagi neerinda melmai tailavannu skimd maduttare, mattu bili vasahatugararu adannu aushadhiya uddeshegaligagi botaliyalli tumbisuttare mattu adannu seneca taila endu kareyuttare. | False | Here along Oil Creek, Indians skimmed the surface oil off the water for domestic uses, and white settlers bottled it for medicinal purposes and called it Seneca Oil. | Here at Oil Creek, the locals skim oil from the water for the nation's strength, while the white people crush it in a pot for medicinal purposes and call it Seneca oil. | 0.8611037 | False | Here along the entire Gulf of Aqaba, Indians skim surface oil from the water for domestic use, and white colonists fill it into bottles for medicinal purposes, calling it Seneca oil. | 0.9003216 | 0.03921790282135007 | False |
| xnli-test-4799 | hypothesis | ಬಿಳಿಯರಿಗೆ ಈ ಎಣ್ಣೆಯು ಒಂದು ಪರಿಣಾಮಕಾರಿ ಔಷಧವಾಗಿತ್ತು. | biliyarige ee enneyu ondu parinamakari aushadhavagittu. | False | The oil was an effective medicine for the white settlers. | For the white person, this has become a contraceptive medicine. | 0.41771814 | False | For the whites, this oil was an effective medicine. | 0.86927927 | 0.45156112540374754 | False |
| xnli-test-1666 | premise | ನೀವು ಅಪಾಯವನ್ನು ನೋಡಬೇಕಿತ್ತು. | neevu apayavannu nodabekittu. | False | You should have seen the danger. | You had to see the danger. | 0.854689 | False | You were supposed to see the danger. | 0.8606279 | 0.005938889633178679 | False |
| xnli-test-1666 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಗಮನ ಹರಿಸಬೇಕು. | neevu hecchu gamana harisabeku. | False | You need to pay more attention. | You should pay more attention. | 0.91869175 | False | You must pay more attention. | 0.9375125 | 0.018820766975402803 | False |
| xnli-test-1554 | premise | "" "ಅಯ್ಯೋ!" ಮತ್ತು ಅದು ಯಾವ ಮಾರ್ಗವಾಗಿರಬಹುದು? | ayyo! mattu adu yava margavagirabahudu? | False | Ah! And what way may that be? | Oh! And which way could that be? | 0.7346854 | False | Oh! And what could be the way? | 0.76970875 | 0.03502335263214107 | False |
| xnli-test-1554 | hypothesis | ಯಾರೋ ಯಾವ ದಾರಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಹೋಗಬೇಕೆಂದು ಕೇಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Yaaro yaava daariyalli hogabekendu keluttiddare. | False | Someone is asking which way to go. | Someone is asking which road to take. | 0.8587963 | False | Someone is asking which way to go. | 1.0 | 0.14120370000000004 | False |
| xnli-test-3475 | premise | ಅವರು ತಮ್ಮ ಹೋಟೆಲ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಉಳಿದ ತಂಡದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಸೇರಿಕೊಂಡರು. | avaru tamma hotelnalli ulida tandadondige serikondaru. | False | He joined the rest of his team at their hotel. | They met the person who stayed in their hotel. | 0.60663605 | False | They joined the rest of the team at their hotel. | 0.93204856 | 0.3254125091888428 | False |
| xnli-test-3475 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಹೋಟೆಲ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಕಾಣಬಾರದೆಂದು ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರು, ಆದರೆ ಬದಲಿಗೆ ಪೋಸ್ಟ್ ಆಫೀಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಬೀದಿಯ ಉದ್ದಕ್ಕೂ ಅವರನ್ನು ಭೇಟಿಯಾಗಲು ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾಪಿಸಿದರು. | avaru hotellnalli kaanabaaradendu thlididdaru, aadare badalige post officenalli beediya uddakkoo avarannu bhetiyaagalu prasthaapisidaru. | False | He knew not to be seen at the hotel, but instead offered to meet them across the street at the post office. | They knew they would not be seen in the hotel, but instead sent them to the post office for a meeting. | 0.75606036 | False | They knew they shouldn't be seen at the hotel, but instead suggested meeting them along the street at the post office. | 0.8344343 | 0.07837391085876466 | False |
| xnli-test-2041 | premise | ಮತ್ತು ತಮ್ಮ ಸುಂದರವಾದ ಕರಾವಳಿ ಕೇವಲ ತಮ್ಮದಲ್ಲ ಎಂಬುದು ಅವರಿಗೆ ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರೂ, ಅವರು ಸಹಿಷ್ಣುತೆಯುಳ್ಳವರೂ ಸಹಾಯಕರರೂ ಆಗಿ ಉಳಿಯುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Mattu thamma sundaravada karavali kevala thammadalla embudu avarige thiliddaru, avaru sahishnuteyullavaru sahayakararu agi uliyuthare. | False | And they remain tolerant and helpful, even though they know that their lovely coast is no longer just theirs. | And they knew that the beautiful coastline did not belong only to them; they are patient people who serve as helpers. | 0.7149409 | False | And even though they know that their beautiful coastline belongs only to them, they remain tolerant and helpful. | 0.8854388 | 0.17049789985809327 | False |
| xnli-test-2041 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಈಗ ಕರಾವಳಿಯನ್ನು ಇತರರೊಂದಿಗೆ ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳಬೇಕಾಗಿದೆ. | avaru iga karavaliyannu itararondige hanchikollabekaagide. | False | They have to share the coast with other people now. | They now have to share the Karavali with others. | 0.48287916 | False | They now have to share the coast with others. | 0.9632964 | 0.4804172534216309 | False |
| xnli-test-3250 | premise | ಮೇ 1996ರ ಆರಂಭದಲ್ಲಿ, ಬಿನ್ ಲಾಡಿನ್ ಸುಡಾನ್ ಬಿಟ್ಟು ಹೋಗುತ್ತಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂಬ ಗುಪ್ತಚರ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಸಿಐಎ ಪಡೆದುಕೊಂಡಿತು. | May 1996ra aarambhadalli, bin ladin sudan bittu hoguttiddare enba guptachara mahitiyannu cia padekoltitu. | False | In early May 1996, the CIA received intelligence that Bin Ladin might be leaving Sudan. | In early May 1996, the CIA obtained secret intelligence that Bin Laden had left Sudan. | 0.89075166 | False | In early May 1996, the CIA obtained intelligence that Bin Laden was leaving Sudan. | 0.90544987 | 0.014698207248535211 | False |
| xnli-test-3250 | hypothesis | ಬಿನ್ ಲಾಡಿನ್ ಮೇ ತಿಂಗಳಲ್ಲೇ ಸುಡಾನ್ ಬಿಟ್ಟು ಹೋಗುತ್ತಾರೆ ಎಂದು ಸಿಐಎ ನಂಬಿತ್ತು. | Bin ladin me tingalalle sudan bittu hoguttare endu cia nambittu. | False | The CIA believed that Bin Ladin would leave Sudan as early as May. | They said they would leave after finishing the house, and I believed them. | 0.19675149 | True | The CIA believed that Bin Laden would leave Sudan in May. | 0.88478565 | 0.6880341621606445 | False |
| xnli-test-2142 | premise | ನೀವು ಚಹಾ ಸಮಾರಂಭದಲ್ಲಿ ಪರಿಣತರಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ, ನೀವು 14 ನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ಪಿಂಗಾಣಿ ಚಹಾ ಬಟ್ಟಲುಗಳು, ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಕ್ಯಾಡಿಗಳ ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಸಂಗ್ರಹವನ್ನು ಪ್ರಶಂಸಿಸುತ್ತೀರಿ. | neevu chaha samaramabhadalli parinataragiddare, neevu 14 ne shatamana pingani chaha battulugalu, chaha ketalgalu mattu caddygalu atyuttama sangrahavanu prashamsisuttiri. | False | If you've become an adept of the tea ceremony, you'll appreciate the excellent collection of 14th-century  ceramic tea bowls, tea kettles, and caddies, as well as bamboo spoons, whisks, and flower vases. | If you took part in the tea war, you have a 14 % collection of tea leaves, tea buds and caddies, praising the finest collection. | 0.62562853 | False | If you are an expert at tea ceremonies, you will appreciate the finest collection of 14th‑century porcelain tea cups, tea kettles, and caddies. | 0.90187454 | 0.27624601223632816 | False |
| xnli-test-2142 | hypothesis | ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳು ವಿರೂಪವಾಗಿವೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅವುಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ತಿಳಿದುಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದು ಬೇಸರದ ಸಂಗತಿ. | chaha kettlegalu viroopagive mattu avugala bagge tilidukolluvudu besarada sangati. | False | The tea kettles are ugly and it's so boring to learn about them. | Those who drink tea are lazy, and learning about them is a serious problem. | 0.58894134 | False | Tea kettles are unattractive and learning about them is a boring matter. | 0.9069749 | 0.3180335716897583 | False |
| xnli-test-1887 | premise | ಬೆದರಿಕೆಗಳು ಸೇವೆ ಮಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ, ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್. | bedarikegalu seve maaduvudilla, captain. | False | Threats will not serve, Captain. | Complaints are not being served, captain. | 0.5189656 | False | Threats don't serve, Captain. | 0.9428472 | 0.42388159228744504 | False |
| xnli-test-1887 | hypothesis | "ಬೆದರಿಕೆಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿರುವುದು ಸ್ವೀಕಾರಾರ್ಹವಲ್ಲ," ಎಂದು ಒಬ್ಬರು ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದರು. | bedarikelannu hondiruvudu sveekararhavalla, endu obbaru captainge helidaru. | False | It was unacceptable to have threats, said one to the Captain. | Someone told the captain that the complaints are not acceptable. | 0.7315361 | False | Having threats is unacceptable, someone said to the captain. | 0.96241194 | 0.23087584009780882 | False |
| xnli-test-611 | premise | ನಾವು ನಿಜವಾದ ನಿಸರ್ಗವಾದಿಗಳಲ್ಲ ಅಥವಾ ಏನೂ ಅಲ್ಲ | Navu nijavada nisargavadigalla athava enu alla | False | we're not real naturalists or anything but um | We are not of true nature or anything else | 0.6232983 | False | We are not true naturals, or anything. | 0.7196189 | 0.09632061651153567 | False |
| xnli-test-611 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಒಂದು ದಿನ ನಿಸರ್ಗಶಾಸ್ತ್ರಜ್ಞರಾಗಲು ಪ್ರಯತ್ನಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ ಆದರೆ ಈಗ ನಾವು ನಿಸರ್ಗಶಾಸ್ತ್ರಜ್ಞರಲ್ಲ. | navu ondu dina nisargashastrajnyaragalu prayatnisuttve adre iga navu nisargashastrajnyaralla. | False | We would like to someday strive to be naturalists but right now we are not. | We try to be natural scientists for a day, but now we are not natural scientists. | 0.66803765 | False | We will try to become naturalists one day, but for now we are not naturalists. | 0.8848072 | 0.21676957904205318 | False |
| xnli-test-4334 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ನಲ್ಲೂ ಈ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಹೇಳಲಾಗದು. ಕೊಕೇನ್ ವಿವಾದದ ಕುರಿತಾದ ತನ್ನ ಸಂಪಾದಕೀಯದಲ್ಲಿ, ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ ಬುಶ್ಗೆ ಪ್ರಾಮಾಣಿಕವಾಗಿರಲು ಸಲಹೆ ನೀಡಿತು, ಮತ್ತು ದೇಶವು ತನ್ನ ಕ್ರಮವನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳಲು ಅವಕಾಶ ಮಾಡಿಕೊಟ್ಟಿತು. | New York Times nallu ee bagge helalagadu. Cocaine vivadada kuritada tanna sampadakiyalli, Times Bushge pramanikavagiru salhe neditu, mattu desavu tanna kramavannu tegedukollalu avakasa madikottitu. | False | The same can't be said for the New York Times. In its editorial on the cocaine controversy, the Times advised Bush to be honest, and to let the country take his measure. | The New York Times has not said anything about this. In its editorial criticizing the cocaine controversy, the Times advised being credible to Bush and gave the country a chance to determine its own course. | 0.79967654 | False | Even the New York Times cannot speak about this. In its editorial on the cocaine controversy, the Times advised Bush to be honest, and allowed the country to take action. | 0.8601814 | 0.06050485123916627 | False |
| xnli-test-4334 | hypothesis | ಬುಷ್ ಶಾಶ್ವತವಾಗಿ ಸುಳ್ಳು ಹೇಳಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ಟೈಮ್ಸ್ ಹೇಳಿದೆ. | bush shashvatavagi sullu helabeku endu times helide. | False | The Times said Bush should just lie to everone. | They kept saying we must keep lying forever. | 0.46178344 | False | The Times said Bush must lie forever. | 0.7845722 | 0.32278874408584596 | False |
| xnli-test-4209 | premise | ಅವರು ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳನ್ನು ಗುಂಡಿಕ್ಕಿ ಕೊಲ್ಲುತ್ತಾರೆ, ಅಲ್ಲವೇ? | avaru vidyarthigalannu gundikki kolluthare, allave? | False | They Shoot Students, Don't They? | They are cheating the students, right? | 0.5641039 | False | They shoot and kill the students, right? | 0.83194184 | 0.2678379430328369 | False |
| xnli-test-4209 | hypothesis | ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳ ಮೇಲೆ ದಾಳಿ ನಡೆಸಲು ಅವರ ಬಳಿ ಶಸ್ತ್ರಾಸ್ತ್ರಗಳಿವೆ. | vidyarthigala mele dali nadesalu avara bali shastrastragaluve. | False | They have weapons they use against students. | We will place their sacrificial weapons on the students. | 0.69506514 | False | They have weapons to carry out an attack on the students. | 0.91132534 | 0.21626019550262454 | False |
| xnli-test-1028 | premise | [ಈ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರವನ್ನು] ಸ್ವಾತಂತ್ರ್ಯದಲ್ಲಿ ರೂಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು ಮತ್ತು ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪುರುಷರು ಸಮಾನವಾಗಿ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟಿದ್ದಾರೆ ಎಂಬ ವಾದಕ್ಕೆ ಸಮರ್ಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | [ee rashtravannu] svatantryadalli roopisalayitu mattu ella purusharu samanavagi srustisalpattaare emba vaadakke samarpisalayitu. | False | [This nation was] conceived in liberty and dedicated to the proposition that all men are created equal. | This country was painted with freedom and dedicated to the claim that all men were created equal. | 0.72738695 | False | This nation was founded in freedom and dedicated to the proposition that all men are created equal. | 0.8949717 | 0.16758477832489016 | False |
| xnli-test-1028 | hypothesis | ಕೆಲವು ಜನರು ಇತರರಿಗಿಂತ ಅಂತರ್ಗತವಾಗಿ ಉತ್ತಮರು ಎಂಬ ನಂಬಿಕೆಯ ಮೇಲೆ ಈ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರವನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | kelavu janaru itharagintha antharghathavaagi uththamaru emba nambikeya mele ee rashtravannu sthaapisalaayithu. | False | This nation was founded on the belief that some people are inherently better than others. | Some people, believing themselves to be superior, established this country. | 0.59438276 | False | This nation was founded on the belief that some people are inherently superior to others. | 0.9316198 | 0.3372370629789734 | False |
| xnli-test-2112 | premise | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯು ಬೆಚ್ಚಗಿನ (ಆದರೆ ಬಿಸಿ ಅಲ್ಲ) ಹವಾಮಾನ ಮತ್ತು ಬೆಚ್ಚಗಿನ ಸಮುದ್ರ ತಾಪಮಾನವನ್ನು ತರುತ್ತದೆ, ಇದು ಡೈವಿಂಗ್, ಸ್ನಾರ್ಕ್ಲಿಂಗ್ ಮತ್ತು ಇತರ ಜಲ ಕ್ರೀಡೆಗಳಿಗೆ ಸೂಕ್ತವಾಗಿದೆ. | besigeyu bechchagina (aadare bisi alla) havaamaana mattu bechchagina samudra taapamaanavannu taruttade, idu daiving, snaarkling mattu itara jala kreetegalige sooktavaagide. | False | Summer brings warm (but not hot) weather and warm sea temperatures, making it ideal for diving, snorkeling, and other water sports. | The breeze is cool (not hot) and brings a sea temperature, making it suitable for diving, snorkeling and other water activities. | 0.6595632 | False | Summer brings warm (but not hot) weather and warm sea temperatures, which are suitable for diving, snorkeling, and other water sports. | 0.98663306 | 0.3270698623626709 | False |
| xnli-test-2112 | hypothesis | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಇಲ್ಲಿ ತಾಪಮಾನ 100 ಡಿಗ್ರಿ ಸೆಲ್ಸಿಯಸ್ ಇರುತ್ತದೆ. | besigeyalli illi tapamana 100 degree celsius iruttade. | False | It is always 100 degrees or hotter in the summer. | The temperature here in the kitchen is 100 degrees Celsius. | 0.5216363 | False | In summer, the temperature here reaches 100 degrees Celsius. | 0.7570981 | 0.23546177872772212 | False |
| xnli-test-2927 | premise | ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಎಂಜಿನಿಯರಿಂಗ್ ಮತ್ತು ಒಪ್ಪಂದದ ಮಾತುಕತೆಗೆ ಆರರಿಂದ ಎಂಟು ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು ಬೇಕಾಗಿದ್ದರೂ, ಎರಡು 900 ಮೆಗಾವ್ಯಾಟ್ ಘಟಕಗಳನ್ನು ಪೂರ್ಣಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಒಟ್ಟು 17ರಿಂದ 19 ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು ಬೇಕಾಗುತ್ತವೆ. | arambhika engineering mattu oppandada maatukatege aarinda entu tingalugalu bekagiddaru, eradu 900 megawatt ghatakagalannu purnagolisalu ottu 17rinda 19 tingalugalu bekaguttave. | False | Even if preliminary engineering and contract negotiation took as long as six to eight months, the total time for completing two 900 MWe units would be about 17 to 19 months. | The initial engineering and the required discussions needed some items, and to complete two 900‑megawatt units 17 to 19 items are required. | 0.527838 | False | Although the initial engineering and contract negotiations require six to eight months, completing the two 900-megawatt units will take a total of 17 to 19 months. | 0.9317403 | 0.4039022839660644 | False |
| xnli-test-2927 | hypothesis | ಒಟ್ಟು ಸಮಯವು 6 ತಿಂಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆಯಿದೆ. | ottu samayavu 6 thingaliginta kadimeyide. | False | The total time is less than 6 months. | The total time is less than six minutes. | 0.47676688 | False | The total time is less than six months. | 0.99243516 | 0.5156682772990417 | False |
| xnli-test-1602 | premise | ನಾನು ಈ ವರ್ಷದ ಹಿಂದೆ ಅವನನ್ನು ಬೇಟೆಯಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದೆ. | nanu ee varshada hinde avanannu beteyaduttidde. | False | I've been hunting him this year past. | I have been beating him since the beginning of this year. | 0.44484228 | False | I was hunting him earlier this year. | 0.91876197 | 0.4739196886126709 | False |
| xnli-test-1602 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಅವರನ್ನು ಒಂದು ವರ್ಷದಿಂದ ಹತ್ತಿರದಿಂದ ಹಿಂಬಾಲಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದೇನೆ. | nanu avaranu ondu varshadinda hattiradinda himbalisuttiddene. | False | I have been following him closely for a year without him knowing. | I have been teasing him for a year since childhood. | 0.4906215 | False | I have been closely following them for a year. | 0.47306985 | -0.017551653369903553 | False |
| xnli-test-2832 | premise | ನಾವು ತಲಾ ಪರಿಮಾಣ ಮತ್ತು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಪ್ರತಿ ತುಣುಕುಗಳ ನಡುವೆ ನೇರ ರೇಖೀಯ ಸಂಬಂಧವನ್ನು ಊಹಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ | navu thala parimaana maththu sadhyaviruva prathi thunukugala naduve nera rekhiya sambandhavannu oohisutheve | False | We assume a direct linear relationship between volume per capita and pieces per possible | We are considering the direct linear relationship between head size and each possible measurement. | 0.4420306 | False | We assume a direct linear relationship between each quantity and each possible fragment. | 0.642829 | 0.20079840094985962 | False |
| xnli-test-2832 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಪ್ರತಿ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾದ ತುಣುಕುಗಳಂತೆ ಪರಿಮಾಣವು ನಿಖರವಾಗಿ ಒಂದೇ ಎಂದು ಊಹಿಸಿದೆವು. | navu prati sadhyavada tunukugalante parimanavu nikharavagi onde endu uhisdevu. | False | We figured the volume is exactly the same as the pieces per possible. | We will declare that the amount is exactly one, like a needle of every possible kind. | 0.46532708 | False | We assumed that the quantity is exactly the same for each possible piece. | 0.749447 | 0.2841199081057739 | False |
| xnli-test-3845 | premise | ಬಲವಾದ ಹಣಕಾಸಿನ ನಿರ್ವಹಣೆ, ಚಲನಶೀಲ ರಂಗಭೂಮಿ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಶೈಕ್ಷಣಿಕ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳ ದಾಖಲೆಯನ್ನು ಮುಂದುವರಿಸಲು ನಮಗೆ ಇನ್ನೂ ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸಹಾಯ ಬೇಕು ಎಂದು ನಿಮಗೆ ತಿಳಿಸಲು ಈ ಪತ್ರವನ್ನು ಬರೆಯಲಾಗಿದೆ. | balavada hanakasina nirvahane, chalanasheela rangabhoomi nirmaanagalu mattu atyuttama kaikshanika karyakramagala dakkaleyannu munduvarisalu namage innu nimma sahaya beku endu nimge tilisalu ee patravannu bareyalagide. | False | This letter is to let you know that we still need your help to continue our record of strong fiscal management, vibrant theatrical productions and outstanding educational programs. | We are writing this letter to let you know that we still need your help to advance the effective implementation, the development of dynamic playgrounds, and the best instant programs. | 0.56251276 | False | This letter is written to let you know that we still need your assistance to continue strong financial management, vibrant stage productions, and an outstanding record of educational programs. | 0.90156174 | 0.33904897706054693 | False |
| xnli-test-3845 | hypothesis | ನಮ್ಮ ರಂಗಭೂಮಿ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣಗಳು ಮುಂದುವರಿಯಲು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸಹಾಯ ಬೇಕು. | namma rangabhumi nirmanagalu munduvariyalu nimma sahaya beku. | False | We need your help to keep our theatrical productions running. | We need your help to move forward with our stage constructions. | 0.6228809 | False | We need your help to continue our stage productions. | 0.9138634 | 0.29098252048645024 | False |
| xnli-test-3648 | premise | ನಿಮಗೆ ಸಹಾಯ ಮಾಡಲು, ಬರೆಯಲು, ಫ್ಯಾಕ್ಸ್ ಅಥವಾ ಇ-ಮೇಲ್ ಮತ್ತು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮಗೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ತಿಳಿಸಿ. | nimage sahaaya maalu, bareyalu, phaks athavaa i-mel mattu nimma bagge nige hecchu thilisi. | False | To better help us assist you, write, fax or E-mail and tell us more about yourself. | To help you, write, fax, or email and tell us more about yourself. | 0.9073441 | False | To help you, write, fax or email and tell us more about yourself. | 0.91832876 | 0.010984662054443373 | False |
| xnli-test-3648 | hypothesis | ನಿಮ್ಮ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮಗೆ ಸಾಕಷ್ಟು ತಿಳಿದಿದ್ದರೆ ನಾವು ನಿಮ್ಮನ್ನು ದಿನಾಂಕದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಹೊಂದಿಸಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವಲ್ಲಿ ಉತ್ತಮವಾದ ಕೆಲಸವನ್ನು ಮಾಡುತ್ತೇವೆ. | nimma bagge namage sakuṣṭu tiḷididdare nāvu nimmannu dināṅkadoḍondige hondsikoḷḷuvalli uttamavāda kelasavannu māḍuttēve. | True | We do a better job at matching you with a date if we know a lot about you. | If we know a little about you, we will do the best work in taking care of you every day. | 0.5405082 | False | If we know enough about you, we will do a better job of matching you with dates. | 0.88209605 | 0.3415878521697998 | False |
| xnli-test-2853 | premise | ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ಟೈಟಲ್ V ಕಾರ್ಯಾಚರಣಾ ಅನುಮತಿಯನ್ನು ಮಾರ್ಪಡಿಸಲು ಒಟ್ಟು ಅಂದಾಜು ಸಮಯವು ಸುಮಾರು 17 ತಿಂಗಳುಗಳು, ಜೊತೆಗೆ ಅನುಸರಣೆ ಪರೀಕ್ಷೆಯನ್ನು ಪೂರ್ಣಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಹೆಚ್ಚುವರಿ ಸಮಯ. | aatradari, title V kaaryaacharana anumatiyannu maarpadisalu ottu andaaju samayavu sumaru 17 tingalugalu, jothege anusarane pariksheyannu purnagolisalu hechchuvari samaya. | False | Therefore, the total estimated time to modify the Title V operating permit is about 17 months, plus the additional time to complete compliance testing. | At present, to change the Title V work‑execution permission, the allotted time is exactly 17 minutes, along with enough time to complete the compliance test. | 0.640587 | False | Therefore, the total estimated time to modify the Title V operational permission is about 17 months, plus additional time to complete compliance testing. | 0.9022918 | 0.26170477474975584 | False |
| xnli-test-2853 | hypothesis | ಟೈಟಲ್ V ಆಪರೇಟಿಂಗ್ ಪರ್ಮಿಟ್ ಅನ್ನು ಮಾರ್ಪಡಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ. | Title V operating permit annu madparisalu sadhyavilla. | False | It is not possible to modify the Title V operating permit. | It is not possible to renew the Title V operating permit. | 0.91088325 | False | The Title V operating permit cannot be modified. | 0.8980147 | -0.012868525745391879 | False |
| xnli-test-4794 | premise | "ಆಲಿಸ್ ಆಕ್ಷೇಪಿಸಿದಾಗ," "ಆದರೆ ಅದು ವಿಭಿನ್ನ ರೀತಿಯ ಕಟ್ಟುಪಾಡು," "ಅವರು ಉತ್ತರಿಸಿದರು," "ಇದು ನನ್ನೊಂದಿಗೆ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ರೀತಿಯ ಕಟ್ಟುಪಾಡಾಗಿತ್ತು, ನಾನು ನಿಮಗೆ ಭರವಸೆ ನೀಡಬಲ್ಲೆ!" | Alis akshepisidaaga, "Aadare adu vibhinna reetiya kattupaadu," Avaru uttarisidaru, "Idu nannondige ella reetiya kattupaadagittu, naanu nimage bharavase needaballe!" | False | When Alice objected,  But that's a different kind of fastness,  he replied,  It was all kinds of fastness with me, I can assure you! | When Alis objected, “But that’s a different kind of nonsense,” he replied, “It has been nonsense in every way for me, and I’ll assure you.” | 0.39874876 | False | When Alice objected, but it was a different kind of trap, they replied, it was a trap of all kinds with me, I can assure you! | 0.52176166 | 0.12301289580749514 | False |
| xnli-test-4794 | hypothesis | ಇವೆಲ್ಲವನ್ನೂ ಆಲಿಸ್ ಒಪ್ಪಿಕೊಂಡಿದ್ದರು. | ivellavannoo alis oppikondiddaru. | False | Alice agreed with all of it. | They all sat together. | 0.348093 | False | Alice agreed to all of it. | 0.8780261 | 0.5299331278152466 | False |
| xnli-test-3746 | premise | ತಿಳಿವಳಿಕೆ ಮತ್ತು ಬದ್ಧತೆಯ ನಾಯಕರ ಹೊಸ ಪೀಳಿಗೆಯನ್ನು ನಾವು ಅಭಿವೃದ್ಧಿಪಡಿಸುವ ಸಲುವಾಗಿ ನಾವು ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರಿಗೆ ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಪ್ರಾಮುಖ್ಯತೆಯ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಶಿಕ್ಷಣ ನೀಡುವುದು ನಿರ್ಣಾಯಕವಾಗಿದೆ. | thilivalike mattu baddhathreya nayakara hosa peeligeyannu naavu abhivrudhipadisuva saluvaagi naavu amerikannarige daanadharmada praamukhyatheya bagge shikshana neevuduvudhu nirnaayakavaagide. | False | It is critical that we educate Americans about the importance of Philanthropy that we develop a new generation of informed and committed leaders. | By developing the new policy of the leaders of Thilivalike and Baddhathre, we are teaching Americans about the importance of charity, which is decisive. | 0.6018998 | False | In order to develop a new generation of knowledgeable and committed leaders, it is essential that we educate Americans about the importance of philanthropy. | 0.9805784 | 0.37867862254638673 | False |
| xnli-test-3746 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಸಾರ್ವಜನಿಕ ಶಾಲೆಗಳನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿಕೊಂಡು ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರಿಗೆ ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಮಹತ್ವವನ್ನು ಕಲಿಸಬೇಕು. | naavu sarvajanika shaalegalannu balasikondu amerikanarige daanadharmada mahatvannu kalisabeku. | False | We should use public schools to teach Americans about the importance of philanthropy. | We should teach Americans the importance of charity by supporting public schools. | 0.8691138 | False | We must use public schools to teach Americans the importance of charity. | 0.8987578 | 0.029644015361022902 | False |
| xnli-test-3079 | premise | ಸೌತ್ ಟವರ್ ಕುಸಿತವನ್ನು ಗಮನಿಸಿದ ಈ ಅಧಿಕಾರಿ, ತನ್ನ ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವ ಸೂಚನೆಯ ಮೂಲಕ ಉತ್ತರ ಟವರ್ನಲ್ಲಿರುವ ESU ಘಟಕಗಳಿಗೆ ವರದಿಯನ್ನು ಸಲ್ಲಿಸಿದರು. | South tower kusithavannu gamanisida ee adhikari, thanna sthalantharisuva soochaneya moolaka uttara towernalliroduva ESU ghatakagalige varadiyannu salisidaru. | False | This officer, who had observed the South Tower collapse, reported it to ESU units in the North Tower in his evacuation instruction. | The official who inspected the South tower’s vibration sent a warning, via his relocation signal, to the ESU components operating the North tower. | 0.623579 | False | The officer who observed the South Tower collapse submitted a report to the ESU units in the North Tower through his relocation notice. | 0.8910613 | 0.26748230599975586 | False |
| xnli-test-3079 | hypothesis | ಉತ್ತರ ಗೋಪುರದಲ್ಲಿ ಇಎಸ್ ಯು ಘಟಕಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ಅಧಿಕಾರಿ ಮಾತನಾಡುವ 30 ನಿಮಿಷಗಳ ಮೊದಲು ದಕ್ಷಿಣ ಗೋಪುರವು ಕುಸಿದು ಬಿದ್ದಿತು. | uttara gopuradalli isu ghatakangalondige adhikari matanaduva 30 nimishagala modalu dakshina gopuravu kusidu bidditu. | False | The South Tower collapsed 30 minutes before the officer talked to ESU units in the North Tower. | In the north tower, the official will speak to the victims for 30 minutes before the south tower collapsed. | 0.7825834 | False | Thirty minutes before the officer spoke with the ISU units at the North Tower, the South Tower collapsed. | 0.8924466 | 0.10986317754898067 | False |
| xnli-test-1766 | premise | ನಮ್ಮಲ್ಲಿ ಬಿಷಪ್ನ ಸೋದರಳಿಯರ ಸರಕು ಇದ್ದರೂ ಅದು ಅವನ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಹಿಡಿಯುವಂತೆ ಮಾಡುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | nammalli bishopna sodaraliyara saraku iddaru adu avana kaiyannu hidiyuvante maduvudilla. | False | Though we had a cargo of Bishop's nieces it wouldn't make him hold his hand. | In our place the bishop’s brothers were there, and they would never do anything like covering his hand. | 0.54530025 | False | Even if we have the bishop’s sister’s belongings, it won’t make him hold his hand. | 0.74219614 | 0.1968958926734924 | False |
| xnli-test-1766 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ತುಂಬ ಒತ್ತಡದ ಸಮಯಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಮಾತ್ರ ಅವನ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಹಿಡಿದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು. | avaru tumbaa ottadada samayagalalli maatra avana kaiyannu hididukolluttiddaru. | False | They would only hold his hand in times of great stress. | They only kept his hand hidden during very busy times. | 0.6361261 | False | They only held his hand during very stressful times. | 0.8528967 | 0.21677059036865232 | False |
| xnli-test-2771 | premise | ಡೇಟಾದ ವಿಶ್ವಾಸಾರ್ಹತೆಯನ್ನು ನಿರ್ಧರಿಸಲು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಆಧಾರವನ್ನು ಪ್ರಸ್ತುತಪಡಿಸಿ. | Datada vishwasarhateyannu nirdharisalu nimma adharavannu prastutupadisi. | False | Present your basis for assessing the reliability of the data as undetermined. | Present your evidence to determine the data’s reliability. | 0.80411357 | False | Present your evidence to determine the reliability of the data. | 0.7989273 | -0.005186262871093805 | False |
| xnli-test-2771 | hypothesis | ಈ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಸಂಪೂರ್ಣವಾಗಿ ವಿಶ್ವಾಸಾರ್ಹ ಎಂದು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | ee mahitiyannu sampurnavagi vishwasarh endu pariganisalagide. | False | The data was assessed to be entirely reliable. | This information is considered completely reliable. | 0.75513244 | False | This information is considered completely reliable. | 0.75513244 | -3.2476806799408564e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-350 | premise | ಆಗ ನಾನು ನನ್ನ ಚಕ್ರವನ್ನು ಸುತ್ತುತ್ತಿದ್ದೆ, ನನಗೆ ಗೊತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ, ದಿನವಿಡೀ ಹಾಗೆ ತೋರುತ್ತಿತ್ತು. | aaga nanu nanna chakravannu suttuttidde, nanage gottilla, dinavidi haage toruttittu. | False | Then I was spinning my wheel for about, I don't know, it seemed like all day that day. | Then I was turning my wheel, I didn’t know, and the day turned out like that. | 0.72330683 | False | Then I was turning my wheel, I don’t know, it seemed like that all day. | 0.8242671 | 0.10096025936691289 | False |
| xnli-test-350 | hypothesis | ಬಹಳ ಹೊತ್ತು ಚಕ್ರ ತಿರುಗಿಸಿದ್ದೆ. | bahala hottu chakra thirugisidde. | False | I spun my wheel for a long time. | It was very hot and the wheel was turning. | 0.53351206 | False | I have been turning the wheel for a long time. | 0.6527798 | 0.1192677575811768 | False |
| xnli-test-279 | premise | ಆಕೆಯ ಸಹೋದರಿ ಬಿಳಿ ಬಣ್ಣಕ್ಕೆ ದಾಟಬಹುದು, ಮತ್ತು ವಾಸ್ತವವಾಗಿ ಬಿಳಿ ಬಣ್ಣಕ್ಕೆ ದಾಟಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | aakeya sahodari bili bannakke daatabahudu, mattu vaastavaagi bili bannakke daatiddare. | False | Her sister could pass for white, and in fact did pass for white. | My sister can be painted white, and indeed was painted white. | 0.7047546 | False | Her sister can switch to white, and in fact has switched to white. | 0.7612169 | 0.0564622788909912 | False |
| xnli-test-279 | hypothesis | ಅವಳ ಸಹೋದರಿಯು ನೆರೆಹೊರೆಯ ಎಲ್ಲರ ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಸುಂದರವಾದ ಚರ್ಮವನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಳು. | avala sahodariyu nerehooreya ellara atyanta sundaravada carmavannu hondiddalu. | False | Her sister had the fairest skin of anyone in the neighborhood. | Her sister also found the extremely beautiful charm of everyone in Nerehoore. | 0.5245973 | False | Her sister had the most beautiful skin among everyone in the neighborhood. | 0.884248 | 0.35965071826477046 | False |
| xnli-test-840 | premise | ತೋಳಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ನೃತ್ಯಗಳು ನಾನು ಕುರಿಮರಿಗಳ ಮೌನವನ್ನು ನೋಡಿದ್ದೇನೆ ಎಂದು ನಾವು ನೋಡಿದ್ದೇವೆ | tolagalondige nrutyagalu nanu kurimarigala mounavannu nodiddene endu navu nodiddevi | False | uh Dances With Wolves we just saw that kind of late what else have i seen uh Silence of the Lambs | From the very beginning, the dances, I saw the silence of the crickets, and that is what we saw. | 0.33627105 | False | Dances with shadows, I have seen the silence of the lambs, we have seen. | 0.66985637 | 0.33358531949539183 | False |
| xnli-test-840 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ವೋಲ್ಫ್ಸ್ ಜೊತೆ ಡ್ಯಾನ್ಸ್ ನೋಡಿದೆ. | naanu wolfs jote dance nodide. | False | I watched Dances with Wolves. | I saw dancing with wolves. | 0.8685026 | False | I watched a dance with Wolves. | 0.85929674 | -0.009205860898590101 | False |
| xnli-test-1076 | premise | ವಿಶ್ವವು ಕನಿಷ್ಠ ಒಮ್ಮೆಯಾದರೂ ಉದ್ದದ ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪ್ರೋಟೀನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ತಯಾರಿಸಲು ವಿಶ್ವದ ಪ್ರಸಕ್ತ ಜೀವನಕಾಲಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಕನಿಷ್ಠ ಅನೇಕ ಪಟ್ಟು ಸಮಯ ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | vishvavu kanishta ommeyadaru uddada sadhyaviruva ella proteingalanu tayarisalu vishvada prasakta jeevanakalakkinta kanishta aneka pattu samaya tgedukolluttade. | False | It would take at least many times the current lifetime of the universe for the universe to manage to make all possible proteins of length at least once. | The world, even the smallest organism, takes a great deal of time to produce all the possible proteins that could exist, compared to the lifespan of a living being on Earth. | 0.6735711 | False | The universe would take at least many times the current age of the universe to produce even a single copy of all possible proteins of maximal length. | 0.9337268 | 0.2601556875671387 | False |
| xnli-test-1076 | hypothesis | ಇದು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿರುವ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪ್ರೋಟೀನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ತಯಾರಿಸಲು 100 ಶತಕೋಟಿ ವರ್ಷಗಳನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತದೆ. | idu sadhyaviruva ella protinkalannu thayarisalu 100 shatakoti varshagalannu thegedukolluthade. | False | It wuold take 100 billion years to make all possible proteins. | It takes 100 billion years to prepare all possible proteins. | 0.9331258 | False | It takes 100 hundred‑million years to produce all possible proteins. | 0.94197595 | 0.008850151194763178 | False |
| xnli-test-2036 | premise | "" "ಮೆಡಿಟರೇನಿಯನ್ನಲ್ಲಿನ ಅತ್ಯುತ್ತಮ ಬಂದರುಗಳು ಜೂನ್, ಜುಲೈ, ಆಗಸ್ಟ್ ಮತ್ತು ಮಾಯೆ" "ಎಂದು 16ನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ವೆನಿಸ್ ಅಡ್ಮಿರಲ್ ಆಂಡ್ರಿಯಾ ಡೋರಿಯಾ ಹೇಳಿದರು, ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯ ಸವಾರಿಯ ಹೊರತಾಗಿ, ಒಂದು ನೌಕಾಪಡೆಯು ಇಲ್ಲಿ ಆಶ್ರಯಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಉತ್ತಮವಾಗಿ ಕಾರ್ಯನಿರ್ವಹಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ." | "mediterrenyannalli na atyuttama bandrugalu june, julai, agust mattu maye" endu 16ne shatamandada venis admiral andriya doria helidaru, besigeya savariya horataagi, ondu naukapadeyu illi ashrayakkinta uttamavagi karyanirvahisalu sadhyavilla. | False | The best ports in the Mediterranean are June, July, August, and Mae,  said the 16th-century Venetian admiral Andrea Doria, noting that beyond the summer sailing season, a fleet couldn't do better than shelter here. | He said that the 16th‑century Venetian admiral Andrea Doria, in a hurry, could not operate even a single warship here any better than by relying on it. | 0.36829707 | False | The best ports in the Mediterranean are June, July, August, and May, said 16th‑century Venetian Admiral Andrea Doria; apart from the summer ride, a fleet cannot operate here any better than taking shelter. | 0.9286941 | 0.5603970589901733 | False |
| xnli-test-2036 | hypothesis | ಬೇಸಿಗೆಯ ಋತುವಿನಲ್ಲಿ ಉತ್ತಮ ಬಂದರುಗಳಿವೆ. | besigeya rutuvinalli uttama bandarulive. | False | The summer season has the best ports. | The best has arrived in the busy season. | 0.32911626 | False | There are good ports in the summer season. | 0.93649197 | 0.6073757062475587 | False |
| xnli-test-1234 | premise | ಸರ್ಕ್ಯೂಟ್ ನ್ಯಾಯಾಧೀಶರಾಗಿ ಏಕಾಂಗಿಯಾಗಿ ಕುಳಿತು, ಮುಖ್ಯ ನ್ಯಾಯಮೂರ್ತಿ ಟ್ಯಾನಿ ಸಾಂವಿಧಾನಿಕ ನಿಬಂಧನೆಗಳನ್ನು ವ್ಯಾಖ್ಯಾನಿಸುತ್ತಾ, ರಿಟ್ ಅರ್ಜಿಯನ್ನು ಅಮಾನತುಗೊಳಿಸಲು ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ನ ಅಧಿಕಾರದ ಅಗತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಪ್ರತಿಪಾದಿಸಿದರು. | circuit nyayadhisharagi ekangiyagi kulitu, mukhya nyayamurthi tany savidhanika nibandhanegalalnu vyakhyanisutta, writ arjiyannu amanatugolisalu congressna adhikarada avashyakatayannu pratipadisdaru. | False | Sitting alone as a circuit judge, Chief Justice Taney interpreted the constitutional provision to require congressional authorization for suspending the writ. | Having sat alone as a circuit judge, he explained the constitutional provisions of the chief justice and asserted the necessity of Congress’s authority to entrust the writ petition. | 0.7484875 | False | Sitting alone as a circuit judge, Chief Justice Tani interpreted constitutional provisions and argued the need for congressional authority to dismiss the writ petition. | 0.85248315 | 0.10399565334320071 | False |
| xnli-test-1234 | hypothesis | ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ಗೆ ಅಧಿಕಾರ ಹಿಡಿಯಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಿಲ್ಲ ಎಂದು ಟಾನಿ ಹೇಳಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | Congressge adhikaara hidiyalu sadhyavilla endu Tony heliddare. | False | Taney said Congress had no power to stop the writ. | Tony said that it is not possible to take away power from the Congress. | 0.5733596 | False | Tani says that Congress cannot take power. | 0.70590425 | 0.13254464537658694 | False |
| xnli-test-2959 | premise | ಸುಮಾರು 2,100 ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯ ವೃತ್ತಿಪರರು ತಮ್ಮ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯ ವರದಿಗಳನ್ನು ಆನ್-ಲೈನ್ ಮೂಲಕ 1999ರಲ್ಲಿ ಪಡೆದುಕೊಂಡರು, ಮತ್ತು HIC ಇತರ ವೈದ್ಯಕೀಯ ವೃತ್ತಿಪರರಿಗೆ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಯನ್ನು ಸೇರಿಸಲು ಮತ್ತಷ್ಟು ಸುಧಾರಣೆಗಳನ್ನು ಕಾರ್ಯಗತಗೊಳಿಸಿತು. | sumaru 2,100 samanya vruttipararu thamma prathikriyeya varadigalannu on-line moolaka 1999ralli padekonbaru, mattu HIC ithara vaidakeeya vruttipararige prathikriyeyannu serisalu mathastu sudharanegalannu karyagathagolisithu. | False | About 2,100 general practitioners accessed their feedback reports on-line during 1999, and HIC implemented further enhancements to include feedback to other medical practitioners. | In 1999, about 2,100 ordinary professionals submitted their feedback online, and HIC also added feedback for other legal professionals and implemented the necessary improvements. | 0.8181179 | False | Approximately 2,100 general professionals obtained their feedback reports online in 1999, and HIC implemented further improvements to add feedback for other medical professionals. | 0.94783247 | 0.129714565171814 | False |
| xnli-test-2959 | hypothesis | ಎಚ್ಐಸಿ ಯಾವುದೇ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆ ಬದಲಾವಣೆಗಳನ್ನು ಮಾಡಿಲ್ಲ. | HIC yavude pratikriye badalavanegalannu madilla. | False | HIC made no feedback changes. | He/she does not change any response. | 0.33068824 | False | HIC has not made any changes to the response. | 0.78965336 | 0.4589651208436584 | False |
| xnli-test-3276 | premise | ಜಾರ್ಜ್ ಎಚ್. ಡಬ್ಲ್ಯು. ಬುಷ್ ಅವರ ಆಡಳಿತದಲ್ಲಿ ಎನ್. ಎಸ್. ಸಿ. ಯ ಸದಸ್ಯರಾಗಿದ್ದ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಭದ್ರತಾ ಸಲಹೆಗಾರ ಕಾಂಡೊಲೀಜಾ ರೈಸ್ ಅವರು ಬುಷ್ ಅವರ ವೈಟ್ ಹೌಸ್ ಸಿಬ್ಬಂದಿಯ ಮುಖ್ಯ ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಗಳಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | George H. W. Bush avra adalitadalli N. S. C. ya sadasyaragida rastriya bhadrata salahegara Condoleezza Rice avru Bush avra White House sibbandiya mukhya vyaktigalagiruttare. | False | The principal figures on Bush's White House staff would be National Security Advisor Condoleezza Rice, who had been a member of the NSC staff in the administration of George H.W. | In George H. W. Bush’s administration, Condoleezza Rice, who was a member of the NSC and the national security adviser, was one of the key figures associated with Bush’s White House. | 0.8128203 | False | National Security Advisor Condoleezza Rice, who was a member of the NSC during George H.W. Bush’s administration, will be a key figure in Bush’s White House staff. | 0.8811942 | 0.06837387428970332 | False |

| xnli-test-4254 | premise | ಸಾಫ್ಟ್ವೇರ್ನೊಂದಿಗೆ, ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಯು ಸ್ವತಂತ್ರ, ವಿಶ್ವಾಸಾರ್ಹ ಸಂಸ್ಥೆಯಾಗಿದ್ದು, ಸಾಫ್ಟ್ವೇರ್ ಅದು ಹೇಳಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವಲ್ಲಿಂದ ಬರುತ್ತದೆ ಎಂದು ಪರಿಶೀಲಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | software nondige, agenciyu svatantra, visvasarha samstheyakittu, software adu helikolluvallinda baruttade endu parishelistade. | False | With software, the agency is an independent, trusted body verifying that software comes from where it claims. | The software is not ... the agency is independent, there is a trustworthy organization, and it is examined that the software comes from being told. | 0.7855625 | False | Along with the software, the agency is an independent, trustworthy organization that verifies that the software comes from the source it claims. | 0.9349596 | 0.14939709043502802 | False |

| xnli-test-2825 | premise | ಅಂಚೆ ಸಾಂದ್ರತೆಯ ಪರಿಣಾಮವು ಫ್ರಾನ್ಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ವೆಚ್ಚದ ಮೇಲೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಪರಿಣಾಮ ಬೀರುತ್ತದೆ ಎಂಬುದನ್ನು ಗಮನಿಸಬೇಕು. | anche sandateya parinaamavu france-nalli vechchada mele hecchu parinaama beetade enbadannu gamanisabeku. | False | It should be noted that the impact of postal density on cost is greater in France than in the | We should note that the result of the agreement does not yield a greater outcome when placed in France. | 0.39612132 | False | It should be noted that the effect of edge density has a greater impact on cost in France. | 0.6882446 | 0.2921232612225342 | False |

| xnli-test-908 | premise | ಹೌದು ನಾನು ಈ ವರ್ಷ ಅವರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಕೇಳಿದ್ದೇನೆ ನನ್ನ ಗೆಳೆಯ ಉಮ್ ಅವರು ಕೆಲವು ರೀತಿಯ ದೇಶೀಯ ಸಂಗೀತವನ್ನು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುತ್ತಾರೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅವರು ಅದನ್ನು ಕೇಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು ಮತ್ತು ಅದು. | haudu naanu ee varsha avara bagge keliddene nanna geleyan um avaru kelu reetiy deshiya sangeetavannu ishtapaduttare mattu avaru adannu keluttiddaru mattu adu. | False | yeah i i just um heard of him this year my boyfriend um he uh likes some types of country music and he was listening to that and it | Yes, I asked about them this year, my sister too, and they like Indian music, and they listened to it, and that. | 0.35093775 | False | Yes, I have heard about them this year; my friend Um likes a certain type of local music and was listening to it. | 0.45760942 | 0.1066716650543213 | False |

| xnli-test-433 | premise | ಇದು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಸರಳವಾಗಿದೆ, ಅವಳು ನನಗೆ ನೀಡಿದಂತೆಯೇ ಎಲ್ಲವೂ ವಿವರವಾಗಿ ಮತ್ತು ಜಟಿಲವಾಗಿದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಎರಡನೆಯದು ಸರಳವಾಗಿದೆ. | idu heccu saralavagide, avalu nanage nididanteye ellavu vivaravagi mattu jatilavagide mattu eradaneyadu saralavagide. | False | It's like more simplified, like the one that she had given me was all detailed and complicated and the second one is all simple. | This is very simple, she is like a dream to me, everything is detailed and complex, and the second one is simple. | 0.61960995 | False | This is simpler, everything is detailed and complex as she gave me, and the second one is simple. | 0.5984876 | -0.021122334414672816 | False |

| xnli-test-2784 | premise | ಜರ್ಮನ್ SCR ವ್ಯವಸ್ಥೆಯ ಪೂರೈಕೆದಾರ ನಾಲ್ಕು ವಾರಗಳಿಗಿಂತ ಕಡಿಮೆ ಅವಧಿಯ ಅವಧಿಯ ಒಳಗೆ ಜರ್ಮನ್ ಸಾಮರ್ಥ್ಯದ ಒಂದು ಗಮನಾರ್ಹ ಭಾಗದಲ್ಲಿ SCR ಅನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಾಪಿಸಿದನು. | german SCR vyavastheya puraikedara nalku varagaliginta kadime avadhiya avadhiya olage german samarthyadha ondu gamanarha bhagadalli SCR annu sthapisidanu. | False | A German SCR system supplier installed SCR on a significant portion of the German capacity within outage periods consisting of less than four weeks. | German SCR was established in less than four years, and within that short period a capable German segment set up the SCR. | 0.62882435 | False | The German SCR system supplier installed SCR in a significant portion of German capacity within less than four weeks. | 0.81946254 | 0.1906381877655029 | False |

| xnli-test-1486 | premise | ಈ ಗುಂಪಿನಲ್ಲಿ ಇಪ್ಪತ್ತನೇ ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯೂ ಇದೆ, ಇದು ಕೊಲಂಬಿಯಾ ಜಿಲ್ಲೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಅರ್ಹರಾಗಿರದ ನಾಗರಿಕರಿಗೆ ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷ ಮತ್ತು ಉಪಾಧ್ಯಕ್ಷರಿಗೆ ಮತ ಚಲಾಯಿಸುವ ಹಕ್ಕನ್ನು ವಿಸ್ತರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | ee gumpinalli ippattene thidupadiyu ide, idu colombia jillayalli arharagirada nagarikarige adhyaksha mattu upadhyaksharige matha chalayisuva hakkannu vistharistade. | False | Also in this group is the Twenty-Third Amendment, which extends the right to vote for the president and vice president to citizens otherwise qualified in the District of Columbia. | In this group there is currently a dispute, and it expands the right of the mayor and deputy mayor of the city in Colombia district to conduct religious activities. | 0.33628827 | False | This group includes the twentieth amendment, which extends the right to vote for president and vice president to citizens who are not eligible in the Columbia district. | 0.9069788 | 0.5706905159916686 | False |
| xnli-test-1486 | hypothesis | 23 ನೇ ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯು ನೀವು ರಾಜಧಾನಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ವಾಸಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದರೆ ನೀವು ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರಪತಿಗೆ ಮತ ಹಾಕಬಹುದು ಎಂದು ಹೇಳುತ್ತದೆ. | 23 ne thiddupadiyu neevu rajadhaniyalli vasisuttiddare neevu rashtrapatige matha hakabahudu endu heluttade. | False | The 23rd amendment says you can vote for the president if you live in the capital unless you were a felon. | If you are 23 years old and living in the capital, you can vote for the president, it says. | 0.7257701 | False | The 23rd amendment says that if you live in the capital, you can vote for the president. | 0.92839074 | 0.20262064134826663 | False |

| xnli-test-3380 | premise | "ಸಿಎನ್ಎನ್ ಮುಂದೆ ಸುಟ್ಟ ಪ್ರೆಡೇಟರ್ ಅನ್ನು ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ ಮೆರವಣಿಗೆ ಮಾಡುವಾಗ ಸಂಭಾವ್ಯ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮವನ್ನು ಮುಕ್ತಾಯಗೊಳಿಸುವ ಅಪಾಯವನ್ನು ಮೀರಿಸುತ್ತದೆ ಎಂದು ನಾನು ನಂಬುವುದಿಲ್ಲ" ಎಂದು ಅವರು ಬರೆದಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | CNN munde sutta predator annu taliban meravanige maduvaga sambhavya karyakramavannu mukhtayagolisuva apayavannu mirisuttade endu nanu nambuvudilla endu avaru barediddare | False | I do not believe the possible recon value outweighs the risk of possible program termination when the stakes are raised by the Taliban parading a charred Predator in front of CNN, he wrote. | I don’t believe they wrote that the disaster that will free the program when the Taliban go on a march after the predator that ran before CNN. | 0.5480417 | False | He wrote, I do not believe that the risk of ending the possible program outweighs the burnt predator in front of CNN during the Taliban parade. | 0.74832076 | 0.20027905834274295 | False |
| xnli-test-3380 | hypothesis | ಸಿಎನ್ಎನ್ ವಾಹಿನಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಪ್ರಿಡೇಟರ್ನಿಂದ ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ ಪಲಾಯನ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಿರುವುದನ್ನು ತೋರಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | CNN vahiniyalli predator ninda taliban palayana maduttiruvudannu torisalagide. | False | The Taliban was shown fleeing from the Predator on CNN. | CNN channel is showing the Taliban fleeing from the predator. | 0.9305656 | False | CNN showed that the Taliban are fleeing from a Predator. | 0.94393224 | 0.013366635240936287 | False |
| xnli-test-3787 | premise | "" "ದುರದೃಷ್ಟವಶಾತ್, ದಾನಧರ್ಮದ ಪ್ರಾಮುಖ್ಯತೆಯ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮ್ಮ ದೃಷ್ಟಿಕೋನವನ್ನು ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರು ಹಂಚಿಕೊಳ್ಳುವುದಿಲ್ಲ." | duradrishtavashat, danadharmada pramukhyateya bagge namma drishtikonavannu ella amerikarru hanchikolluvudilla. | False | Unfortunately, our view of the importance of philanthropy is not shared by all Americans. | Unfortunately, not all Americans will embrace our perspective on the importance of charity. | 0.83320355 | False | Unfortunately, not all Americans share our perspective on the importance of charity. | 0.86640084 | 0.03319728789825438 | False |
| xnli-test-3787 | hypothesis | ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನರು ಚರ್ಚ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಬೆಂಬಲಿಸುವುದು ಮುಖ್ಯವೆಂದು ಭಾವಿಸುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | ella amerikannaru churchgalannu bembalisuvudhu mukhyavendu bhaavisuvudilla. | False | Not all Americans think it's important to support churches. | All Americans do not consider building churches to be important. | 0.59854496 | False | Not all Americans think supporting churches is important. | 0.9845302 | 0.3859852504949951 | False |
| xnli-test-1796 | premise | ನಂತರ ಕೆಳಕಂಡಂತಿವೆಃ "" "ನೀವು ಅದನ್ನು ಆದೇಶಿಸಿದಿರೋ?" "" "ಅಪನಂಬಿಕೆಯ ಉಚ್ಚಾರಣೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಅವನು ಕೇಳಿದನು, ಆದರೆ ಲಾರ್ಡ್ ಜೂಲಿಯನ್ ಹುಬ್ಬೇರಿಸಿದನು." | xnli-test-2657 | premise | ಮೌಲ್ಯ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸುವ, ವ್ಯಾಪಾರ ಫಲಿತಾಂಶಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಗ್ರಾಹಕ ಕೇಂದ್ರಿತ ಪಾಲುದಾರರಾಗಲು | maulya srustisuva, vyapara phalitamangalalli grahaka kendrita paludararagalu | False | To be a Value Creating, Customer Focused Partner in Business Results | Value‑creating, customer‑focused initiatives in profit‑driven businesses | 0.6841837 | False | To become a customer‑centric partner in value‑creating business outcomes | 0.8659576 | 0.18177391775970464 | False |
