| xnli-test-4581 | premise | ಎಲ್ಲಕ್ಕಿಂತ ಮಿಗಿಲಾಗಿ, ನಾವು ಅಸಂತೋಷದ ಸತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದೇವೆ, ವಾಕ್ಚಾತುರ್ಯದ ಬರವಣಿಗೆಯು ಕೆಲವೊಮ್ಮೆ ಸ್ಮರಣೀಯವಾಗಿದೆ, ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಯನ್ನು ಜಟಿಲಗೊಳಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | Ellakkinta migilaagi, naavu asantoshaada satyavannu hondiddeve, vaakchaaturyada baravanigeyu kelvomme smaraniyavaagaitu, samasyeyannu jatilagolisuttade. | False | On top of all this, we have the unhappy fact that eloquent writing is indeed sometimes memorable, compounding the problem. | More than anything, we have uncovered the truth of discontent, the rain of the verse has become memorable, and the problem is getting tangled. | 0.32055742 | False | Above all, we have the truth of unhappiness, the writing of wit is sometimes memorable, it complicates the problem. | 0.5402003 | 0.21964287306411745 | False |
| xnli-test-4581 | hypothesis | ಜನರು ಕಳಪೆ ಬರವಣಿಗೆಯನ್ನು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ನೆನಪಿಸಿಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತಾರೆ. | janaru kalape baravanigeyannu hecchu nenapisikolluttaare. | False | People are much more likely to remember poor writing. | People tend to remember the dream more. | 0.41147003 | False | People tend to remember poor handwriting more. | 0.8126695 | 0.4011994856097412 | False |
| xnli-test-4136 | premise | ಈ ಎಂಟು ಸರಳ ತಂತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿಕೊಂಡು, ನೀವು ನಿಮ್ಮ ಸ್ವಂತ ಮನೆಯಲ್ಲೇ ಆರಾಮವಾಗಿ ಒಂದು ಸುದ್ದಿ ಕಥೆಯನ್ನು ರಚಿಸಬಹುದು. | ee entu sarala tantragalannu balasikondu, neevu nimmag swanta maneyalle aramavaagi ondu suddi katheyannu rachisabahudu. | False | Using these eight simple techniques, you can fabricate a news story in the comfort of your own home. | By using these simple tools, you can comfortably write a short story in your own home. | 0.48274055 | False | Using these eight simple techniques, you can comfortably create a news story right in your own home. | 0.9505714 | 0.4678308678085327 | False |
| xnli-test-4136 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಕೆಲವು ಸರಳ ತಂತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು ಅನುಸರಿಸಿದರೆ ನೀವು ಯಾವುದೇ ಸ್ಥಳದಿಂದ ಸುದ್ದಿ ಕಥೆಯನ್ನು ಬರೆಯಬಹುದು. | neevu kelavu sarala tantragalannu anusarisidare neevu yaavude sthaladinda suddi katheyannu bareyabahudu. | False | You can write a news story from any location if you follow some simple techniques. | If you follow some simple steps, you can write a story from any place. | 0.7216295 | False | If you follow some simple techniques, you can write a news story from anywhere. | 0.90503395 | 0.18340444603729245 | False |
| xnli-test-1490 | premise | ತಿದ್ದುಪಡಿಯ ಧ್ವನಿಯು ಚುನಾವಣಾ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯ ಮೇಲೆ ರಾಜ್ಯಗಳ ನಿಯಂತ್ರಣಕ್ಕೆ, ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಕಚೇರಿಗೆ ಸಹ ಗೌರವಯುತವಾಗಿ ಉಳಿದಿದೆ. | tiddupadiya dhvaniyu chunavana prakriyeya mele rajygala niyantranakke, rashtriya kacherige saha gouravayutavagi ulidide. | False | The tone of the amendment remains deferential to the states' control of the electoral process, even for national office. | The drum’s sound has risen proudly for the states’ control over the selection process, along with national security. | 0.3406452 | False | The voice of the amendment has remained respectfully directed toward the national office as well, concerning the states' control over the electoral process. | 0.8654705 | 0.5248253286026001 | False |
| xnli-test-1490 | hypothesis | ರಾಜ್ಯಗಳು ಚುನಾವಣಾ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯನ್ನು ನಿಯಂತ್ರಿಸುತ್ತವೆ. | rajyagalu chunavana prakriyeyanu niyantrisuttave. | False | The states rule the electoral process. | States control the selection process. | 0.6848881 | False | States control the election process. | 0.9057256 | 0.2208374983352661 | False |
| xnli-test-1279 | premise | "ದುಃಖದ ಹುಡುಗಿ" ಯಂತಹ ಹೆಸರುಗಳು | Duhkhada hudugi yanthaha hesarugalu | False | Names, such as  Sad Girl | The sad girl has many names | 0.6504857 | False | Names like “Sad Girl” | 0.90501463 | 0.2545289341323853 | False |
| xnli-test-1279 | hypothesis | ಹೆಸರುಗಳು, ಸಂತೋಷದ ಹುಡುಗಿಯಂತೆ | hesarugalu, samtoshada hudugiyante | False | Names, like happy girl | Names, like a happy girl | 0.88776386 | False | Names, like a happy girl | 0.88776386 | -2.15850826101871e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-1809 | premise | ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಬ್ಲಡ್ನ ಪಕ್ಕದಲ್ಲಿ ನಿಂತು, ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಸೂಚಿಸಿದ ನಂತರ, ಅವನು ತಿರುಗಿ ನೋಡುತ್ತಾನೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಆಶ್ಚರ್ಯದಿಂದ ಕೂಗುತ್ತಾನೆ. | captain bloodna pakkadalli nintu, captain kaiyannu soochisida nantara, avanu thirugi noduthaane, mattu aashcharyadinda kooguthaane. | False | Standing beside Captain Blood, he looked astern, following the indication of the Captain's hand, and cried out in his amazement. | Standing beside Captain Blood, after the captain pointed his hand, he turned to look and flinched in surprise. | 0.83448124 | False | Standing beside Captain Blood, after the captain points his hand, he turns to look and shouts in surprise. | 0.85211146 | 0.017630218778381335 | False |
| xnli-test-1809 | hypothesis | ಕ್ಯಾಪ್ಟನ್ ಬ್ಲಡ್ ಆತ್ಮಸ್ಥೈರ್ಯವನ್ನು ಉತ್ತೇಜಿಸುವಲ್ಲಿ ಕುಖ್ಯಾತರಾಗಿದ್ದರು. | captain blood atmashairyavannu uttejisuvalli kukhyataragiddaru. | False | Captain Blood was notoriously good at promoting morale. | Captain Blood was famous for encouraging self‑pride. | 0.7770346 | False | Captain Blood was renowned for encouraging bravery. | 0.83474624 | 0.05771164156951902 | False |
| xnli-test-447 | premise | ತನಗೇನು ಅಗತ್ಯವಿದೆಯೆಂದು ಅವನು ನನಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದನು ಮತ್ತು ಇಂದು ತನಗೇನು ಅಗತ್ಯವಿದೆಯೆಂದು ಅವನು ನನಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದನು. | tanaganu agatyavideyendu avanu nanage helidanu mattu indu tanaganu agatyavideyendu avanu nanage helidanu. | False | He told me what exactly he needed and that he did need it by today. | He told me that he is needed and then he told me again that he is needed. | 0.53017795 | False | He told me what he needed, and today he told me what he needed. | 0.5993188 | 0.06914085235671996 | False |
| xnli-test-447 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಅದನ್ನು ಯಾವಾಗ ಬೇಕಾದರೂ ಹಾಕಬಹುದು ಎಂದು ಅವರು ಹೇಳಿದರು. | Naanu adanu yavaga bekadaru hakabahudu endu avaru helidaru. | False | He said I could turn it in whenever. | I told him that he can put it whenever he wants. | 0.6128291 | False | They said that I could put it in whenever I wanted. | 0.59946597 | -0.013363133775329605 | False |
| xnli-test-256 | premise | ನೀವು ಶಾಲೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ದೊಡ್ಡವರಾಗಿದ್ದಾಗ, ನೀವು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುವ ಅಥವಾ ದ್ವೇಷಿಸುವ ಯಾವುದಾದರೂ ಪುಸ್ತಕಗಳಿವೆಯೇ? | neevu shaaleyalli doddavaragiddaga, neevu ishtapaduva athava dveshisuva yavudadaru pusthakagaliveye? | False | Um, are there any, you said you don't remember reading anything in particular, like when you were older in school, were there any books that you read that you liked or hated? | When you were older in school, are there any books you liked or disliked? | 0.7144165 | False | When you were an adult at school, were there any books you liked or disliked? | 0.6902251 | -0.02419137564086915 | False |
| xnli-test-256 | hypothesis | ನೀವು ಕೆಲವು ಪುಸ್ತಕಗಳನ್ನು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುತ್ತೀರಾ ಅಥವಾ ದ್ವೇಷಿಸುತ್ತೀರಾ? | neevu kelavu pustakagalannu ishtapaduttira athava dveshisuttira? | False | Did you like or hate certain books? | Do you like or dislike some books? | 0.9160671 | False | Do you like or dislike some books? | 0.9160671 | 2.341308591446989e-08 | False |
| xnli-test-4813 | premise | ಸಶಸ್ತ್ರ ಪಡೆಗಳ ಒಂದು ವಿಭಾಗವೆಂದರೆ ನಿರ್ಮಾಣ ಬ್ಯಾಟಾಲಿಯನ್, ಇದನ್ನು ತ್ವರಿತವಾಗಿ CB ಎಂದು ಸಂಕ್ಷಿಪ್ತಗೊಳಿಸಲಾಯಿತು. | sashastra padegala ondu vibhagavendare nirmaana battalion, idannu thritavaagi CB endu sankshiptagolisalayitu. | False | One segment of the armed forces is the Construction Batallion, which was quickly abbreviated to C.B. | A construction battalion is a unit of the armed forces, and it was later abbreviated as CB. | 0.74864846 | False | One of the armed forces units is the construction battalion, which was quickly abbreviated as CB. | 0.8412111 | 0.0925626205511475 | False |
| xnli-test-4813 | hypothesis | ನಿರ್ಮಾಣ ಬ್ಯಾಟಾಲಿಯನ್ ಸಶಸ್ತ್ರ ಪಡೆಗಳ ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಪ್ರಮುಖ ಶಾಖೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದಾಗಿದೆ. | nirmaana battalion sashastra padegala atyanta pramukha shakhegalalli ondagide. | False | The Construction Batallion is one of the most important branches of the armed forces. | The construction battalion is present in the most important branches of the armed forces. | 0.86852115 | False | The construction battalion is one of the most important branches of the armed forces. | 0.8912096 | 0.022688452355957067 | False |
| xnli-test-4980 | premise | "" "ನನ್ನ ಕರೆಯನ್ನು ಸ್ವೀಕರಿಸಿದ್ದಕ್ಕೆ ಧನ್ಯವಾದಗಳು" "ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯುತ್ತಾರೆ." | nanna kareyannu sveekarisiddakke dhanyavaadagalu endu kareyuttaare. | False | Caller says,  Thank you for taking my call. | They say thank you for accepting my request. | 0.47461346 | False | They say thank you for accepting my call. | 0.7862692 | 0.3116557279272461 | False |
| xnli-test-4980 | hypothesis | ಕರೆಗೆ ಯಾರೂ ಸ್ಪಂದಿಸಲಿಲ್ಲ. | Karege yaaru spandaslilla. | False | No one answered the caller. | No one feels any sympathy for him. | 0.22350118 | False | No one responded to the call. | 0.9313003 | 0.7077991024783326 | False |
| xnli-test-1660 | premise | ಅಯ್ಯೋ-- ಸುಳ್ಳುಗಳ ಒಪ್ಪಂದ, ನಾನು ನಿಮಗೆ ಸಾಬೀತುಪಡಿಸಬಹುದಾದಂತೆ. | ayyo-- sullugala oppanda, nanu nimage sabithupadisabahudadante. | False | Aye--a deal of lies, devil a doubt, as I could prove to you. | Oh dear, stop the nonsense, I can teach you the syllabus. | 0.22008613 | False | Oh—a pact of lies, as I can prove to you. | 0.6957178 | 0.47563168158447267 | False |
| xnli-test-1660 | hypothesis | ನಿಮ್ಮ ಅರಿವಿಲ್ಲದೆ ನಾನು ಸಂಗ್ರಹಿಸಿದ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಪುರಾವೆಗಳಿಂದಾಗಿ ನಾನು ಇದನ್ನು ಸಾಬೀತುಪಡಿಸಲು ಸಾಧ್ಯವಾಯಿತು. | nimma arivillade naanu samgrahisida ella puravegalindagi naanu idanu saabithupadisalu sadhyavayitu. | False | I could prove this because of all the evidence I had collected without your knowledge. | Without your knowledge, I was able to prove that I am the one who collected all the evidence. | 0.85526854 | False | I was able to prove this because of all the evidence I gathered without your knowledge. | 0.9107779 | 0.05550938644500736 | False |
| xnli-test-4358 | premise | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ ಬೀದಿಯಲ್ಲಿ (ಬಾಸ್ ಟ್ವೀಡ್ನ ಪ್ರೇತದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಡೊನಾಲ್ಡ್ ಟ್ರಂಪ್ ನಗ್ನ ಸ್ವಿಂಗ್ ನೃತ್ಯವನ್ನು ಹೊರತುಪಡಿಸಿ) ಅತ್ಯಂತ ಕಿರಿಕಿರಿಯನ್ನುಂಟುಮಾಡುವ ದೃಶ್ಯವೆಂದರೆ ಯಾರಾದರೂ ಸೆಲ್ಫೋನ್ ಬಳಸುತ್ತಿರುವುದು. | New York beediyalli (boss tweedna prethadondige Donald Trump nagna swing nruthyavannu horathupadisi) atyantha kirikiriyuntu maaduva drushyavendare yaaradaru cellphone balasuthiruvadhu. | False | The most irritating sight on a New York street (excluding Donald Trump's naked swing dancing with the ghost of Boss Tweed) is anyone jabbering into a cell phone. | In New York City (the boss made Donald Trump do a swing dance to impress the president), the scene is so wildly exciting that someone is holding a cellphone. | 0.67081547 | False | The most irritating sight on a New York street (aside from Donald Trump’s naked swing dance with the ghost of Boss Tweed) is someone using a cellphone. | 0.95061547 | 0.27979999564102176 | False |
| xnli-test-4358 | hypothesis | ನ್ಯೂಯಾರ್ಕ್ನಲ್ಲಿ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ಜನರು ಆಪಲ್ ಫೋನ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | New Yorknalli hechchina janaru apple phonegalannu hondiddare. | False | People mostly have Apple phones in New York. | Wealthy people in New York have Apple phones. | 0.8631375 | False | Many people in New York have Apple phones. | 0.96747494 | 0.10433743743896484 | False |
| xnli-test-439 | premise | ಇಸವಿ 1962ರ ಕೊನೆಯಲ್ಲಿ, ಆಹ್, ವಾಷಿಂಗ್ಟನ್ ಡಿ. | isavi 1962ra koneyalli, aah, washingtan di. | False | Late 1962, uh, I got orders to go to Washington, D.C. | At the end of 1962, ah, I was washing. | 0.5327999 | False | At the end of AD 1962, ah, Washington D.C. | 0.6502998 | 0.11749988752136231 | False |
| xnli-test-439 | hypothesis | ಸೇನೆಯು ನನ್ನನ್ನು ತಕ್ಷಣವೇ DCಗೆ ಕಳುಹಿಸಿತು. | seneyu nannannu takshanave DCge kaluhisitu. | False | The army sent me to DC right away. | The army just sent me to DC. | 0.84064746 | False | The army sent me to DC immediately. | 0.96591765 | 0.1252701868849182 | False |
| xnli-test-3353 | premise | ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆಯ ವಿರುದ್ಧದ ಹೋರಾಟದಲ್ಲಿ ಈ ವ್ಯತ್ಯಾಸಗಳು ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಕೃತಕವಾಗಿ ತೋರುತ್ತಿವೆ. | bhayotpaadaneya viruddhada horaatadalli ee vyatyasagalu hechchu krutakavaagi thoruttive. | False | In the struggle against terrorism these distinctions seem increasingly artificial. | In resisting oppression, these differences become more evident. | 0.51762104 | False | In the fight against terrorism, these differences appear more artificial. | 0.9040543 | 0.3864332440957642 | False |
| xnli-test-3353 | hypothesis | ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆ ವಿರುದ್ಧ ಹೋರಾಟವಿದೆ. | bayotpadane viruddha horatavide. | False | There is a struggle against terrorism. | It is a contrary behavior. | 0.11324946 | True | There is a fight against terrorism. | 0.9525567 | 0.8393072693167114 | False |
| xnli-test-156 | premise | ನಾನು ಏನಾದರೂ ಮಾಡಬಹುದಿತ್ತು. | naanu enadaru madabahudittu. | False | If there was anything I could do. | I could have done something. | 0.515877 | False | I could have done something. | 0.515877 | 8.438110321584702e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-1082 | premise | ಸೊಂಜಾ ತನ್ನ ಮಗಳ ಕಿರುಚಾಟವನ್ನು ಅನುಕರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಾರಂಭಿಸಿದಳು. | sonja thanna magala kiruchatavannu anukarisalu prarambhisidalu. | False | Sonja the  child  began to mimic her daughter's tantrums. | Sonja began to think about her own daughter's cuteness. | 0.6030333 | False | Sonja began to imitate her daughter's shouting. | 0.82846737 | 0.22543406907958985 | False |
| xnli-test-1082 | hypothesis | ಸೋನ್ಯಾ ಅಸಮಾಧಾನಗೊಂಡಿದ್ದಳು. | Sonya asamadhanagondiddalu. | False | Sonja was upset. | Sonya was dissatisfied. | 0.5823371 | False | Sonia was upset. | 0.6387562 | 0.056419115572357126 | False |
| xnli-test-4691 | premise | ಕೊನೆಯದಾಗಿ, ನಿರಂತರತೆಯು ಒಂದು ಸದ್ಗುಣವಾಗಿದೆ, ಅಥವಾ ಅದನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿಲ್ಲದವರು ಅದನ್ನು ತಮ್ಮ ಮೇಲೆ ಹೇರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | koneyadagi, nirantareyuyondu sadgunavagide, athava adanu hondilladavaru adanu thamma mele heruthare. | False | Continence, after all, is a virtue, or so say those who do not have it imposed on them. | Finally, it has become a continuous virtue, or those who are not good will bring it upon themselves. | 0.5808321 | False | Finally, continuity is a virtue, or those who lack it impose it upon themselves. | 0.5540471 | -0.02678499230346676 | False |
| xnli-test-4691 | hypothesis | ತಮ್ಮ ಮೇಲೆ ನಿರ್ಬಂಧವನ್ನು ಹೇರದಿರುವವರು ಅದನ್ನು ಉತ್ತಮ ಗುಣವೆಂದು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | thamma mele nirbandhavannu heradiruvavaru adannu uttama gunavendu pariginisuvaru. | False | Those who do not have continence imposed on them consider it a good quality. | Those who impose obligations on themselves regard it as a great virtue. | 0.49925244 | False | Those who do not impose restrictions on themselves consider it a good quality. | 0.73211014 | 0.2328577027078247 | False |
| xnli-test-2521 | premise | ಈ ವಿಶ್ಲೇಷಣೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಬಳಸಲಾದ ಮೂಲ ಅಧ್ಯಯನಗಳ ನಮ್ಮ ಪರೀಕ್ಷೆಯು GAM ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಗಳಿಂದ ಸಂಭಾವ್ಯ ಪರಿಣಾಮ ಬೀರುವ ಆರೋಗ್ಯ ಅಂತ್ಯ ಬಿಂದುಗಳು ಬೇಸ್ ಮತ್ತು ಪರ್ಯಾಯ ಅಂದಾಜುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಆಸ್ಪತ್ರೆಯ ಪ್ರವೇಶವನ್ನು ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮಾಡಿವೆ ಎಂದು ಕಂಡುಹಿಡಿಯುತ್ತದೆ. | ee vishleshaneyalli badasalada mula adhyayanagala namma pariksheyu GAM samasyegalinda sambhavya parinama beuru aarogya anthya bindugalu base mattu paryaya andajugalalli aspatreya praveshavanu kammi madive endu kanduhidiyuttade. | False | Our examination of the original studies used in this analysis finds that the health endpoints that are potentially affected by the GAM issues  reduced hospital admissions in both the Base and Alternative Estimates | In this analysis, our review of the underlying studies indicates that because of GAM problems the likely outcome is a reduction in health endpoints, and based on the base and alternative estimates, hospital admissions will be lowered. | 0.8964219 | False | Our review of the primary studies used in this analysis finds that potential health outcomes affected by GAM issues have reduced hospital admissions in both the base and alternative estimates. | 0.92356455 | 0.02714265326080323 | False |
| xnli-test-2521 | hypothesis | ಆರೋಗ್ಯ ಎಂಡ್ಪಾಯಿಂಟ್ಗಳು ಕೆಲವು ಆಸ್ಪತ್ರೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಜನರ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆಯನ್ನು ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮಾಡಿದವು. | arogya endpointgalu kelu aspatregalli janara sankhyeyanu kamdi madidavu. | False | Health endpoints reduced the number of people in some hospitals. | The health endpoints reduced the number of people in the hospital. | 0.92098975 | False | Health endpoints reduced the number of people in some hospitals. | 0.9999999 | 0.0790101307907104 | False |
| xnli-test-2143 | hypothesis | ನೂರಾರು ವರ್ಷಗಳಷ್ಟು ಹಳೆಯದಾದ ಚಹಾ ಕೆಟಲ್ಗಳನ್ನು ನೀವು ಇಷ್ಟಪಡುತ್ತೀರಿ. | ooraru varshagalashtu haleyadada chaha kettalgalannu neevu ishtapaduttiri. | False | You will like the tea kettles that are hundreds of years old. | You like very old tea kettles. | 0.77321047 | False | You like tea kettles that are hundreds of years old. | 0.8272623 | 0.05405181237152101 | False |

| xnli-test-2829 | premise | 13, 212 ವಸತಿ ಮಾರ್ಗಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಪ್ರತಿಯೊಂದಕ್ಕೂ ಪರಿಮಾಣ ಮತ್ತು ವಿತರಣೆಯ ಅಂಕಿಅಂಶಗಳ ಜೊತೆಗೆ, ಸಿಸಿಎಸ್ ಪ್ರತಿ ಮಾರ್ಗದಿಂದ ಸೇವೆ ಸಲ್ಲಿಸುವ ಸಂಬಂಧಿತ 5-ಅಂಕಿಯ ಜಿಪ್ ಕೋಡ್ ಅನ್ನು ಒದಗಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | 13, 212 vasathi margagalalli pratiyondakku parimana mattu vitaraneya ankialgala jothege, sics prati margadinda seve sallisuva sambandhita 5-ankiya zip code annu odagisuttade. | False | In addition to the volume and delivery statistics for each of the 13,212 residential routes, CCS provides the associated 5-Digit ZIP Code served by each route. | 13,212 residential streets each have measurement and distribution numbers, and the associated five-digit ZIP code is read from each road’s service. | 0.7420926 | False | For each of the 13,212 housing routes, along with quantity and distribution statistics, the CCS provides the related 5‑digit ZIP code served by each route. | 0.94984037 | 0.20774776684036256 | False |

| xnli-test-4308 | premise | ಇಂದಿನ ಪ್ರಶ್ನೆಯು ನಾನು ರೇಡಿಯೋ ಸಿಟಿ ಮ್ಯೂಸಿಕ್ ಹಾಲ್ ಕ್ರಿಸ್ಮಸ್ ಸ್ಪರ್ಧೆಗೆ ಹೋದ ಒಮ್ಮೆ ಮಾತ್ರ ನನಗೆ ನೆನಪಿಸುತ್ತದೆ, ಅಲ್ಲಿ ಅವರು ಇತರ ವಿಷಯಗಳ ಜೊತೆಗೆ, ಲಿವಿಂಗ್ ನೇಟಿವಿಟಿ ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯಲ್ಪಡುವ ಏನನ್ನಾದರೂ ನೀಡುತ್ತಾರೆ. | indina prashneyu naanu rediyo siti myusik hal krisamas spardhege hoda omme matra nange nenapisuttatte, alli avaru itara vishyagala jotege, living netivi endu kareyalpaduva enannadaru niduttare. | False | Today's question reminds me of the one and only time I went to the Radio City Music Hall Christmas Pageant, at which, among other things, they offer something called the Living Nativity. | Today's question reminds me of only one thing—the radio, music, and the Christmas competition—and there, along with other topics, they say that living is something that makes people fall asleep. | 0.30871 | False | Today's question reminds me of the one time I went to the Radio City Music Hall Christmas competition, where, along with other things, they offered something called "Living Nativity." | 0.95102614 | 0.6423161416435241 | False |

| xnli-test-3496 | premise | ಇಸ್ಲಾಮಿಕ್ ಜಗತ್ತಿನಲ್ಲಿ ತನ್ನನ್ನು ತಾನು ವ್ಯಾಖ್ಯಾನಿಸಿಕೊಳ್ಳಲು ಅಮೆರಿಕ ಆಕ್ರಮಣಕಾರಿಯಾಗಿ ವರ್ತಿಸದಿದ್ದರೆ, ಉಗ್ರಗಾಮಿಗಳು ಸಂತೋಷದಿಂದ ನಮಗಾಗಿ ಕೆಲಸ ಮಾಡುತ್ತಾರೆ. | Islamic jagattinali tannannu tanu vyakhyanisikollalu amerika akramanakariyagi vartisadiddare, ugragamigalu santoshadinda namagi kelasa maduttare. | False | If the United States does not act aggressively to define itself in the Islamic world, the extremists will gladly do the job for us. | If the Islamic world tries to explain itself aggressively like America, the militants will work for us with joy. | 0.72851986 | False | If America does not act as an aggressor to define itself in the Islamic world, the militants will gladly work for us. | 0.8349392 | 0.10641932180465696 | False |

| xnli-test-2883 | premise | 61-ಫೆಡರಲ್ ನೌಕರರು ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ಭದ್ರತೆ 62 ಮತ್ತು ಮೆಡಿಕೇರ್ ನಂತಹ ಸಾಮಾಜಿಕ ವಿಮಾ ಕಾರ್ಯಕ್ರಮಗಳ ವ್ಯಾಪ್ತಿಗೆ ಒಳಪಡಬಹುದು. | 61-federal naukararu samajika bhadrate 62 mattu medicare nanta samajika vima karyakramagala vyaptige olpadabahudu. | False | 61--Federal employees may be covered by social insurance programs such as Social Security62 and Medicare under the same terms and conditions as the rest of the covered population. | Federal employees can be covered under social welfare, Medicare and other social insurance programs. | 0.89242727 | False | Federal employees may be covered by social insurance programs such as Social Security and Medicare. | 0.91150355 | 0.019076283390502935 | False |

| xnli-test-4060 | premise | ಆಗಸ್ಟ್ 25ರಂದು, ಅಟ್ಲಾಂಟಿಕ್ ಸಿಟಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಡೆಮಾಕ್ರಟಿಕ್ ಅಧಿವೇಶನ ಆರಂಭವಾದ ನಂತರ, 56 ವರ್ಷದ ಎನ್. ಜೆ. ಜಾನ್ಸನ್, ಅಧ್ಯಕ್ಷೀಯ ಸ್ಪರ್ಧೆಯಿಂದ ಹಿಂದೆ ಸರಿಯಲು ಮೂರು ಧ್ವನಿಮುದ್ರಿತ ಸಂಭಾಷಣೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಬೆದರಿಕೆ ಹಾಕಿದರು. | August 25randu, Atlantic Cityyalli Democratic adhiveshana aarambhavada nanthara, 56 varshada N. | xnli-test-2623 | premise | ಹಿಂಪಡೆಯಲು ಒಂದು ಔಪಚಾರಿಕ ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾವನೆ ಇಲ್ಲದೆ, ಫೆಡರಲ್ ನ್ಯಾಯಾಲಯದಲ್ಲಿ ದಾಖಲೆಯ ವಕೀಲರು, ನೈತಿಕವಾಗಿ ಮತ್ತು ನ್ಯಾಯಾಲಯದ ನಿಯಮಗಳ ಅಡಿಯಲ್ಲಿ, ಉದ್ಭವಿಸಬೇಕಾದ ಯಾವುದೇ ವಿಷಯಕ್ಕೆ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯಿಸಲು ಜವಾಬ್ದಾರರಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | himpedayalu ondu oupacharika prastavane illade, federal nyayalayadalli dakhaleya vakilaru, naitikavagi mattu nyayalayada niyamagala adiyalli, udbhavisabakada yavude vishayakke pratiyisalu javabdararagiruttare. | False | Absent a formal motion to withdraw, an attorney of record in federal court remains responsible, both ethically and under the rules of court, for responding to any matter that should arise. | For the underprivileged, without any administrative proposal, the intervening lawyers in the federal court, ethically and at the foundation of the court’s rules, are responsible for responding to any emerging issue. | 0.5470847 | False | Without a formal proposal to withdraw, the record attorneys in federal court are responsible, ethically and under the court’s rules, to respond to any matter that may arise. | 0.8973856 | 0.3503008972290039 | False |
| xnli-test-2623 | hypothesis | ಹಿಂತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುವ ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾವನೆ ಇಲ್ಲದೆ, ಪೌರತ್ವವನ್ನು ಒಳಗೊಂಡ ಒಂದು ಹೊರತುಪಡಿಸಿ ಯಾವುದೇ ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆಗೆ ಅಟಾರ್ನಿ ಜವಾಬ್ದಾರನಾಗಿರುತ್ತಾನೆ. | hintegedukolluva prastavane illade, pouratvavannu olgonda ondu horutupadisi yavude pratikriyage atarni javabdaranaagiruttane. | False | Without a motion to withdraw, an attorney is responsible for any response except for one involving citizenship. | Without the preceding proposal, causing a disturbance, he will be held responsible for any reaction. | 0.44011828 | False | Without a proposal to withdraw, the attorney is responsible for any response except one that includes citizenship. | 0.87865424 | 0.4385359615618897 | False |

| xnli-test-4234 | premise | ಮುಖಪುಟದ ಕಥೆಯು ಕೊಸೊವೊವನ್ನು ಕೆತ್ತಲು ಅಥವಾ ಅದನ್ನು ರಕ್ಷಣಾಧಿಕಾರಿಯಾಗಿ ನಿರ್ವಹಿಸುವುದು ನ್ಯಾಟೋದ ಗುರಿಯಾಗಿರಬೇಕೆ ಎಂಬುದರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಚರ್ಚಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | mukhaputada katheyu kosovovannu kettalu athava adanu rakshanadhikariyagi nirvahisuvudu nyatoda guruyagirabeke embudara bagge charchisuttade. | False | The cover story debates whether NATO's goal should be to carve up Kosovo or administer it as a protectorate. | It discusses whether the story of the mask should be heard or carried out as a protective authority, and that it must be treated as a serious matter. | 0.30301344 | False | The cover story discusses whether NATO's goal should be to carve up Kosovo or to administer it as a protectorate. | 0.97739345 | 0.6743800083528138 | False |

| xnli-test-2767 | premise | ಒಮ್ಮೆ ಈ ನಿರ್ಧಾರಗಳನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಂಡ ನಂತರ, CIO ಸಂಸ್ಥೆಯು ಸಂಪನ್ಮೂಲಗಳ ಸಮರ್ಥ ಹಂಚಿಕೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅದರ ಜವಾಬ್ದಾರಿಗಳ ದೈನಂದಿನ ಕಾರ್ಯನಿರ್ವಹಣೆಯ ಮೂಲಕ ಪರಿಣಾಮಕಾರಿ, ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯಾತ್ಮಕ ಬೆಂಬಲವನ್ನು ಒದಗಿಸಬೇಕು. | Omme ee nirdharagalannu thegedukonda nanthara, CIO samstheyu sampamoolagala samartha hanchike mattu adara javabdarigala dainadina karyanirvahaneya moolaka parinamakari, prathikriyathmaka bembalavannu odagisabeku. | False | Once these decisions are made, the CIO organization must provide effective, responsive support through efficient allocation of resources and the day-to-day execution of its responsibilities. | After taking these decisions, the CIO organization must read the result‑oriented, responsive impact through the daily execution by its responsible personnel. | 0.7322971 | False | Once these decisions are made, the CIO organization must provide effective, responsive support through efficient allocation of resources and daily execution of its responsibilities. | 0.99459183 | 0.2622947321609497 | False |
| xnli-test-2767 | hypothesis | ಸಿಐಒ ಸಂಪನ್ಮೂಲ ಹಂಚಿಕೆ ನಿರ್ಧಾರಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಭಾಗಿಯಾಗಿಲ್ಲ. | CIO sampanmoola hanchike nirdharagalalli bhagiyagilla. | False | The CIO is not involved in resource allocation decisions. | The CIO’s basic decisions are not lucky. | 0.3098896 | False | The CIO is not involved in resource allocation decisions. | 0.9999999 | 0.6901102807907105 | False |
| xnli-test-3332 | premise | ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ನನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವ ಎಲ್ಲ ಭರವಸೆಗಳು ಮಸುಕಾಗುತ್ತಿದ್ದಂತೆ, ಆಡಳಿತದ ವಿರೋಧಿಗಳಿಗೆ ಗುಪ್ತ ನೆರವು ನೀಡುವ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಚರ್ಚೆಯು ಪುನರುಜ್ಜೀವನಗೊಂಡಿತು. | talibannannu sthalantarisuva ella bharavasegalu masukaguttiddante, adalatada virodhigalude gupta neravu niduva bagge carcheyu punarujjivanagontitu. | False | As all hope in moving the Taliban faded, debate revived about giving covert assistance to the regime's opponents. | All the promises to relocate the student seem to have collapsed, and the debate about the secret timing of the court’s opponents has been revived. | 0.3760692 | False | As promises to relocate the Taliban faded, the discussion about covertly supporting the government's opponents was revived. | 0.852725 | 0.4766558289916992 | False |
| xnli-test-3332 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ತಾಲಿಬಾನ್ನನ್ನು ಸ್ಥಳಾಂತರಿಸುವ ಭರವಸೆಯನ್ನು ಬಿಟ್ಟುಕೊಡಲು ನಿರಾಕರಿಸಿದರು ಮತ್ತು ಅದನ್ನು ಏಕಾಂಗಿಯಾಗಿ ಮಾಡಲು ಒಪ್ಪಿಕೊಂಡರು. | avaru talibannannu sthalantarisuva bharavaseyanu bittukodalu nirakarisidaru mattu adanu ekangiyagi madalu oppukondaru. | False | They refused to give up hope in moving the Taliban and were in agreement about doing it alone. | They refused to keep the promise of relocating the student and decided to do it on their own. | 0.2848645 | False | They refused to abandon the promise to relocate the Taliban and agreed to do it alone. | 0.8129275 | 0.528062984512329 | False |
| xnli-test-127 | premise | ಇದು 30 ಅಥವಾ 40 U2 ವಿಮಾನಗಳು, ಮತ್ತು ನಾವು ತರಬೇತಿ ಆರಂಭಿಸಿದ್ದೆವು ಚೀನಾದ ಪೈಲಟ್ಗಳಿಗೆ, ಬ್ರಿಟಿಷ್ ಪೈಲಟ್ಗಳಿಗೆ, ಜಗತ್ತಿನಾದ್ಯಂತ, ನಾವು ಪಾಲುದಾರರಾಗಿದ್ದೆವು. | idu 30 athava 40 U2 vimanagalu, mattu navu tarabeti arambhiddevu chinada pilotgalage, british pilotgalage, jagattinadyanta, navu paludararagiddevu. | False | It's 30 or 40 U2 aircraft, and we'd started the training Chinese pilots, British pilots in them, just all over the, the world that we were allies with. | These are 30 or 40 U‑2 aircraft, and we have begun training Chinese pilots, British pilots, and pilots from around the world, and we have been very successful. | 0.8262116 | False | These are 30 or 40 U2 aircraft, and we began training Chinese pilots, British pilots, worldwide, we were partners. | 0.87542707 | 0.04921546727981563 | False |
| xnli-test-127 | hypothesis | ನಾವು ಬ್ರಿಟಿಷರೊಂದಿಗೆ 5 ವಾರಗಳ ಕಾಲ ತರಬೇತಿ ಪಡೆದಿದ್ದೆವು. | navu britisharondige 5 varagala kala tarabheti padediddevu. | False | We trained for 5 weeks with the British. | We were under the British for five years. | 0.6376542 | False | We received training with the British for five weeks. | 0.9414834 | 0.3038291784103394 | False |
| xnli-test-677 | premise | ಇದು ವಾಷಿಂಗ್ಟನ್ ನಡುವೆ ಹೋಗುತ್ತದೆ-ಅವರು ಸೋಲಿಲ್ಲದ ಮತ್ತು ಬಫಲೋ ನ್ಯೂ ಆರ್ಲಿಯನ್ಸ್ ಮತ್ತು ಚಿಕಾಗೊ ಏಕೆಂದರೆ ಚಿಕಾಗೊ ಕೇವಲ ಎರಡು ಬಾರಿ ಸೋತಿದೆ ಮತ್ತು ಅವುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಒಂದು ಬಫಲೋಗೆ ಮಾತ್ರ ಸೋತಿದೆ | idu washingtan naduve hoguttade-avaru soliladata mattu baphalo nyu orliyanas mattu chikago ekendare chikago kevala eradu bari sotide mattu avugalalli ondu baphaloge matra sotide | False | it's pretty much going between Washington they're undefeated and uh Buffalo New Orleans and Chicago because Chicago's only lost twice and one of those was to Buffalo | It goes through the middle of Washington— they said, and in Boston you’re something, and in Chicago, actually, Chicago only has two instances, and among them only one instance is present. | 0.3907068 | False | It goes between Washington— they are undefeated, and Buffalo, New Orleans and Chicago, because Chicago has only lost twice and one of those losses was only to Buffalo. | 0.8016896 | 0.41098282478637693 | False |

| xnli-test-1373 | hypothesis | ತುಗೆಂದಾಟ್ ಮನೆಯ ಮುಖ್ಯ ವಾಸಸ್ಥಾನವನ್ನು ಅಸಹ್ಯವೆಂದು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ. | thugendaat maneya mukhya vaassthaanavannu asahyavendu pariganisalagide. | False | The main living space of the Tugendhat House is considered an abomination. | The main resident of the house is considered intolerable. | 0.6153468 | False | The main residence of the Tugendat house is considered unpleasant. | 0.7771494 | 0.16180257925338748 | False |

| xnli-test-4448 | premise | ನಿಮ್ಮ ಅನೇಕ ಸಲಹೆಗಳು ಮೋಜಿನ ಮತ್ತು ಕ್ರೂರವಾದರೂ, ಕಾನೂನುಬಾಹಿರವಲ್ಲ ಆದರೆ ಕೇವಲ ಅಸಂಭವವಾಗಿರುವ ಕ್ರಿಯೆಗಳನ್ನು ಒಳಗೊಂಡಿದ್ದವು (ಕೋತಿ ಕೋಣೆಯಲ್ಲಿರುವಾಗ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ಪುರುಷರು ಅದನ್ನು ಎತ್ತುವುದಕ್ಕೆ ತುಂಬಾ ಹೆದರುತ್ತಾರೆ. | nimma aneka salahegalu mojina mattu krooravadaru, kanunubahiralalla adare kevala asambhavavagirue kriyakalgalannu olagondiddavu (kothi koneyalliruvaga hechina purusaru adanu ettuvudakke tumba hedaruttare. | False | Many of your suggestions involved actions that, while amusing and cruel, are not illegal but merely unlikely (Most men would be too scared to lift it while the monkey was in the room.) | Your many suggestions are amusing and cruel, not lawful but only impossible actions we have kept (when the rabbit is at the end, the big men are very eager to catch it) | 0.53599846 | False | Your many suggestions were fun and cruel, not illegal but involving actions that are merely impossible (most men are too scared to lift it when the monkey is in the room). | 0.8452789 | 0.3092804587431336 | False |
| xnli-test-4448 | hypothesis | ನಿಮ್ಮ ನಡವಳಿಕೆ ಕಾನೂನುಬಾಹಿರ. | nimma nadavalike kaanunubaahira. | False | Your actions are outright illegal. | Your behavior is illegal. | 0.830589 | False | Your behavior is illegal. | 0.830589 | -3.589630148681522e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-3669 | premise | ನೀವು ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಸುವ ಹೊಸ ಸಾಧ್ಯತೆಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಯಾವುದೇ ತಾಯಿಯಂತೆ ವಾಂಡಾಗೂ ತಿಳಿದಿದೆ, ಅದನ್ನು ತಿಳಿದುಕೊಳ್ಳಲು ಅವಳು ಅದ್ಭುತ ವಿಷಯ ಎಂದು ಕರೆಯುತ್ತಾಳೆ. | neevu srustisuva hosa sadhyategala bagge yavde tayiyante vandagu tilide, adanu tilikollalu avalu adbhuta vishaya endu kareyuttale. | False | Wanda is as aware as any mother can be about the new possibilities you create, which she calls a wonderful thing to know. | You have heard about the new possibilities you are creating, and she calls them a wonderful thing to learn about. | 0.6007925 | False | Even a mother knows about the new possibilities you create, and to understand it she calls it a wonderful thing. | 0.6961265 | 0.09533402063369756 | False |
| xnli-test-3669 | hypothesis | ವೆಂಕಟಯ್ಯನಿಗೆ ಮಕ್ಕಳಿಲ್ಲ. | Venkatayyanige makkalilla. | False | Wanda does not have any children. | Venkatayya has no children. | 0.6207497 | False | Venkataiah has no children. | 0.5831114 | -0.03763829462738033 | False |
| xnli-test-2155 | premise | ಆಗಸ್ಟ್ ತಿಂಗಳ ದಾಖಲೆ-ಉರುಳುವ ಸ್ಪರ್ಧೆಗಳಿಗೆ ಹೆಸರುವಾಸಿಯಾದ ಸ್ಕ್ವಾಮಿಶ್ ಪಟ್ಟಣವು ಗರಿಬಾಲ್ಡಿ ಪ್ರಾಂತ್ಯದ ಉದ್ಯಾನವನಕ್ಕೆ ಚಾರಣ ಪ್ರವಾಸಗಳಿಗೆ ಉಪಯುಕ್ತ ನೆಲೆಯನ್ನು ಮಾಡಿಕೊಡುತ್ತದೆ. | August tingala dakhale-uruluva spardhegalige hesaruvasiyada squamish pattanavu garibaldi prantyada udyanavanake charana pravasagalige upayukta neleyannu madikoduttade. | False | Famous for its August log-rolling contests, the town of Squamish makes a useful base for hiking tours into Garibaldi Provincial Park. | In August, the competition of the shining stars is called Squamish, and the city of Garibaldi in the province provides suitable land for the garden’s travel routes. | 0.67744625 | False | The town of Squamish, renowned for its record‑breaking August competitions, provides a suitable base for trekking trips to the Garibaldi region park. | 0.8413234 | 0.16387712570190427 | False |
| xnli-test-2155 | hypothesis | ಸ್ಕ್ವಾಮಿಷ್ ಲಾಗ್-ರೋಲಿಂಗ್ ಸ್ಪರ್ಧೆಗಳಿಗೆ ಹೆಸರುವಾಸಿಯಾಗಿದೆ. | Squamish log-rolling spardhegalige hesaruvasiyagide. | False | Squamish is famous for log-rolling contests. | Squamish log-rolling competition needs a name. | 0.8171896 | False | Squamish is famous for log-rolling competitions. | 0.9697585 | 0.15256891058959965 | False |
| xnli-test-4744 | premise | ಶ್ರೀ ಕಪ್ಲಾನ್ನ ಪೀಳಿಗೆಯು ಬಹುಮಟ್ಟಿಗೆ ಸತ್ತುಹೋಗಿದೆ, ಮತ್ತು ಅದರ ಸಂತತಿಯು ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ನೀರಾಗಿದೆ. | Shri kaplanna peeligeyu bahumattige sattuhogide, mattu adara santatiyu amerikanneeraagide. | False | Mr. Kaplan's generation has largely died off, and its progeny have become Americanized. | Mr. Kaplana’s peel has become very popular, and its offspring have become American. | 0.44407466 | False | The Kaplan family line has largely died out, and its descendants have become American. | 0.8825999 | 0.43852523023208617 | False |
| xnli-test-4744 | hypothesis | ಕಪ್ಲಾನ್ನ ಪೀಳಿಗೆಯ ಎಲ್ಲರೂ ಇಂದಿಗೂ ಬದುಕುತ್ತಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | kaplanna peeligeya ellaru indigu badukuttiddare. | False | All of Mr Kaplan's generation still lives today. | All the people of Kaplanna are living here. | 0.59014857 | False | All members of the Kaplan generation are still alive today. | 0.9173187 | 0.3271701317440796 | False |
| xnli-test-1406 | premise | ಮೆಕ್ಸಿಕನ್ ಕಲಾವಿದ ಮತ್ತು ಮುದ್ರಕ ಜೋಸ್ ಗ್ವಾಡಲೂಪೆ ಪೋಸಾಡಾ ಹತ್ತೊಂಬತ್ತನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ಕೊನೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ಈ ರಜಾದಿನಕ್ಕೆ ಹೊಂದಿಕೊಳ್ಳಲು ಕಲವೆರಾಗಳನ್ನು ಚಿತ್ರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಾರಂಭಿಸಿದರು. | Meksikan kalavid mattu mudraka Jose Gwadalupe Posada hattonbattane shatamanada koneyalli ee rajadinakke hondikollalu kalaveragalannu chitrisalu prararambisidaru. | False | The Mexican artist and printmaker Jose Guadalupe Posada started drawing calaveras during the late nineteenth century to coincide with this holiday. | Mexican artist and printmaker José Guadalupe Posada, at the end of the 19th century, began to draw scenes to glorify this kingdom. | 0.689354 | False | Mexican artist and printer José Guadalupe Posada began painting skulls to suit this holiday at the end of the 19th century. | 0.7602522 | 0.07089817771530149 | False |
| xnli-test-1406 | hypothesis | ಹತ್ತೊಂಬತ್ತನೇ ಶತಮಾನದ ಕೊನೆಯಲ್ಲಿ ರಜಾದಿನದಲ್ಲಿದ್ದಾಗ ಜೋಸ್ ಗ್ವಾಡಲೂಪೆ ಪೋಸಾಡಾ ತಲೆಬುರುಡೆಗಳು ಅಥವಾ ಕಲವೆರಾಸ್ಗಳನ್ನು ಚಿತ್ರಿಸಲು ಪ್ರಾರಂಭಿಸಿದರು. | hattonmbatnene shatamanda koneyalli rajadinadalliddaga joses gwadalupe posada taleburudegalu athava kalaverasgalannu chitrisalu prarambhisi daru. | False | While on holiday in the late nineteenth century Jose Guadalupe Posada started to draw skulls or calaveras. | When the hundred thousand are in the final stage of the kingdom, Joseph Guadalupe Posada's students or their mixtures begin to draw them and start the work. | 0.45976248 | False | At the end of the 19th century, while on vacation, José Guadalupe Posada began to depict skulls or calaveras. | 0.93080497 | 0.47104248788024905 | False |
| xnli-test-119 | premise | ನನ್ನ ಅಜ್ಜಿ ತನ್ನ ಬೆಳವಣಿಗೆಯ ವರ್ಷಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನನಗೆ ಅನೇಕ ವಿಭಿನ್ನ ಕಥೆಗಳನ್ನು ಹೇಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದರು, ವಿಶೇಷವಾಗಿ, ಆಹ್, ಅವಳು ತನ್ನ ಕುಟುಂಬದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಮಾತನಾಡುತ್ತಿದ್ದಳು, ಮತ್ತು ಆ ಸಮಯದಲ್ಲಿ ಅದು ಹೇಗಿತ್ತು. | nanna ajji tanna belavanigeya varshagala bagge nanage aneka vibhinna kathegalanu heluttiddaru, visheshavagi, ah, avalu tanna kutumbada bagge matanaduttiddalu, mattu a samayadalli adu hegittu. | False | My grandmother used to tell me a lot of different stories about her growing-up years and, uh, especially, uh, she used to talk about her family, and what it was like during those times. | My grandmother told me many different stories about her younger years, especially about her family, and that’s how it was back then. | 0.83752906 | False | My grandmother used to tell me many different stories about her growing-up years, especially, ah, she was talking about her family, and how it was at that time. | 0.8908751 | 0.05334604108947749 | False |
| xnli-test-119 | hypothesis | "" "ನನ್ನ ಅಜ್ಜಿ ಅವರು ಬೆಳೆದ ಸಮಯದಲ್ಲಿ ಅವರ ಕುಟುಂಬದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಸಾಕಷ್ಟು ವಿಷಯಗಳನ್ನು ನನಗೆ ಹೇಳಿದರು." | nanna ajji avru beleda samayadalli avra kutumbada bagge sashtu vishayagalu nanage helidru. | False | My grandmother told me lots of stuff about her family during the time that she was growing up. | My grandmother told me many things about her family when she was alive. | 0.94362414 | False | My grandmother told me many things about her family when she was growing up. | 0.9469217 | 0.0032975661996460515 | False |
| xnli-test-2880 | premise | ಈ ಗುಂಪು ಈ ಹಿಂದೆ ಚರ್ಚಿಸಲಾದ ಮತ್ತು/ಅಥವಾ ಅನುಷ್ಠಾನಗೊಳಿಸಲಾದ ಉಪಕ್ರಮಗಳ ಸ್ಥಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಚರ್ಚಿಸಲು ಮತ್ತು ಪ್ರಸ್ತುತ ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಸಂಭಾವ್ಯ ಉಪಕ್ರಮಗಳನ್ನು ಪ್ರಸ್ತಾಪಿಸಲು ಮತ್ತು ಚರ್ಚಿಸಲು ಮಾಸಿಕ ಸಭೆ ನಡೆಸುತ್ತದೆ. | ee gumpu ee hinde charchisalada mattu/athava anushthanagolisalada upakramagala sthitiyannu charchisalu mattu prastuta samseyagalu mattu sambhavya upakramagalannu prastapisalu mattu charchisalu masika sabhe nadesuttade. | False | The group meets monthly to discuss the status of previously discussed and/or implemented initiatives and to propose and discuss current problems and potential initiatives. | This group holds a monthly meeting to discuss the status of initiatives that have not been previously discussed or implemented, to examine current problems, propose possible initiatives, and discuss them. | 0.9045957 | False | This group holds a monthly meeting to discuss the status of initiatives previously discussed and/or implemented, and to propose and discuss current issues and possible initiatives. | 0.9277693 | 0.023173603321838376 | False |
| xnli-test-2880 | hypothesis | ಅನೇಕ ಗುಂಪಿನ ಸದಸ್ಯರು ಮಾಸಿಕ ಸಭೆಗಳು ಅನಗತ್ಯ ಎಂದು ಭಾವಿಸುತ್ತಾರೆ. | aneka gumpina sadasyaru masika sabhegalu anagatya endu bhavisuttare. | False | Many group members think that monthly meetings are unnecessary. | Members of various groups consider the monthly meetings to be uncertain. | 0.64230424 | False | Many group members consider monthly meetings unnecessary. | 0.96642196 | 0.3241177217843628 | False |
| xnli-test-4738 | premise | "" "ಈ ವಿವರಣೆಯಿಲ್ಲದೆ, ಫ್ರೆಂಚ್ ಸರ್ನೋಮ್ನಿಂದ ಅಡ್ಡಹೆಸರು ಎಂಬ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯು ನನಗೆ ಬಹಳ ಕಡಿಮೆ ಆಸಕ್ತಿದಾಯಕವಾಗಿದೆ." | Ii vivaraneyillade, french surnomninda addahesaru emba mahitiyu nanage bahal kadime asaktidayakavide. | False | 'Without this explanation, the information that surname is from French surnom seems to me to be of very little interest. | Without this detail, the fact that they are called by a French surname is very little known to me. | 0.681599 | False | Without this description, the information that it is a French surname is of very little interest to me. | 0.84096575 | 0.159366747833252 | False |
| xnli-test-4738 | hypothesis | "ಫ್ರೆಂಚ್ ಪದ" "ಸರ್ನಮ್" "ಯಾವುದೇ ವಿವರಣೆಯನ್ನು ಲೆಕ್ಕಿಸದೆ ಸಂಪೂರ್ಣವಾಗಿ ಗಮನಾರ್ಹವಲ್ಲ." | french pada "surname" yavude vivaraneyannu lekkisade sampurnavagi gamanarhavalla. | False | The French word Surnom meaning surname is purely unremarkable regardless of any explanation. | The French term “surname” is not entirely appropriate without any description. | 0.61742127 | False | French word surname is not entirely noteworthy without any explanation. | 0.7077012 | 0.09027993620727537 | False |
| xnli-test-2493 | premise | ವಸ್ತುಸಂಗ್ರಹಾಲಯಗಳು ಅದ್ಭುತವಾಗಿ ನಿರ್ಮಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟಿವೆ ಮತ್ತು ಬಹುತೇಕ ವಸ್ತುಸಂಗ್ರಹಾಲಯಗಳು ಕರಪತ್ರಗಳನ್ನು (ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯವಾಗಿ ಜರ್ಮನ್ ಮತ್ತು ಫ್ರೆಂಚ್ ಭಾಷೆಗಳಲ್ಲಿ) ಪ್ರದರ್ಶನದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ವಿವರವಾದ ಮಾಹಿತಿಯನ್ನು ಒದಗಿಸುತ್ತವೆ ಹಣ ಪಾವತಿಸಲು ನೀವು ಪ್ರಾಮಾಣಿಕತೆಯ ಪೆಟ್ಟಿಗೆಗಳನ್ನು ಕಂಡುಕೊಳ್ಳುವಿರಿ. | vastusamgrahalayagalu adbhutavagi nirmisalpattive mattu bahuteka vastusamgrahalayagalu karapatratagalannu (samanyavagi german mattu french bhashegalalli) pradarshanada bagge vivaravada mahitiyannu odagisuttave hana pavatisalu neevu pramanikateya pettigegalannu kandukolluviri. | False | Museums are superbly laid out, and most provide leaflets (usually in German, but often in English and French) with detailed information about the exhibits; you will find honesty boxes for payment freely dotted about. | Libraries have been wonderfully built and many of them provide detailed information about the rights (usually in German and French) for the exhibition; to read them you should locate the authenticity tags. | 0.60286194 | False | Museums are wonderfully built, and most museums provide detailed information about the exhibits (usually in German and French); you will find authenticity boxes to pay the money. | 0.8352529 | 0.23239094105010982 | False |
| xnli-test-2493 | hypothesis | ವಸ್ತುಸಂಗ್ರಹಾಲಯಗಳು ಕಳಪೆ ಮಟ್ಟದಲ್ಲಿವೆ. | vastusangrahalayagalu kalpe mattadallive. | False | Museums are laid out poorly. | We are talking about museums. | 0.65720856 | False | Museums are in a poor state. | 0.7453239 | 0.088115336408081 | False |
| xnli-test-1794 | hypothesis | ಅವನು ಮಾತನಾಡಿ, ಲಾರ್ಡ್ ಜೂಲಿಯನ್ ತನ್ನ ಸಿಂಹವನ್ನು ಎತ್ತಿದನು. | avanu matanadi, lord julian thanna simhavannu ettidanu. | False | He spoke and Lord Julian raised his lion. | He said Lord Julian killed his lion. | 0.753569 | False | He spoke, Lord Julian lifted his lion. | 0.9677742 | 0.21420521236038204 | False |
| xnli-test-3339 | premise | ನಾವು ಕೆಎಸ್ಎಂ ಅನ್ನು ಇಂದು ಅಲ್ ಖೈದಾಗೆ ಹೋಲಿಸಿದರೂ, 9/11ರ ಮೊದಲು ಹೀಗಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ. | navu ksm annu indu al khaidaage holisidaru, 9/11ra modalu hegiralilla. | False | Although we readily equate KSM with al Qaeda today, this was not the case before 9/11. | We killed KSM today for eating, and there was no such thing before 9/11. | 0.6696142 | False | Even though we compared KSM to Al Khayda today, it wasn't like this before 9/11. | 0.8769552 | 0.2073410111625671 | False |
| xnli-test-3339 | hypothesis | ಯಾರೂ ಅದನ್ನು ಅರಿತಿರದಿದ್ದರೂ, ಕೆಎಸ್ಎಂ ಯಾವಾಗಲೂ ಅಲ್ ಖೈದಾದೊಂದಿಗೆ ಸಂಪರ್ಕ ಹೊಂದಿತ್ತು. | yaaru adanu aritiradidduroo, ksm yaavaagaloo al khaidaadondige samparka hondittu. | False | Although no one realized it, KSM was always connected with al Qaeda. | Whoever knew him, KSM always kept in touch with Al Khidaadondige. | 0.7115058 | False | Even though no one understood it, KSM always had contact with Al Khayda. | 0.7573189 | 0.045813113936615024 | False |
| xnli-test-4356 | hypothesis | ಡೊನಾಲ್ಡ್ ಟ್ರಂಪ್ಗೆ ನೃತ್ಯ ಮಾಡಲು ಬರುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | donald trumpge nruthya maadalu baruvudilla. | False | Donald Trump cannot dance. | Donald Trump will not come to dance. | 0.78487116 | False | Donald Trump does not come to dance. | 0.8365133 | 0.05164212086853026 | False |
| xnli-test-365 | premise | ಆಹ್, ನಾನು ಇನ್ನೂ ಒಂಬತ್ತು ಎರಡು ಎರಡು ಮಾತ್ರ ನಿಯಂತ್ರಕಕ್ಕೆ ಇಂಜೆಕ್ಷನ್ ಹಾಕಿದ್ದೆ. | aah, naanu innu ombattu eradu eradu maatra niyantrakakke injekshan haakidde. | False | Uh, I was still the one and only nine two-two that had ever set the injection on the regulator. | Ah, I still gave an injection of 108‑2‑2 units for control. | 0.48469603 | False | Ah, I only gave the controller an injection at 9:22. | 0.5063329 | 0.021636844298095703 | False |
| xnli-test-365 | hypothesis | ನಾನು ಯಾವತ್ತೂ 922 ವರ್ಷದವನಾಗಿರಲಿಲ್ಲ. | naanu yaavattuu 922 varshadavanagiralilla. | False | I was never a 922. | I am not 922 years old. | 0.553911 | False | I have never been 922 years old. | 0.638897 | 0.08498600174331661 | False |
| xnli-test-3312 | premise | ಗುಪ್ತಚರ ಮತ್ತು ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆ ನಿಗ್ರಹಕ್ಕೆ ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ನ ಮೇಲ್ವಿಚಾರಣೆ ಈಗ ನಿಷ್ಕ್ರಿಯವಾಗಿದೆ. | guptachara mattu bhayotpadane nigrahakke congressna melvicharane iga nishkriyavagide. | False | Congressional oversight for intelligence-and counterterrorism-is now dysfunctional. | The Congress’s deliberation on covert operations and counter‑terrorism is now inactive. | 0.5075788 | False | Congress's oversight of intelligence and terrorism control is now inactive. | 0.64652544 | 0.13894664260025025 | False |
| xnli-test-3312 | hypothesis | ಕಾಂಗ್ರೆಸ್ ಯಾವತ್ತೂ ಗುಪ್ತಚರ ಅಥವಾ ಭಯೋತ್ಪಾದನೆ ನಿಗ್ರಹದ ಮೇಲ್ವಿಚಾರಣೆ ಮಾಡಿಲ್ಲ. | Congress yavattu guptachara athava bhayotpadane nigrahada melvicharane madilla. | False | Congress has never had oversight for either intelligence or counterterrorism. | Congress has never examined the control of secrecy or intimidation. | 0.77336705 | False | Congress has never overseen intelligence or counter‑terrorism. | 0.91733384 | 0.1439667913238526 | False |
| xnli-test-4698 | premise | "ಇದಕ್ಕೆ ಉತ್ತರಿಸಿದ ಪಿಯಾನೋವಾದಕ ಲಿಲ್ ಆರ್ಮ್ಸ್ಟ್ರಾಂಗ್," "ಅದು 'ಮಸ್ಕ್ರಾಟ್ ರಾಂಬ್ಲೆ'" ಅದು ಸರಿ ಅಲ್ಲವೇ, ರೆಡ್? | Idakke uttrisida pianovadaka lil armstrong, "adu 'muscrat ramble'" adu sari allave, red? | False | Lil Armstrong, who was the pianist for the session, improvised the answer,  That's named `Muskrat Ramble'; isn't that right, Red? | The pianist who replied to this, Lil Armstrong, “that ‘muscrat ramble’”—is that correct, right? | 0.7744024 | False | The pianist who answered this, Lil Armstrong, "it's 'Mascrat Rumble'," that's right, Red? | 0.71843314 | -0.05596925829162602 | False |
| xnli-test-4698 | hypothesis | ಪಿಯಾನೋವಾದಕ ಲಿಲ್ ಆರ್ಮ್ಸ್ಟ್ರಾಂಗ್, ಹಾಡುಗಳನ್ನು ಸುಧಾರಿಸಲು ಸಮರ್ಥರಾಗಿದ್ದರು. | piyanovadaka lil armsstrong, haadugalannu sudharisalu samartharagiddaru. | False | The pianist, Lil Armstrong, was able to improvise songs. | Piyanovadaka Lil Armsstrong was able to improve the songs. | 0.4859283 | False | Pianist Lil Armstrong was able to improve songs. | 0.8663414 | 0.38041311206741335 | False |
| xnli-test-4235 | hypothesis | ಮ್ಯಾಸಿಡೋನಿಯಾವನ್ನು ರಕ್ಷಿಸುವ ನ್ಯಾಟೋದ ಗುರಿಯನ್ನು ಕಿರು ಸಂಪಾದಕೀಯವು ದೃಢಪಡಿಸುತ್ತದೆ. | Macedoniannavannu rakshisuva Natoda guriyannu kiru sampadakiyavu drudhapadisuttade. | False | The short op-ed confirms NATO's goal to protect Macedonia. | The editor strongly emphasizes the theme of protecting Macedonians. | 0.6365886 | False | The short editorial affirms NATO's goal of protecting Macedonia. | 0.9212409 | 0.28465232578887945 | False |
| xnli-test-4731 | premise | [XVIIನೆಯ, 4] ಎಂಬ ಪದವು ಹುವೊಸ್ ಎಂಬ ಪದವನ್ನು ಅದರ ಸ್ಲ್ಯಾಂಗ್ ಅರ್ಥದಲ್ಲಿ ಸೂಚಿಸುತ್ತದೆ, 'ಚೆಂಡುಗಳು,' ಅಕ್ಷರಶಃ ಅರ್ಥದಲ್ಲಿ ಅಲ್ಲ, 'ಮೊಟ್ಟೆಗಳು.' | [XVIIne, 4] enba padavu huvos enba padavannu adara slang arthadalli suchisutte, 'chendugalu,' aksharasha arthadalli alla, 'mottegalu.' | False | [XVIII,4] refers to huevos in its slang sense, `balls,' not its literal sense, `eggs. | The word “XVIIne, 4” refers to the word “huvos” in its slang sense, “chendugalu,” not in its literal sense, “mottegalu.” | 0.6006535 | False | The term “XVIIನೆಯ, 4]” refers to the word “huvos” in its slang sense, “balls,” not in the literal sense, “eggs.” | 0.7140658 | 0.11341229017639165 | False |
| xnli-test-4731 | hypothesis | "" "ಹುವೊಸ್" "ಎಂದರೆ ಚೆಂಡುಗಳು ಎಂದರ್ಥವಲ್ಲ." | "Huvos" endare chendugalu endarthavalla. | False | Huevos does not mean balls. | "Huvos" does not mean "chendugalu". | 0.48809677 | False | Huvo's does not mean balls. | 0.73022115 | 0.24212438230560301 | False |
| xnli-test-3165 | premise | ಅವರು ಉತ್ತರ ಗೋಪುರದ ಮೆಝನೈನ್ ಲಾಬಿ ಮಟ್ಟದ ಸುತ್ತಲೂ ನಿಂತಿದ್ದರು, ಮೆಟ್ಟಿಲುಗಳನ್ನು ಬಿಟ್ಟುಹೋಗುವ ನಾಗರಿಕರಿಗೆ ಎ ಮತ್ತು ಸಿ ಅನ್ನು ಎಸ್ಕಲೇಟರ್ನಿಂದ ಕೆಳಗಿಳಿಸಲು ನಿರ್ದೇಶಿಸಿದರು. | avaru uttara gopurada mezanine lobby mattada suttalu nintiddaru, mettilugalannu bittohoguvana nagarikarige A mattu C annu escalatorninda klegalisalu nirdesisidaru. | False | They were positioned around the mezzanine lobby level of the North Tower, directing civilians leaving stairwells A and C to evacuate down an escalator to the concourse. | They were standing in the north gate’s mezzanine lobby and its surrounding area, and directed a resident who was dropping the stones to go from A and C using the escalator. | 0.7020291 | False | They were standing around the mezzanine lobby level of the North Tower, directing civilians leaving the stairs to take E and C down via the escalator. | 0.9185331 | 0.21650398677673344 | False |
| xnli-test-3165 | hypothesis | ಅವರು ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯ ಮಟ್ಟದಲ್ಲಿ ನಾಗರಿಕರಿಗೆ ಎಸ್ಕಲೇಟರ್ ಅನ್ನು ಕೊಂಡೊಯ್ಯುವಂತೆ ನಿರ್ದೇಶಿಸಿದರು. | avaru samanya mattadalli nagarikarige escalator annu kondoyyuvante nirdeshisidaru. | False | They directed civilians at the mezzanine level to take an escalator to the concourse. | They said that they would provide an escalator for the city residents in the usual conversation. | 0.5390599 | False | They instructed that the escalator be provided to civilians at a normal level. | 0.6494436 | 0.11038372640380856 | False |
| xnli-test-1921 | premise | "ಪೇತ್ರನೇ, ನಿನಗೆ ಏನಾದರೂ ಸಂಭವಿಸಿದರೆ," ಎಂದು ಅವರು ಹೇಳಿದರು. | Petrane, ninage enadaru sambhavisidare, endu avaru helidaru. | False | If anything should happen to you, Peter,  he said, as Blood was going over the side,  Colonel Bishop had better look to himself. | Petra, they said what would happen if something happens to you. | 0.28503525 | False | Peter, if something happens to you, he said. | 0.6360238 | 0.3509885694465637 | False |
| xnli-test-1921 | hypothesis | ಕರ್ನಲ್ ಬಿಷಪ್ ತನ್ನ ವ್ಯಕ್ತಿಯನ್ನು ಪರೀಕ್ಷಿಸುವುದನ್ನು ಖಚಿತಪಡಿಸಿಕೊಂಡರು. | kernel bishop tanna vyaktiyannu parikshisuvudannu khachitapadisukondaru. | False | Colonel Bishop made sure to check his person. | The bishop made sure that his own self was examined. | 0.68580943 | False | Colonel Bishop made sure to test his man. | 0.8406181 | 0.15480864394027705 | False |
| xnli-test-1190 | premise | ಬಂದೂಕುಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಇತರ ರೀತಿಯ ಶಸ್ತ್ರಾಸ್ತ್ರಗಳು ಈ ವರ್ಗಕ್ಕೆ ಸೇರುತ್ತವೆ. | bandookugalu mattu itara reetiya shastraastragalu ee vargakke seartve. | False | Guns and other forms of weaponry fall into this category. | Guns and other types of weapons belong to this category. | 0.96152294 | False | Guns and other types of weapons belong to this category. | 0.96152294 | -3.179016161425352e-09 | False |
| xnli-test-1190 | hypothesis | ಬಂದೂಕುಗಳು ಒಂದು ವರ್ಗದಲ್ಲಿ ಇಲ್ಲ. | bandookugalu ondu vargadalli illa. | False | Guns are not in a category. | Guns are not in one class. | 0.72160333 | False | Guns are not in one category. | 0.9433265 | 0.221723143236084 | False |
| xnli-test-1484 | premise | ಈ ಹಂತದಲ್ಲಿ, ಪಾದ್ರಿಯು ತನ್ನ ಕೈಯನ್ನು ಮಿಸ್ಸಾಲ್ನ ಮೇಲೆ ಇಟ್ಟು ಕಣ್ಮರೆಯಾಗುತ್ತಾನೆ. | ee hantadalli, padriyu tanna kaiyannu missalna mele ittu kanmareyaguttane. | False | At this point the priest places his hand on the missal and vanishes. | In this situation, the father puts his hand on the spoon and it starts to itch. | 0.22003193 | False | At this point, the priest places his hand on the missal and disappears. | 0.9826638 | 0.7626318802531433 | False |
| xnli-test-1484 | hypothesis | ಪಾದ್ರಿಯು ಪುಸ್ತಕವನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಂಡು ಬೆಚ್ಚಗಾಗಲು ಅದನ್ನು ಬೆಂಕಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಹಾಕುತ್ತಾನೆ. | paadriyu pustakavannu tegedukondu becchagaagalu adannu benkiyalli haakuttaane. | False | The priest takes the book and throws it into the fire to burn for warmth. | Father takes the book and puts it in the fire for the children. | 0.7426766 | False | The priest takes the book and puts it in the fire to warm it. | 0.9316879 | 0.18901129148330686 | False |
| xnli-test-188 | premise | ಪ್ರತಿಯೊಬ್ಬರೂ ಶ್ಯಾಂಪೇನ್ ಅನ್ನು ಪಡೆಯುತ್ತಾರೆ ಮತ್ತು ಕೆಲವು ಜನರು ಅದನ್ನು ಕುಡಿಯುವುದಿಲ್ಲ ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ಮಕ್ಕಳು ಏನು ಕುಡಿಯುತ್ತಾರೆ ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ ನಾವು ಈ ಎಲ್ಲಾ ಶ್ಯಾಂಪೇನ್ ಅನ್ನು ಕುಡಿಯುತ್ತಿದ್ದೆವು. | pratiyobbaru champagne annu padeyutare mattu kelu janaru adanu kudiyuvudilla haage makkalu enu kudiyuvudaru haage navu ee ella champagne annu kudiyuttiddevu. | False | Everybody gets champagne and some people don't drink it so what's left the kids drink so we were going around drinking all this champagne. | Everyone calls it champagne, and some people don’t drink it, just as children drink something, we have been drinking all this champagne. | 0.7725547 | False | Everyone receives champagne, and some people don’t drink it, so what do the children drink, and we ended up drinking all this champagne. | 0.86567867 | 0.09312396802215572 | False |
| xnli-test-188 | hypothesis | ಇಡೀ ಪಾರ್ಟಿಯು ಒಣಗಿತ್ತು ಮತ್ತು ಯಾವುದೇ ಆಲ್ಕೋಹಾಲ್ ನೀಡಲಾಗಲಿಲ್ಲ. | idhee partyu onagittu mattu yavude alcohol needalagilla. | False | The whole party was dry and no alcohol was served. | This party is over and there is no need for any alcohol. | 0.51426685 | False | The whole party was dry and no alcohol was served. | 1.0 | 0.48573314999999995 | False |
| xnli-test-937 | premise | huh-uh-uh ಇಳಿಜಾರು ಅಲ್ಲ ನಾವು ಅಡ್ಡ ದೇಶದ ಆಕಾಶವನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿದ್ದೇವೆ ಮತ್ತು | huh-uh-uh ilijaaru alla naavu adda deshaakashavannu hondiddeve mattu | False | huh-uh uh not downhill we do have cross country skies and | No, no, no, it's not Ilijaaru; we have found another country. | 0.085584044 | True | huh-uh-uh it's not a slope, we have the sky of a foreign land and | 0.5930766 | 0.5074925427233277 | False |
| xnli-test-937 | hypothesis | ನಮ್ಮಲ್ಲಿ ಐದು ಸೆಟ್ ಕ್ರಾಸ್ ಕಂಟ್ರಿ ಸ್ಕೀಯಿಗಳಿವೆ. | nammalli aidu set cross country skiyigalive. | False | We have five sets of cross country skiies. | We have five cross-country skiers. | 0.8063414 | False | We have five sets of cross-country skis. | 0.9618573 | 0.15551591887817384 | False |
| xnli-test-3773 | premise | 1914ರಿಂದ ಸಿವಿಕ್ ತನ್ನ ಅನನ್ಯತೆಯನ್ನು ಕಾಪಾಡಿಕೊಂಡು ಬಂದಿದೆ | 1914rinda civic tanna ananyateyannu kaapaadikondu bandide | False | Since 1914, Civic has maintained its uniqueness by staying true to its | Civic has been coming, protecting its own uniqueness, since 1914. | 0.8114488 | False | Since 1914, Civic has maintained its uniqueness. | 0.9277176 | 0.11626882609481815 | False |

| xnli-test-20 | premise | ಇದು ಫ್ಯಾನಿ ಫ್ಲೋನೊ, ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳು ಅಗಸ್ಟಾ, ಜಿಎನಲ್ಲಿ ಬೆಳೆದಳು, ಮತ್ತು ಅವಳು ತನ್ನ ಬಾಲ್ಯದ ಕೆಲವು ಕಥೆಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಮಾತನಾಡಲು ಹೋಗುತ್ತಾಳೆ. | idu fyani flono, mattu avalu agsta, jienalli beledalu, mattu avalu tanna balyada kelvu kathegala bagge matanadalu hoguttale. | False | This is Fannie Flono, and she grew up in Ag-- Augusta, GA, and she is going to talk about some stories from her childhood. | He is a funny guy, and she is angry, to be killed in life, and she goes to talk about her own past questions. | 0.39055508 | False | This is Fancy Flono, and she is Augusta, who grew up in GA, and she is going to talk about some stories from her childhood. | 0.9159651 | 0.5254100002612305 | False |

| xnli-test-2815 | premise | ಕೆಲವೊಂದು ನಿರ್ದಿಷ್ಟ ವಿನಾಯಿತಿಗಳನ್ನು ಹೊರತುಪಡಿಸಿ, ಪ್ರಯಾಣಿಕರಿಗೆ ಕೇವಲ $75 ಅಥವಾ ಅದಕ್ಕಿಂತಲೂ ಕಡಿಮೆ ಮೊತ್ತದ ಎಲ್ಲ ಖರ್ಚುಗಳನ್ನು ಪಟ್ಟಿಮಾಡಲು ಅನುಮತಿಸುವ ಒಂದು ಉದ್ಯೋಗಿ ಪ್ರಯಾಣ ಹಕ್ಕು ಕಾರ್ಯವಿಧಾನವನ್ನು ಜಾರಿಗೆ ತರುವ ಉದ್ದೇಶವನ್ನು ಒಂದು ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಯು ಹೊಂದಿತ್ತು. | kelavondu nirdishta vinayitigalannu horatupadisi, prayanikarige kevala $75 athava adakkintalu kadime mottada ella kharchugalannu pattimadalu anumatisuva ondu udyogi prayana hakku karyavidhananubhavannu jarige taruva uddeshavannu ondu ajensiyu hondittu. | False | One agency intended to implement an employee travel claim procedure allowing travelers, with certain exceptions, to merely list an aggregate amount of all expenses that individually cost $75 or less. | By eliminating certain specific restrictions, an agency has introduced a policy that allows travelers to claim all expenses up to a total of $75 or less as a business travel entitlement. | 0.7731413 | False | An agency intended to implement an employee travel entitlement program that, except for certain specific exemptions, allows travelers to list all expenses of $75 or less. | 0.8793914 | 0.10625007220382687 | False |
| xnli-test-2815 | hypothesis | ಈ ಕಲ್ಪನೆಯನ್ನು t = ಇತರ ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಗಳು ಉತ್ತಮವಾಗಿ ಸ್ವೀಕರಿಸಲಿಲ್ಲ. | ee kalpneyannu t = itara ejensigalu uttamavagi sveekarisalilla. | False | This idea was not well-received by t=other agencies. | This concept is not accepted well by other agencies. | 0.7956722 | False | This concept t = was not well received by other agencies. | 0.91985685 | 0.12418464633255 | False |

| xnli-test-3556 | premise | ಕಂಪ್ಯೂಟರ್ ಮತ್ತು ಮೋಡೆಮ್ ಹೊಂದಿರುವ ಯಾರಿಗಾದರೂ ನಮ್ಮ ನೆಲಕ್ಕೆ ಪ್ರವೇಶವನ್ನು ತೆರೆಯಲಾಗುತ್ತದೆ. | computer mattu modem hondiruva yarigadaru namma nelakke praveshavannu tereyalaguttade. | False | Access to our grounds will be opened to anyone with a computer and a modem. | Anyone who has a computer and modem can access our network. | 0.69680053 | False | Anyone who has a computer and a modem will be granted access to our land. | 0.70304686 | 0.006246328310699489 | False |

| xnli-test-4094 | premise | ಈ ಬಜೆಟ್ ಕೆಲವು ದೊಡ್ಡ-ಸಂದೇಹಾಸ್ಪದವಾಗಿದ್ದರೆ-ಸೂಚ್ಯವಾಗಿ ಮಾತ್ರ ಆಯ್ಕೆಗಳನ್ನು ಮಾಡುತ್ತದೆ. | ee budget kelavu dodda-sandehaaspadavagiddare-soochyavagi maatra aykegalannu maduttade. | False | This budget makes some big--if dubious--choices, if only implicitly. | If this budget has some major doubts, it merely raises questions as a notice. | 0.58501136 | False | If this budget is somewhat large and questionable, it only makes choices clearly. | 0.80013454 | 0.21512317960418703 | False |

| xnli-test-1101 | premise | ಮತ್ತೊಂದೆಡೆ, ಕಣಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಮೂರು ನಾಂಗ್ರಾವಿಟೇಶನಲ್ ಬಲಗಳು ಇನ್ನೂ ಸ್ಪಿನ್ ನೆಟ್ವರ್ಕ್ ಚಿತ್ರದಲ್ಲಿ ಸೇರಿಸಲ್ಪಟ್ಟಿಲ್ಲ. | mattondede, kanagalu mattu mooru nongravitational balagalu innu spin network chitradalli serisapattilla. | False | On the other hand, particles and the three nongravitational forces have yet to be incorporated into a spin network picture. | Once again, dreams and the three non-gravitational forces have still not been incorporated into the spin network picture. | 0.69297886 | False | On the other hand, particles and the three non-gravitational forces have not yet been added to the spin network diagram. | 0.89391863 | 0.20093977346099856 | False |
| xnli-test-1101 | hypothesis | ಸ್ಪಿನ್ ನೆಟ್ವರ್ಕ್ ಚಿತ್ರಣವು ಪೂರ್ಣಗೊಳ್ಳಲು ನಾನ್-ಗ್ರಾವಿಟೇಶನಲ್ ಬಲಗಳನ್ನು ಸಂಯೋಜಿಸಬೇಕು. | spin network chitranavu purnagollalu non graviteshonal balagalannu samyojisabeku. | False | The nongravitational forces must be incorporated in order for the spin network picture to be complete. | Spin network representation must combine non‑gravitational forces. | 0.80226994 | False | To complete the spin network diagram, non-gravitational forces must be incorporated. | 0.8477694 | 0.04549943961578373 | False |
| xnli-test-3020 | premise | ನಿಜವಾದ ಚೆಕ್ಪಾಯಿಂಟ್ ಸ್ಕ್ರೀನಿಂಗ್ಗೆ ಸೂಕ್ತವಾದ ಏಕೈಕ ಭದ್ರತಾ ಪದರಕ್ಕೆ ಸಂಬಂಧಿಸಿದಂತೆ ಅವುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಯಾವುದೂ ವಿಶಿಷ್ಟವಾಗಿಲ್ಲ. | nijavada check point screeningge suktavada ekika bhadrata padarake sambandhisidante avugalalli yavudu visistavagilla. | False | Nothing stands out about any of them with respect to the only security layer that was relevant to the actual  checkpoint screening. | If the genuine checkpoint screening is connected to the alleged issue, there is nothing special about them. | 0.5775395 | False | None of them is unique regarding the single security layer suitable for a real checkpoint screening. | 0.76276267 | 0.1852231657485962 | False |

| xnli-test-3875 | premise | ಅಂತಾರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಸಂದರ್ಶಕರ ರಾಷ್ಟ್ರೀಯ ಮಂಡಳಿ, ದಕ್ಷಿಣ ಆಫ್ರಿಕಾ ಇಂಟರ್ನ್ಶಿಪ್ ಪ್ರೋಗ್ರಾಂ ಮತ್ತು ಚೈನೀಸ್ ಮೆಡಿಕಲ್ ಇಂಟರ್ನ್ಶಿಪ್ಗಳ ಸ್ಥಳೀಯ ಆತಿಥೇಯ ಸಂಸ್ಥೆಯಾಗಿ, 1999ರಲ್ಲಿ ಕೇಂದ್ರವು 100 ಕ್ಕೂ ಹೆಚ್ಚು ಅತಿಥಿಗಳನ್ನು ಮಧ್ಯ ಇಂಡಿಯಾನಾಕ್ಕೆ ಸ್ವಾಗತಿಸಿತು. | antaraashtriya sandarshakara rashtriya mandali, dakshina aphrika intership progran mattu chainees medical intershipgala sthaliya atitheya samstheyaagi, 1999rali kendravu 100 kku metchu athithigalannu madhya indianakke swagatisitu. | False | As the local host organization for the National Council of International Visitors, the South Africa Internship Program and Chinese Medical Internships, in 1999 the Center welcomed over 100 guests to central Indiana. | International observers' national committee, South Africa internship program and Chinese medical internships as a local host organization, in 1999 the center welcomed over 100 guests to Central India. | 0.90748084 | False | The International Visitors' National Board, the South Africa Internship Program, and the local host organization for Chinese medical internships welcomed over 100 guests to Central India in 1999. | 0.8966298 | -0.010851029666748002 | False |
| xnli-test-3875 | hypothesis | ಇಂಡಿಯಾನಾಕ್ಕೆ ಅನೇಕ ಅತಿಥಿಗಳು ಆಗಮಿಸಿದ್ದರಿಂದ ಕೇಂದ್ರವು ಸಂತೋಷಗೊಂಡಿತು. | Indianakke aneka athithigalu agamissiddarinda kendravu santhoshagonditu. | False | The center was happy to have so many guests come to Indiana. | The center was very happy because many guests had arrived. | 0.8044335 | False | The center was delighted because many guests arrived in India. | 0.76464677 | -0.039786731430053734 | False |

His dominance was waiting for an answer for a moment. | 0.59805083 | 0.1479873927484131 | False |
| xnli-test-1798 | hypothesis | ಲಾರ್ಡ್ ತನ್ನ ಸಮಯವನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಳ್ಳುತ್ತಿದ್ದನು ಏಕೆಂದರೆ ಅವನು ತಮಾಷೆಯ ಉತ್ತರದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಯೋಚಿಸಬೇಕಿತ್ತು. | Lord tanna samayavannu tegedukolluttiddanu eke-endare avanu tamasheya uttarada bagge yochisabekittu. | False | The lord was only taking his time because he needed to think of a witty response. | Lord took his time because he had to think about the answer to the riddle. | 0.7529648 | False | Lord was taking his time because he had to think about a witty answer. | 0.88957036 | 0.13660555541534425 | False |
| xnli-test-1155 | premise | ಅದೇ ರೀತಿಯಾಗಿ, ಚಂಚಲವಾದ ಗ್ರಾಹಕರು, ಹಲವಾರು ಮಾರಾಟದ ಋತುಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ವಿಭಜಿತ ಮಾರುಕಟ್ಟೆಗಳೊಂದಿಗೆ ಜೊತೆಗೂಡಿದ ಒಟ್ಟಾರೆ ಅಪಾಯವು ಪ್ರಸ್ತುತ ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ ಚಿಲ್ಲರೆ ವ್ಯಾಪಾರಿಗಳು ಮತ್ತು ಉತ್ಪಾದಕರಿಗೆ ಒಂದು ಕಠಿಣ ಕ್ಷೇತ್ರವಾಗಿ ಪರಿಣಮಿಸಿದೆ. | adhe reethiyagi, chanchalavada grahakaru, halavaru maratada ruthugalu mattu vibhajita marukattegalondige jotegoodida ottare apayavu prasthuta amerikan chillare vyaparigalu mattu utpadakarige ondu kathina kshetravagi parinamiside. | False | By the same token, the overall risk associated with fickle consumers, numerous selling seasons, and segmented markets'along with fierce overseas competition'has currently made this a tough arena for American retailers and manufacturers. | Similarly, fickle customers, numerous seasonal trends and fragmented market segments have created a single crisis that has become a difficult area for American retailers and manufacturers. | 0.78484786 | False | Similarly, the overall risk associated with fickle customers, multiple sales seasons, and fragmented markets has become a tough area for current American retailers and manufacturers. | 0.9359958 | 0.1511479571844483 | False |
| xnli-test-1155 | hypothesis | 90% ಅಮೆರಿಕನ್ ಚಿಲ್ಲರೆ ವ್ಯಾಪಾರಿಗಳು ವಿಫಲರಾಗಿದ್ದಾರೆ. | 90% amerikan chillare vyaparigalu viphalaragiddare. | False | 90% of American retailers are failing. | 90% of American child traders have failed. | 0.7196803 | False | 90% of American retailers have failed. | 0.9625578 | 0.24287749266357417 | False |
| xnli-test-2267 | premise | ಅರ್ಬೋರೆಟಮ್ನಿಂದ ಒಂದು ಮೈಲಿಗಿಂತಲೂ ಕಡಿಮೆ ದೂರದಲ್ಲಿ, ರಸ್ತೆಯ ಬಲಬದಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಎರಡು ಚರ್ಚುಗಳನ್ನು ನೀವು ನೋಡುವಿರಿ. | arboretum ninda ondu mailigintalu kamee dooradalli, rosteya balabadiyalli eradu churchugalannu neevu noduviri. | False | Less than a mile past the Arboretum, you will find two churches on the right side of the road, backed by hundreds of white painted family tombs on the hillside. | From the arboretum, about a mile away, at the foot of the ridge, you can see two churches. | 0.64125764 | False | Less than a mile from the arboretum, on the right side of the road, you will see two churches. | 0.7675036 | 0.1262459791940308 | False |
| xnli-test-2267 | hypothesis | ರಸ್ತೆಯ ಬಲಬದಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ಎರಡು ಚರ್ಚುಗಳು ಕಂಡುಬರುತ್ತವೆ. | rastey balabadiyalli eradu churchugalu kandubartave. | False | Two churches can be found on the right side of the road. | You will see two churches on the road in the playground. | 0.6679988 | False | On the right side of the road, two churches can be seen. | 0.98033714 | 0.31233834294433593 | False |
| xnli-test-2800 | premise | ಭವಿಷ್ಯದ ದೃಷ್ಟಿಯಿಂದ, ಸುಮಾರು ಮೂರನೇ ಒಂದು ಭಾಗದಷ್ಟು ಪ್ರತಿಕ್ರಿಯೆ ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಗಳು ವಿನ್ಯಾಸ ಪರಾಮರ್ಶೆ ಕಾರ್ಯಗಳನ್ನು ಮತ್ತಷ್ಟು ಹೊರಗುತ್ತಿಗೆ ನೀಡುವ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಯೋಚಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದೇವೆ ಎಂದು ವರದಿ ಮಾಡಿವೆ. | bhavishyada drushtiyinda, sumaru murane ondu bhagadastu pratikriye ajencygalu vinyasa paramarshhe karyagalu mattashtu horaguttige niduva bagge yochisuttiddeve endu varodi madive. | False | Looking to the future, about onethird of the responding agencies reported that they are considering further outsourcing of design review functions. | From a future perspective, we are considering how agencies respond, design consultancy work, and also thinking about stepping out. | 0.6444388 | False | From a future perspective, about one-third of respondents reported that design consultancy firms are considering outsourcing design consultancy work further. | 0.7589258 | 0.11448699555511477 | False |
| xnli-test-2800 | hypothesis | ಯಾವುದೇ ಏಜೆನ್ಸಿಗಳು ಹೊರಗುತ್ತಿಗೆ ವಿನ್ಯಾಸ ಪರಾಮರ್ಶೆ ಕಾರ್ಯಗಳನ್ನು ಪರಿಗಣಿಸುತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ. | yavude aejensigalu horaguttige vinyasa paramarshe karyagalannu pariganisuttilla. | False | No agencies are considering outsourcing design review functions. | No agencies consider outsourcing design consultancy work. | 0.87238693 | False | No agencies are considering outsourcing design consultancy tasks. | 0.92030096 | 0.04791403054077148 | False |
| xnli-test-4305 | premise | ಅಷ್ಟುಮಾತ್ರವಲ್ಲದೆ, ನಮಗೆ ತಿಳಿದಿರುವಂತೆ, ಭೂಮಿಯ ಮೇಲೆ ಜೀವವು ಒಮ್ಮೆ ಮಾತ್ರ ಅಸ್ತಿತ್ವಕ್ಕೆ ಬಂದಿತು. | Ashtu matravallade, namage thilidiruvantha, bhoomiya mele jeevavu omme mathra asthithvake bandithu. | False | Moreover, as far as we know, life emerged here on earth only once. | And not only that, the life on earth that we know of has only a single existence. | 0.7907866 | False | Not only that, as we know, life on Earth came into existence only once. | 0.93833476 | 0.14754816305007934 | False |
| xnli-test-4305 | hypothesis | ಭೂಮಿಯ ಮೇಲೆ ಬದುಕು ಒಮ್ಮೆ ಮಾತ್ರ ಸೃಷ್ಟಿಯಾಯಿತು. | bhoomiya mele baduku omme matra srushtiyayitu. | False | Life on earth emerged only once. | Only one life has been created on the earth. | 0.8015247 | False | Life on Earth was created only once. | 0.91245294 | 0.11092823617248537 | False |
| xnli-test-4432 | premise | ಇವುಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ಯಾವುದೂ, ಐಷಾರಾಮಿ ವಸ್ತುಗಳಾಗಿದ್ದರೂ, ಈಗಿನ ಜನಪ್ರಿಯತೆಯನ್ನು ಹೊಂದಿರುವುದಿಲ್ಲ. | Ivugalalli yaavudoo, aishaaraami vastugalaagiddaru, eagina janapriyateyannu hondiruvudilla. | False | None of these has much current appeal, luxe notwithstanding. | None of them were anything; they were useless items and had no popularity. | 0.44989717 | False | None of these, even though they are luxury items, are popular now. | 0.7140936 | 0.2641964555455017 | False |
| xnli-test-4432 | hypothesis | ಇವುಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಈಗಿನ ಜನರಲ್ಲಿ ಸಾಕಷ್ಟು ಒಲವು ಇದೆ. | ivugala bagge egina janaralli sakashtu olavu ide. | False | There is much current appeal with these. | There is a great deal of affection among the people who have come here. | 0.33858204 | False | People nowadays have a lot of affection for these. | 0.74525166 | 0.4066696155786133 | False |
| xnli-test-4228 | premise | ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ಅದರ ಬಾಡಿಗೆ ನೀತಿಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ವಿಚಾರಿಸಲು ನಾನು ಯು-ಹೌಲ್ಗೆ ಕರೆ ಮಾಡಿದೆ. | iddre, adara badige nitigala bagge vicharisalu nanu u-haulge kare madide. | False | So, I called U-Haul to inquire about its rental policies. | If that's the case, I thought about its fence posts and filed a complaint with U‑Haul. | 0.5865679 | False | Therefore, I called U‑Haul to inquire about its rental policies. | 0.9167576 | 0.33018968361816403 | False |
| xnli-test-4228 | hypothesis | ಅವರ ಎರಡನೇ ಚಾಲಕ ನೀತಿಗಳ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಮಾಹಿತಿ ಪಡೆಯಲು ನಾನು ಯು-ಹಾಲ್ ಗೆ ಕರೆ ಮಾಡಿದ್ದೆ. | avara eradane chalaka nitigala bagge mahiti ಪಡೆಯಲು nanu u-hal ge kare madidde. | True | I called U-Haul to get information about their second driver policies. | I requested U‑Hal to get information about their second mobile numbers. | 0.39485958 | False | I called U-Haul to get information about their second driver policies. | 0.99999994 | 0.6051403603953552 | False |
| xnli-test-1339 | premise | ಸಮಾನತೆಯ ಬಗೆಗಿನ ಅಮೆರಿಕದ ದೃಷ್ಟಿಕೋನದ ವಿರೋಧಾಭಾಸವೆಂದರೆ, ಆರ್ಥಿಕ ಸಮಾನತೆ ಮತ್ತು ಸಂಪತ್ತಿನ ತಾರತಮ್ಯದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ನಮ್ಮ ಕಾಳಜಿಯಲ್ಲಿ ನಾವು ಯುರೋಪಿಯನ್ ಸಮಾಜಗಳನ್ನು ಅನುಸರಿಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದರೂ, ಸಮಾನತಾವಾದಿ ಚಿಂತನೆಯ ಇತರ ಕ್ಷೇತ್ರಗಳಲ್ಲಿ ನಾವು ಜಗತ್ತನ್ನು ಮುನ್ನಡೆಸುತ್ತಿದ್ದೇವೆ. | samanateya bagegina amerikada drustikonada virodhabhasavendare, arthika samanateye mattu sampattina taratamayada bagge namma kalajiyalli navu europosn samajagalannu anusarisuttiddaru, samanatavadi chintaneya itara kshetragalli navu jagattannu munnadesuttiddeve. | False | The paradox of the American approach toward equality is that though we trail European societies in our concern about economic equality and wealth discrimination, we lead the world in other areas of egalitarian thinking. | If the American view of equality is contradictory—economically equal and yet stratified by wealth—our culture has followed European societies, and in other areas of egalitarian thinking we are moving the world forward. | 0.9131589 | False | The paradox of America's view on equality is that while we follow European societies in our concern for economic equality and wealth disparity, we lead the world in other areas of egalitarian thinking. | 0.96859837 | 0.05543946578369141 | False |
| xnli-test-1339 | hypothesis | ಯುರೋಪಿಯನ್ ಸಮಾಜಗಳು ಆರ್ಥಿಕ ಸಮಾನತೆ ಮತ್ತು ಸಂಪತ್ತಿನ ತಾರತಮ್ಯದ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಕಾಳಜಿ ಹೊಂದಿವೆ. | Europiyan samajagalu arthika samanate mattu sampathina tharatamyada bagge kalaji hondive. | False | European societies are concerned about economic equality and wealth discrimination. | European societies have become aware of economic equality and wealth disparity. | 0.8450495 | False | European societies are concerned about economic equality and wealth disparity. | 0.94872224 | 0.10367274330902099 | False |
| xnli-test-4224 | premise | ಯಾವ ವಿಷಯಗಳು ಗರ್ಭಪಾತಕ್ಕೆ ಸಂಬಂಧಿಸಿವೆ ಎಂಬುದರ ಬಗ್ಗೆ ಹೆಚ್ಚಿನ ಚರ್ಚೆಗಳು ನಡೆಯುತ್ತವೆ. | yaava vishyagalu garbhapaatakke sambandhisive embudara bagge hechinad charchegalu nadeyuttave. | False | Slope vs.  Much of the debate turns on which issues are associated with abortion. | There are ongoing discussions about which topics are related to pregnancy. | 0.52746505 | False | There are many discussions about which issues are related to abortion. | 0.6990733 | 0.17160826466674806 | False |
| xnli-test-4224 | hypothesis | ಗರ್ಭಪಾತದ ಚರ್ಚೆ ಒಂದು ದೊಡ್ಡ ಸಮಸ್ಯೆಯಾಗಿದೆ. | garbhapatada charcha ondu dodda samasyeyagide. | False | The abortion debate is a big issue. | Discussion of pregnancy is a big problem. | 0.7357252 | False | Abortion is a major issue. | 0.9412321 | 0.20550688522796634 | False |
| xnli-test-4655 | premise | ಕೆಲವು ಹೆಸರುಗಳು ಆಕ್ಷೇಪಾರ್ಹವಾಗಿದ್ದರೂ ಅವುಗಳನ್ನು ಬದಲಾಯಿಸಲಾಗಿಲ್ಲ. | kelavu hesarugalu aksheparhavagiddaru avugalannu badalayisalagilla. | False | Some names, although they may be objectionable, are not changed. | Some names were offensive and could not be changed. | 0.72363985 | False | Although some names were objectionable, they were not changed. | 0.8341301 | 0.11049025835647586 | False |
| xnli-test-4655 | hypothesis | ಸಂಪ್ರದಾಯಕ್ಕೆ ಗೌರವ ತೋರಿಸಲು, ಮೂಲ ಹೆಸರುಗಳನ್ನು ಯಾವುದೇ ಬದಲಾವಣೆಗಳಿಲ್ಲದೆ ಉಪಯೋಗಿಸಬೇಕೆಂಬುದು ಆವಶ್ಯಕ. | sampradayakke gourava torisalu, mula hesarugalanu yavude badalavanegalillade upayogisabekembudu avasyaka. | False | Respect for tradition demands that the original names be used without changes. | To show respect for tradition, it is necessary to use the original names without any changes. | 0.91931075 | False | To honor tradition, it is essential to use the original names without any alterations. | 0.8952874 | -0.02402335547637935 | False |
| xnli-test-2411 | premise | ಎರಡು ಶತಮಾನಗಳ ಧಾರ್ಮಿಕ ಪಾಷಂಡತ್ವದ ನಂತರ, ಅಸೀಸಿಯ ಫ್ರಾನ್ಸಿಸ್ನಲ್ಲಿ (1182-1226) ಪರಿಪೂರ್ಣ ಮಿತ್ರನನ್ನು ಹುಡುಕುವ ಮೂಲಕ ಚರ್ಚ್ಗೆ ಆಧ್ಯಾತ್ಮಿಕ ನವೀಕರಣದ ಅಗತ್ಯವಿತ್ತು. | eradu shathamanagala dharmika pashandathvada nanthara, aseesiya francisnalli (1182-1226) paripurna mitrannu hudukuva mulaka churchge adhyatmika naveekanada avashyakatheyithu. | False | After two centuries of religious heresy, the Church needed a spiritual renewal, finding the perfect ally in Francis of Assisi (1182-1226), pious without being troublesomely militant. | After two centuries of religious persecution, during the Franciscan period (1182‑1226) the church needed spiritual renewal by invoking a perfect friend. | 0.7678614 | False | After two centuries of religious decadence, the Church needed spiritual renewal by seeking a perfect companion in the Franciscan of Assisi (1182‑1226). | 0.8421701 | 0.07430871928558347 | False |
| xnli-test-2411 | hypothesis | ಈ ಚರ್ಚ್ ಫ್ರಾನ್ಸಿಸ್ ಆಫ್ ಅಸ್ಸಿಸಿಯೊಂದಿಗೆ ಹತ್ತಿರದಲ್ಲಿತ್ತು. | ee church francis of assisiyondige hattiradallittu. | False | The Church was close with Francis of Assisi. | This church was built for Francis of Assisi. | 0.8165166 | False | This church was close to Francis of Assisi. | 0.9408566 | 0.12433997596588131 | False |
| xnli-test-2867 | premise | ಪರಿಶಿಷ್ಟ A ಯಲ್ಲಿನ A-3ರಲ್ಲಿ ತೋರಿಸಿರುವಂತೆ, ಈ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯು ನಿರ್ಮಾಣ ಪರವಾನಗಿ ಅರ್ಜಿಯ ಪ್ರಕ್ರಿಯೆಯೊಂದಿಗೆ ಏಕಕಾಲದಲ್ಲಿ ಸಂಭವಿಸಬಹುದು. | parisista A yallina A-3rali thorisiruvananthe, ee prakriyeyu nirmana paravanagi arjiyara prakriyeyondige yekakaladalli sambhavisabahudu. | False | As shown in Exhibit A-3 in Appendix A, this process can occur simultaneously with the processing of the construction permit application. | The parasite A is observed in all A‑3rali, and this process can occur at any stage from construction to the final stage of the organ. | 0.26064885 | False | As shown in A‑3 of Appendix A, this process can occur simultaneously with the construction permit application process. | 0.97160673 | 0.710957881414795 | False |
